# Algorithm methodology follow-ups

**Status:** open review and implementation backlog. Accepted methodology is
identified in linked records; runtime-change authorization is not implied. **Audit:** 2026-11-05, source revision
`7e82fd03ad98346727769e88dd5ac5d84b56e9fc`.

This backlog consolidates **603 review items across 33 source scopes with open items**
in the [algorithm catalogue](README.md). Accepted methodology is identified
in the owning records; other pending decisions retain their labels.
Owning records provide formulas, source/test/reference evidence and scope
limits. The [central follow-ups](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#financial-methodology-audit)
retain cross-domain coordination.

This register owns mutable algorithm work. Records and inventories retain
formulas, observed behavior, material limitations and evidence qualifications,
and link here for action details. Each action has one authoritative entry; use
links for work owned by the central register. Record progress and completion
evidence with the owning entry instead of duplicating task lists in records.

The labels distinguish the work required:

- **Observed** (159): a static implementation, contract, dispatch or
  documentation difference. Its financial impact is not necessarily reproduced.
- **Investigate** (49): a source-derived numerical or economic concern
  requiring targeted reproduction, an independent reference or convergence
  study before a defect conclusion.
- **Decision** (236): a financial convention, supported domain, approximation,
  failure policy or result meaning requiring explicit agreement.
- **Reconciliation** (96): implementation and verification work against an
  accepted contract, without reopening that contract for adoption.
- **Evidence** (63): a particular primary-reference, empirical-calibration,
  independent-fixture or numerical-qualification gap.

These categories are not severity rankings. A decision can expose implementation
work, and an observed difference can require numerical investigation. Earlier
agreed directions remain implementation reconciliation requirements; they are
not reopened as optional choices. The [audit boundary](AUDIT_BOUNDARY.md) records
the source and evidence limits; [ADR 0026](governance/0026-methodology-decisions-and-inventory.md)
defines adoption and independent statuses.

Record closure in this register, linking to the agreed scope/rationale and
appropriate implementation or validation evidence in the owning methodology.
Applied documentation corrections are excluded unless an underlying policy or
runtime question remains. Shared-kernel issues appear at their owner; distinct
product consequences remain in their product scope.

## Core conventions

[Scope inventory](core-conventions/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted holiday engine and jurisdiction calendars.** The complete packages in [004](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md#accepted-decision-holiday-engine-and-data-boundaries) and [005](core-conventions/005-jurisdiction-calendars.md#accepted-decision-jurisdiction-calendar-purposes-and-coverage) were accepted on 2026-11-07. Their common engine contract and 26-retained/2-retired purpose matrix, forecast permissions and historical/annual-data boundaries are not reopened. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: `HolidayCalendar` membership and counting are fallible (`Result`) and errors propagate through core adjustment/search, Bus252, cashflow and valuation callers and both hosts; effective ranges gate the nominal event year once with spillovers retained; contributing nominal years are derived from each rule's occurrence window (no fixed year±1 probe, no prior-year rematerialization); transform arithmetic is checked; invalid ordinals, impossible month/days, zero-length spans, multi-occurrence bridge festivals and non-positive Easter years are errors; missing lunar/equinox table years error while irrelevant neighbouring years do not; failed years are never cached; reversed business-day counts error. Remaining engine work is strict JSON/CSV admission and coverage metadata (004 items 8, 10, 11). Implement the selected Frankfurt/Hesse correction, dated NSW Anzac additions, SIFMA full/early-close distinctions, Indian exceptional notices, complete SSE annual notices and epoch-correct Japanese citizens rules. Remove generic cme/cnbe without aliases and automatic SSE estimates/bridge selection; constrain NERC to labels and exchange IDs to their declared purposes. Independently qualify closures and open dates, HK collision/2012 transitions, Japan substitutes/Olympics/equinox status, historical weekend limits, amendments and each advertised source range. Partial India 2024–2026 moving rows and China 2004–2026 Spring/Labour spans are not whole-calendar qualification; Singapore's maintained moving window is 2024–2027 and Matariki ends in 2052. Preserve frozen provenance, forecast status and explicit errors outside admitted domains. Runtime implementation, data refresh and independent qualification remain pending.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted calendar-input admission contract.** Unknown JSON fields and semantic date, nth-weekday, span and effective-bound checks are not uniformly enforced; missing fields can select no observation, false ignore-weekends or zero offset. The date CSV reader can ignore surplus trailing cells. No invalid maintained holiday row was established. Reconcile admission with [004's accepted input contract](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md#accepted-decision-holiday-engine-and-data-boundaries): recursive unknown-field/ID/rule validation, explicit applicable financial controls, complete row shapes and declared table ranges separate from cache bounds. Qualify malformed and boundary inputs independently; a passing renderer check does not validate financial inputs or close independent qualification.

- **Observed — Context-dependent day counts remain exposed by a context-free XIRR API.** The audited XIRR path supplies default day-count context, so Bus252, ACT/365L and ACT/ACT ICMA fail for nonempty intervals needing calendar, frequency or coupon boundaries. [017's accepted complete package](core-conventions/017-irr.md#accepted-decision-irr-and-xirr-timing-eligibility-and-numerical-acceptance) selects fixed ACT/365F and removes generalized clocks; this is no longer an open choice between a supported list and context-bearing XIRR. The existing accepted IRR/XIRR reconciliation entry below owns the runtime removal and qualification work. Records: [002](core-conventions/002-coupon-context-day-counts.md), [017](core-conventions/017-irr.md).

- **Decision — CDS product boundaries and maturity composition.** [008's accepted schedule contract](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md#accepted-decision-schedule-grids-events-and-failure-boundaries) removes the generic CDS mode, its defaults and automatic start substitution; that choice is not reopened. Products/calibration must supply actual selected accrual boundaries, frequency/stub and maturity under their own contractual regime. [009's complete accepted package](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md#accepted-decision-unadjusted-roll-dates-and-product-boundaries) narrows the semiannual helpers to explicit lattices, without adopting a universal trade/tenor-to-maturity composition. Reconcile the unconditional March/September-floor-plus-tenor/June-December-ceiling caller, including the dated 2015-12-22/3M counterexample and official 3M/9M maturity fixtures. Resolve actual tenor/regime eligibility, short-tenor handling, first accrual, protection, settlement and endpoint inclusion at the product owners rather than inferring them from core date helpers. Remove the CDS-option prior-roll `unwrap_or(as_of)` boundary fallback while preserving required errors. Records: [008](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md), [009](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md), [CDS materialization](calibration-framework/07-cds-materialization.md), [CDS-option quadrature](models-and-valuations-credit/15-cds-option-quadrature.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted 30/360 family and interval boundary.** [003's full package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/003-thirty360.md#accepted-decision-synthetic-day-count-conventions): five explicit formulas, distinct high-level ISDA bond-basis selection, fixed US EOM identity without PSA equivalence, explicit optional-presence termination status for 30E/360 ISDA, one public DayCount interval API with raw helpers/duplicate selector made internal, validated equal-date zero and signed reversal retaining the chronological later-endpoint termination fact. Remove default-false and reverse-clearing behavior, expose the bond-basis variant and remove raw exports/host wrappers. Reconcile synthetic zero/nonadditivity, exact integer/finite-error rules and cross-host/wire contracts; independently qualify February/31st/leap/termination, signed/equal, malformed context, synthetic-zero and nonadditivity cases. Accepted choices are not reopened. Product mapping, runtime implementation and independent vendor qualification remain separate; acceptance does not execute those changes.

- **Observed — Day-count evidence and complexity prose need correction.** The stored QuantLib fixture incorrectly calls 2024-02-28 leap-year February month-end, although its numerical expectation is unchanged. Its certified flag is not independently verified provenance; SIA/PSA and vendor aliases conflate distinct 30/360 choices. Bus252 prose also claims universal scanning despite prefix-cache use for built-in calendars; Act360/Act365F Rustdoc reverses ISDA 4.16(e)/(d) citations. Correct source descriptions and external-code mappings against exact definitions/editions, independently regenerate the reference fixture and document the selected AFB variant rather than claiming universal vendor agreement. Records: [001](core-conventions/001-actual-day-counts.md), [003](core-conventions/003-thirty360.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted FX point resolution, authority, state and shocks.** [013's complete package](core-conventions/013-fx-resolution-and-shocks.md#accepted-decision-fx-resolution-state-and-relative-shocks) was accepted on 2026-11-07: explicit point-date policies and optional pivot, removal of PeriodAverage and financial defaults, matrix-over-provider/scoped-before-global/direct-before-inverse authority, literal directed quotes and ordered observations, missing-only routing with preserved causes, actual-result numeric checks and limited provenance, coherent non-reused generations, fallible complete snapshots, atomic no-duplicate writes/fresh restoration, and frozen all-scope pair shocks using an explicit reference query and each orientation's baseline. Reconcile Rust/host/config/schema/parity/state and actual statements/valuations/scenario consumers together; remove aggregate approximations, unordered cache normalization, merge restore and live-sharing shock fallback. Independently qualify averages counterexamples, authority/orientation/cold-warm behavior, tiny/overflowing rates, missing versus outage/invalid failures, provider races and revisionless call limits, unsupported/empty/full snapshots, atomic duplicates/state, directional shocks, dependent crosses, ineffective bumps and scope-faithful replay. Quote-date/type, vendor/publication provenance, owner-specific accounting averages and price-risk qualification remain separate. Record: [013](core-conventions/013-fx-resolution-and-shocks.md).

- **Observed — FX state loading is a non-atomic merge.** `load_from_state` merges global/pinned overrides, ignores replacement provider/config and can leave earlier writes after a later invalid value. Default-empty provider snapshot hooks do not establish full replay of a live/calculated provider. Current relative shocks can share live provider state, normalize an independently supplied reverse quote and change derived crosses. These are source observations, not executed defect reproductions; reconciliation of replacement, complete capture and frozen directional shock semantics is governed by the preceding [accepted 013 package](core-conventions/013-fx-resolution-and-shocks.md#accepted-decision-fx-resolution-state-and-relative-shocks).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted FX quote metadata and value-date boundaries.** [011's complete FX quote/date package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/011-fx-date-and-quote-conventions.md#accepted-decision-explicit-fx-quote-metadata-and-value-dates); its ordered explicit metadata, reciprocal, inference removals and forward-value-date choices are not reopened. Reconcile FX spot/forward/swap/NDF and XCCY calibration callers as a working slice, including actual/owned spot dates, orientation-matched pips and product-payload defaults; do not retain private heuristic copies or add a generic rules engine. Implement explicit-calendar Natural/conditional M/Y business-EOM branches, open-spot/open-result/strictly-later admission and necessary-data/error boundaries. Propagate removed factories/enums/fields/helpers and new canonical inputs across Rust/hosts/state/schema/parity/examples. Independently qualify quote units/reciprocals, closed/collapsed dates, business versus calendar EOM, fully closed preserved months, Natural cross-month rolls, required coverage/date limits and host errors. Actual pair/product/venue conventions and source editions remain separately owned; dated EBS/ANZ references do not certify universal present-day defaults. Runtime implementation and independent product/current-market qualification remain pending. Record: [011](core-conventions/011-fx-date-and-quote-conventions.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted schedule grid, event and failure contract.** [008's complete schedule-generation package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md#accepted-decision-schedule-grids-events-and-failure-boundaries). Its explicit selections/ranges, direction-specific anchored grids and literal stubs, EOM/quarterly-IMM admission, CDS-mode removal, calendar/lag recipes, necessary comparisons, 100,000-final-date ceiling, strict failure and result/state/nominal-reference boundaries are not reopened. Remove obsolete modes/defaults/policies/warnings, repair success and unchecked state paths across Rust/hosts/schema/parity/examples. Reconcile CDS/accrual-adjustment callers with product owners; independently qualify stubs, direction-specific clamping, date/resource limits, calendar failures, lag ordering, collisions, nominal references and restored states. Actual product accrual/observation convention selection remains separately owned. Runtime work and independent qualification remain pending. Record: [008](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted reporting/fiscal periods, statistical factors and LTM completeness.** [014's complete period/LTM package](core-conventions/014-fiscal-periods-ltm.md#accepted-decision-reporting-periods-fiscal-context-and-ltm-windows) was accepted on 2026-11-07: exact Gregorian/ISO and actual-opening fiscal partitions/labels, removal of fiscal defaults/country presets, explicit retained PeriodCalendar context, strict signed grammar/ranges/actuals, checked contextual stepping, structural total ordering, statistical-only annualization_factor and synthetic-prior removal, validated private period/plan state, reporting-slot and calendar-day LTM with weekly excluded, target presence and exact geometric missing counts distinct from data/value eligibility. Reconcile parser/Ord, Gregorian FY rejection, context-free fiscal paths, all state/hosts/schema/parity and actual analytics/statements/reporting consumers together; propagate malformed-window errors rather than masking them as availability. Independently qualify year0/negative/finite-domain boundaries, ISO/fiscal short weeks, opening/month-anchor clamping, source/result shifts, cutoff/prefix/range/resource rules, round-trip context and invalid state, total-order laws, daily/reporting LTM boundaries and incomplete versus malformed history. The current mixed comparator's FY2025W53/2025/2025M12 cycle and Gregorian-builder FY handling are static findings, not executed Rust defect reproductions. [007's accepted tenor/date package](core-conventions/007-tenor-date-arithmetic.md#accepted-decision-tenor-and-date-arithmetic-conventions) is not reopened; its existing pricing/scenario/seasoning caller reconciliation and independent approximation/date qualification remain required. Financial owners retain actual fiscal regimes, explicit observation factors, value/missing/summation/rounding and eligible additive-flow policies; no inferred business closure or 52-week accounting convention is adopted here. Records: [007](core-conventions/007-tenor-date-arithmetic.md), [014](core-conventions/014-fiscal-periods-ltm.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted monetary precision, rounding and currency identity.** [012's complete monetary/currency package](core-conventions/012-money-decimal-precision.md#accepted-decision-monetary-precision-rounding-and-currency-identity) was accepted on 2026-11-07 within [0020's accepted boundaries](conventions/0020-numerical-representation-and-rounding.md): one exact text/integer/Decimal/wire path; shortest-roundtrip-text float admission; exact-or-error sums; once-rounded bounded Decimal transforms with explicit half-quantum targets and overflow/nonzero-underflow errors; correctly rounded float views and direct dimensionless ratios; explicit financial scale/mode and precision-preserving absent ingest overrides; display-only defaults; Decimal-rate FX with exact-unit same-currency admission; alphabetic identity, unique-only numeric lookup and frozen currency provenance. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: one exact text grammar (`parse_decimal`, final value tested after the exponent), exact `DecimalWire` restoration with canonical normalized fixed-point output, exact Python integers and Money zero normalization. **Blocked for owner decision:** item 4's shortest-text float admission rejects nonzero values below `1e-28`, and applied as written it fails pricers that place negligible model values (deep out-of-the-money PVs, Monte Carlo and tranche expected losses, CMS replication, structured-credit flows) into Money; each consumer needs an explicit choice (error, an owner rounding stage, or a later float boundary) before the converter can change. Reconcile the remaining JS number/text paths, arithmetic/rate adapters, configuration, mode rename, display bounds, enum casts, generated data/schema/parity/examples and all affected financial callers together. Independently qualify exact and inexact scientific input, malformed/restored state, precision/range/cancellation/halfway/underflow boundaries, float and ratio budgets, conditional rounding, same-currency nonunit rates and numeric reuse (ANG/XCG532). Audit known XCG/ZWG omissions and supported historical metadata against pinned SIX sources; no automatic redenomination/status engine. Required numerical guarantees remain targets until implementation and independent qualification, not established dependency behavior. Contract-specific rounding stages/accuracy remain with their existing owners. Record: [012](core-conventions/012-money-decimal-precision.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted cashflow representation/admission and NPV boundaries.** [015's complete cash/balance/accrual package](core-conventions/015-cashflow-semantics.md#accepted-decision-cash-settlement-principal-effects-and-accrual-metadata) was accepted on 2026-11-07: amount always signed settlement cash, zero-cash PIK/write-downs, explicit paired principal delta/date for every principal-like row including deliberate zero, literal/exhaustive kind meanings with Floating/ProtectionPayment and coupon/repayment-like classifications, distinct clocks, complete single-slice DayCountContextState, literal zero/signed scalar metadata, complete immutable validated construction/restoration and faithful cash-versus-event views. Reconcile principal replay/opening-balance heuristics, par-versus-cash repayment consumers, PIK interest readers, protection payouts, collateral assets/call dates, accrual boundary/factor fallbacks, long-stub/composite metadata and principal-date exports across Rust/hosts/state/schema/parity and all actual products together. Independently qualify cash/balance roll-forwards, explicit zero/opening funding, premiums/corrections/date lags, every kind, required context/long stubs, nonfinite/zero rates/factors, atomic updates and exact event round trips. Schedule calculation order, financial formula/PIK eligibility/recovery allocation and full product qualification remain separately owned. **016's complete package was also accepted on 2026-11-07:** [the discounting/NPV contract](core-conventions/016-discounting.md#accepted-decision-dated-npv-selection-arithmetic-and-continuous-discounting) requires explicit include_today/as_of, full currency/finiteness validation before selection, Money-empty error versus scalar-empty zero, no unused curve queries, source-owned dated relative factors, strict positive-factor/numeric failures, literal 012 Money conversion/products/exact sums and ordered checked scalar Neumaier arithmetic. It retains continuous flat discounting with positive finite output, permits negative rates/subnormal factors and rejects zero underflow; no universal scalar/exp accuracy guarantee or implicit curve currency is adopted. Reconcile real product same-day/empty/cash-filter choices, source-date use, renamed scalar helper, shared numeric checks and Monte Carlo/host product behavior together without new low-level host APIs. Independently qualify same-day/zero/invalid excluded inputs, curve normalization/calendar/coverage overrides, Money factor and precision domains, scalar cancellation/subnormal/overflow and flat exponential extremes against independent references and owner accuracy budgets. The accepted NPV choices are not reopened; complete numerical/product qualification remains separate from 015's row/filter contract. Records: [015](core-conventions/015-cashflow-semantics.md), [016](core-conventions/016-discounting.md). Endpoint wording was clarified on2026-11-08 with [CASHFLOWS-001](cashflows/01-schedule-periods.md#accepted-decision-contractual-periods-calendars-and-day-count-context): actual calculation dates include explicitly selected accrual adjustment; nominal references remain separate and day-count/single-context rules unchanged.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted IRR/XIRR stream, clock, solver and numerical acceptance.** [017's complete IRR/XIRR package](core-conventions/017-irr.md#accepted-decision-irr-and-xirr-timing-eligibility-and-numerical-acceptance) was accepted on 2026-11-07: per-period original spacing, fixed ACT/365F dated effective annual returns, one canonical checked rounded net/date used consistently, one-switch eligibility in either direction, underdetermination/rate-domain errors, no public guess/generalized-clock inputs, full finite log bracket with direct rate endpoints, shared fallible Brent at100 iterations/1e-12 operational tolerance, and one finite returned-rate/residual<=1e-8 gate on every success path. Reconcile signatures, preprocessing, error/objective paths and portfolio/PE/loan/bond-return-floor/host consumers together without silently changing product-specific yields or copying retired generic behavior. Remove automatic periodic IRR on flattened captured MC components; component order does not establish a complete regular timed investment path. Independently qualify model netting/eligibility, cancellation/origin/zero spacing, leap timing, extreme rate endpoints, every exit path, finite residual and poor conditioning against exact/high-precision references. Operational residual/log tolerances do not certify uniform true rate error, ledger-exact netting, Excel root compatibility or scale/sign-flip success invariance. Preserve currency/sign/initial-terminal preparation, owner-specific reporting/candidate/yield policies and separate runtime/product qualification. Record: [017](core-conventions/017-irr.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted ratings and scorecards.** [019's complete package](core-conventions/019-credit-ratings.md#accepted-decision-generic-ratings-and-explicit-scorecard-scales) was accepted on 2026-11-07: consecutive rated AAA..D quality with NR outside comparisons/notch arithmetic; literal grade/default-marker meanings; finite strict alias tables, canonical wire labels and fallible Moody-style rendering; removal of unsupported embedded numeric scales/default score 50/implicit sp and every unknown-scale fallback; explicit immutable custom registry/selected snapshot with nonblank source, normalized strict IDs/labels/references, complete [0,100] score bands and representative self-membership; privately validated atomic construction/restoration and faithful policy snapshots. Reconcile Rust comparisons and ordered-key containers, registry/config/schema/data removal, actual statements-analytics scorecard selection/classification/minimum labels, hosts/parity/examples and model/margin/coverage boundaries together without compatibility aliases or fallback copies. Independently qualify NR/incomparable/rank/alias/wire/style cases, normalized collisions, full-domain boundary classification, representative consistency, missing/malformed registry errors, invalid-state atomicity, snapshot replay and host errors. The existing embedded representatives classify into adjacent better bands, and Moody's bottom minimum leaves a domain gap; this does not establish a replacement agency calibration. Metric formulas/weights/threshold gaps and ties/missing-factor rules, PD/transition/recovery/WARF data, NR collateral policies and regulatory/product eligibility remain with their owners. Runtime implementation and independent quantitative/production qualification are pending. Record: [019](core-conventions/019-credit-ratings.md).

- **Evidence — Barrier observation and settlement qualification at each consumer.** [020's package](core-conventions/020-barrier-classification.md#accepted-decision-barrier-classification-touch-and-conditional-payment-timing) is reconciled in runtime: no enum defaults, rebate timing required exactly with a rebate, knock-in `AtHit` rejected, and enum wire/predicate tests complete. Independently qualify, at each actual consumer (analytic, Monte Carlo and PDE equity/FX barriers and FX touch), equality, adjacent-value and already-beyond observations; window, history and missing-data behavior; and conditional hit/expiry cash with its settlement and discount boundaries. Analytic/Monte Carlo/PDE monitoring, bridge/continuity corrections, rebate economics, model selection, Greeks and historical-settlement policy remain separately owned. Record: [020](core-conventions/020-barrier-classification.md).

- **Evidence — Astronomical calendar generation and source provenance.** The manual lunar generator uses unpinned lunar_python for seven CSVs, with Qing Ming UTC+8 and Japanese equinox UTC+9 adjustments. The active renderer only validates/emits maintained inputs; it performs no astronomical or official-data refresh. Establish pinned generator/environment/input/output revisions, the library civil-time contract, independent dated vectors including local-midnight boundaries, and CNY CSV provenance/reproducible maintenance. Distinguish published and projected ranges using actual publication editions, including Japanese Gazette evidence, rather than table presence or current wall-clock date. Remove unsupported cross-library/official-calibration claims; the existing digest and renderer check are not independent astronomical or holiday validation. Records: [004](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md), [005](core-conventions/005-jurisdiction-calendars.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted actual-day and coupon-context contracts.** [001's actual-day package](core-conventions/001-actual-day-counts.md#accepted-decision-actual-day-conventions) and [002's coupon-context package](core-conventions/002-coupon-context-day-counts.md#accepted-decision-coupon-context-day-counts) were accepted in full on 2026-11-07. Reconcile 001's bypassable runtime/state context validation, zero-shortcut order, Bus252 counting controls, ISDA arithmetic and cross-host contracts. For 002, remove inferred grids/months/frequency-only shortcuts and 512-period restrictions; implement contained ICMA reference/frequency validation and schedule-owned composition, end-year ACT/365L without the annual exception/frequency dependency, and explicit context through the canonical tenor bridge. Reconcile source/wire/host documentation and incorrect A008 attribution. Independently qualify leap/century/date-limit, zero/reversed, malformed/restored context, cached/custom-calendar counts, complete/short/long coupons, frequency mismatch, roll ambiguity, ACT/365L discriminators, partial-period additivity and endpoint-adjustment failures. These accepted choices are not reopened. Runtime work and independent vendor qualification remain pending; instrument-specific selection retains its own owner.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted rolling/joint-calendar and roll-date contracts.** [006's rolling/joint-calendar package](core-conventions/006-business-day-adjustments.md#accepted-decision-rolling-and-joint-calendar-conventions) and [009's complete roll-date package](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md#accepted-decision-unadjusted-roll-dates-and-product-boundaries) were accepted on 2026-11-07; their choices are not reopened. Reconcile 006's obsolete guards/fallbacks/defaults, year/month comparisons and required error/host/wire boundaries; independently qualify ties/coverage, long closures, fully closed months, date extremes, offsets/counts, composite parsing and frozen restoration. For 009, propagate exact unadjusted lattice/search meaning and host input/error boundaries; remove the obsolete private inclusive-IMM helpers with 008's schedule caller rewrite (the generic IMM option-expiry helpers were removed from core/hosts/parity/examples on 2026-11-08). Independently qualify equality, year transitions, membership, finite exhaustion, Good-Friday raw dates and host conversions. Actual product expiry/cutoff/session rules remain separately owned; CDS product decisions and affected composition remain in the existing CDS product-boundary entry. Runtime changes and independent qualification remain pending.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted SIFMA published-date, class and coverage contract.** [010's complete published-settlement package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/010-sifma-settlement.md#accepted-decision-published-settlement-dates-classes-and-coverage); its choices are not reopened. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: explicit class everywhere (no Default, no Python/WASM default-class paths), checked `Result` exact lookup with not-found versus validation errors, class-specific strict-next search over at most the current and following months, removal of the business-day-index estimator, and fixed-date coverage/gap/equality tests replacing the rolling twelve-month test. Agency/term classification was also removed: `SifmaSettlementClass::from_agency_term` and the TBA/dollar-roll inference are gone and both products require `settlement_class` (owner resolution O2). Remaining: implement canonical Date-domain data admission in the generator; freeze source identity, edition, retrieval date, hash and per-class coverage beside `sifma_settlements.csv`; and independently qualify source-row extraction, gaps, month-end bypass, date extremes and frozen-edition replay. Actual product classification/settlement and separately selected projection models remain with their financial owners. Record: [010](core-conventions/010-sifma-settlement.md).

## Core numerics

[Scope inventory](core-numerics/README.md).

MVA already validates its spread grid; do not claim its existing empty-data fallback is reachable or impose two points/DF positivity on singleton EAD profiles. Independently qualify exact knot/endpoint preservation, left/inward derivatives, flat/extended/error boundaries, nonfinite queries, signed/log domains, close/translated grids, overflow/subnormal/near-equal-log/PCHIP harmonic cases, shape/C1 claims, malformed restore and host errors. Runtime implementation and independent qualification remain pending. Records: [02](core-numerics/value-interpolation.md), [03](core-numerics/forward-interpolation.md).

Reconcile shared actual parameter consumers and preserve each algorithm's admission and record 09's integration and price authority. Runtime and independent complete-path qualification remains open.

## Trees and finite differences

[Scope inventory](models-numerical/README.md).

- **Observed — Floored rates-credit calibration can accept failed solves.** Brent failure becomes bracket midpoint without a final repricing gate; invalid intermediate sums can produce a zero shift. Specify failure/residual policy and independent fit evidence before adopting these fallbacks. Records: [008](models-numerical/rates-credit-marginal-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Rates-credit floor-mass and variance limits.** target>=total_mass rejects even attainable equality; KAPPA_MAX=0.15 does not constrain kappa*T, and clipped transitions remove conditional variance. Define floor feasibility, clipping and reported variance-loss acceptance thresholds. Records: [008](models-numerical/rates-credit-marginal-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Theta stability guard omits advection and reaction.** For theta<0.5, the CFL check uses diffusion only; pure advection gets an infinite bound and reaction stiffness is ignored. Narrow stability claims and qualify combined-operator, domain and finiteness checks. Define finite-result and residual-check requirements as well as coefficient domains; reconcile the broad unconditional-stability source comments for unrestricted callbacks. Records: [014](models-numerical/theta-time-march-and-thomas.md).

- **Investigate — Mixed derivatives on uneven meshes.** Four-corner cross derivatives use averaged spacing. Bilinear exactness does not establish second-order convergence on arbitrary nonuniform grids; obtain nonlinear manufactured-solution refinement evidence or constrain the supported claim. Agree admissible grids and coefficient domains, including negative/nonfinite diffusion and ellipticity. Records: [012](models-numerical/pde-spatial-stencils.md).

- **Evidence — American post-step penalty accuracy.** Early exercise is a finite convex pull after stepping, not a coupled complementarity solve, and leaves intrinsic shortfall. Adopt price/free-boundary tolerances and compare against independent American benchmarks. Specify obstacle shortfall tolerance, free-boundary reporting meaning and separate American/Bermudan error budgets. Records: [015](models-numerical/pde-exercise-projection.md).

- **Decision — Startup damping and ADI stability.** One-dimensional Rannacher uses full implicit intervals; two-dimensional startup uses factorized halfsteps with an explicit mixed term. Adopt these distinct methods, reconcile stability assertions after upwinding and establish mixed-term/ellipticity guards. Records: [014](models-numerical/theta-time-march-and-thomas.md), [016](models-numerical/mcs-adi-time-march.md).

- **Investigate — BDT/BK fit diagnostics and clamped tails.** Per-row aggregation only updates when error>max, so NaN errors could be skipped. Reproduce malformed/nonfinite regimes and assess BDT tail-clamp option error even when ZCB fit is good. Define the BDT volatility/horizon support envelope, BK convergence/error budgets and its repeated terminal-rate convention; obtain independent option-price benchmarks for both. Records: [005](models-numerical/bdt-lognormal-curve-fit.md), [006](models-numerical/black-karasinski-trinomial.md).

- **Observed — Tree input contracts disagree.** Common OptionMarketParams rejects zero vol and permits zero expiry; tree pricing accepts zero vol and requires positive expiry. Decide intended shared versus engine-specific input domains. Set CRR/LR accuracy budgets by moneyness, volatility and early-exercise regime. Records: [001](models-numerical/equity-binomial-construction.md).

- **Decision — Tree extrapolation, floors and raw callbacks.** Terminal rows extrapolate and extreme compounding bases floor. Rates-credit callback state rates floor while discounting, projection and sampled rates remain raw. Adopt these distinctions and their stress-domain limits. Resolve Ho–Lee diagnostic acceptance semantics: the separate hardcoded <1 bp helper differs from the configured 0.1 relative-DF bp default. Define domain error budgets and terminal-rate use. Records: [003](models-numerical/tree-discounting-and-induction.md), [007](models-numerical/hull-white-event-grid-and-affine-bonds.md), [010](models-numerical/rates-credit-coupon-folding.md), [004](models-numerical/ho-lee-curve-fit.md).

- **Evidence — Hull–White lattice and affine-bond consistency.** The tree uses Euler moments and interval curve-fit alpha; conditional bonds use continuous affine moments and instantaneous shift. Quantify finite-grid and noncontinuous-compounding differences for products using both. Records: [007](models-numerical/hull-white-event-grid-and-affine-bonds.md).

- **Decision — Rates-credit coupon-fold scope.** The unit claim omits exercise/recovery within reset-to-payment folding. Specify supported reset/payment/termination combinations and caller-owned survival/recovery economics; model metadata alone does not choose the convention. Define rate-dependent payoff semantics for floored callback rates and obtain independent coupon/exercise timing benchmarks. Records: [010](models-numerical/rates-credit-coupon-folding.md).

- **Evidence — PDE domains, boundaries and coordinate Greeks.** Greeks snap to nearest nodes, boundaries clamp/extrapolate, Heston variance boundary is linear rather than limiting-PDE closure, and public bridge coefficients lack validation. Set domain/truncation/Greek error budgets and independent boundary cases. Agree direct-bridge validation responsibilities, variance-domain truncation, degenerate lower-edge treatment, corner compatibility and homogeneous-cache promises. Set query-Greek and domain-truncation acceptance criteria. Records: [011](models-numerical/pde-grids-interpolation-and-greeks.md), [013](models-numerical/pde-boundary-elimination.md), [017](models-numerical/black-scholes-and-heston-pde-bridges.md).

- **Evidence — Tree and damping primary references.** Add dedicated CRR, Leisen–Reimer, Ho–Lee, BDT, Black–Karasinski, startup-damping and floored rates-credit references; distinguish ADI papers currently mapped to one catalogue anchor. Test pins, curve round trips and in-repository Fourier comparisons are not vendor qualification. Record a startup-damping accuracy target alongside its primary citation. Records: [001](models-numerical/equity-binomial-construction.md), [004](models-numerical/ho-lee-curve-fit.md), [005](models-numerical/bdt-lognormal-curve-fit.md), [006](models-numerical/black-karasinski-trinomial.md), [008](models-numerical/rates-credit-marginal-calibration.md), [014](models-numerical/theta-time-march-and-thomas.md), [016](models-numerical/mcs-adi-time-march.md).

- **Decision — Escrowed-dividend and mapped-exercise convention.** Agree the error envelope for using original dividend times in reserves, rounded event nodes for exercise and volatility on the escrow component. Obtain a primary reference and independent numerical validation for the specific escrow/exercise rule, including American/Bermudan timing. Records: [002](models-numerical/dividends-and-lattice-exercise.md).

- **Decision — Rates-credit innovation correlation and degenerate tails.** Adopt the product interpretation of Bernoulli innovation correlation separately from terminal-level/default correlation, including the inability of degenerate marginals to express arbitrary rho. Establish the required external market-calibration evidence. Records: [009](models-numerical/rates-credit-coupling-and-sampling.md).

## Monte Carlo models

[Scope inventory](models-monte-carlo/README.md).

- **Observed — QE-Heston correction fallback changes the step.** If the conditional MGF does not exist, the martingale correction silently becomes an uncorrected step. exact_variance selects a mean-reversion-adjusted approximation to integrated variance. Agree failure/refinement, naming and boundary stress accuracy. Qualify prices and forward bias across grids around the MGF boundary; determine whether fallback should error or require step refinement. Records: [09](models-monte-carlo/09-heston-qe.md).

- **Observed — Credit-line process can select an economically different generic scheme.** No dedicated required scheme is declared, so generic Euler can omit spread-linked utilization targets, clamping and deterministic-rate endpoint updates implemented by CreditLineDiscretization. Resolve scheme compatibility and obtain empirical utilization/spread calibration evidence. Specify the bounded-diffusion interpretation and admissible spread-target sensitivity; distinguish clamped OU endpoint mass and instantaneous driver correlation from exact finite-step transition correlation. Records: [11](models-monte-carlo/11-credit-line.md).

- **Observed — Captured-path IRR loses cashflow dates.** Captured amounts feed periodic IRR after discarding times, and failures are omitted. Define irregular/simultaneous-flow meaning or require dated-return treatment in diagnostics. Decide whether any IRR is meaningful for arbitrary captured diagnostics, and identify eligible diagnostic cashflows. Records: [28](models-monte-carlo/28-capture-and-simulation-contracts.md).

- **Observed — Range-accrual missing state changes the denominator.** Missing SPOT is deferred, nonfinite spots count, and only consumed observations enter the denominator, unlike fail-fast payoff families. Decide explicit missing-fixing economics versus validation failure. Decide whether every expected observation must be consumed and whether missing/nonfinite state must fail immediately. Records: [22](models-monte-carlo/22-range-accrual.md).

- **Investigate — Fully seasoned cliquet can omit accrued value.** With no reset_spots, compute_return returns zero before applying locked sum/growth. Reproduce no-future-reset seasoned contracts and specify required locked-return ownership. Choose support versus explicit rejection for no-future-reset seasoned contracts; adopt the final nonnegative payoff clamp and obtain independent seasoned contract validation. Records: [21](models-monte-carlo/21-cliquet.md).

- **Decision — Skipped observation dates reuse one endpoint.** Autocall, cliquet and range accrual reuse current state for every skipped scheduled date. Require aligned observation grids or qualify bias. Autocall minima are discrete and final unpaid memory is not automatically redeemed; adopt term-sheet treatment. For autocalls, adopt each redemption formula, coupon-before-autocall ordering, zero/nonempty schedule domains, seasoning and payment-date mapping against the owning term sheet. Records: [20](models-monte-carlo/20-autocallable.md), [21](models-monte-carlo/21-cliquet.md), [22](models-monte-carlo/22-range-accrual.md).

- **Decision — Rough-driver identity and leverage.** Riemann–Liouville Volterra and fractional Brownian motion have different covariance laws; finite-window fBM can violate t^(2H) compensation and tests exempt that regime. Adopt generator/leverage interpretation and obtain price validation. Set finite-memory window sizes, generator/model pairings and covariance/price error budgets; qualify rough-Bergomi step/grid convergence and tail sampling separately from sampling uncertainty. Records: [13](models-monte-carlo/13-fractional-noise.md), [14](models-monte-carlo/14-rough-bergomi.md), [15](models-monte-carlo/15-rough-heston.md), [16](models-monte-carlo/16-rough-cheyette.md).

- **Observed — Rough-Heston kernel-grid identity is not checked.** Generic validation checks step count, not the actual precomputed time grid or Hurst consistency. Same-size incompatible grids can use wrong kernels. Define semantic process/grid binding validation. Bind Hurst as well as exact grid coordinates if required by the chosen contract, and define strong/weak convergence and variance-truncation boundary-bias tolerances. Records: [15](models-monte-carlo/15-rough-heston.md).

- **Evidence — Rough Cheyette parameter units and coarse-grid state.** sigma_base is carried by ForwardVarianceCurve but consumed as volatility amplitude; Euler y can become negative on coarse grids. Resolve units, positivity/domain treatment and a primary formulation/calibration benchmark. Resolve sigma-base container naming, phi calibration and admissible fractional generators alongside coarse-grid stability. Records: [16](models-monte-carlo/16-rough-cheyette.md).

- **Decision — LSMC policy value, uncertainty and compatibility.** Ordinary CIs omit regression-selection/training error; price_unbiased values a fixed learned policy, not the optimal American claim. Sparse ITM dates are skipped, maturity is always payable, and policy compatibility checks dimensions rather than semantics. Adopt scope/error targets and add Longstaff–Schwartz provenance. Adopt the minimum-ITM rule, policy identity checks, two-pass default, product-specific basis family/degree, SVD tolerance and rank/conditioning diagnostics. Qualify fallback model selection and exercise accuracy independently of polynomial recovery tests. Records: [23](models-monte-carlo/23-lsmc-exercise.md), [24](models-monte-carlo/24-regression-policies.md).

- **Investigate — Randomized QMC with unequal replicate sizes.** The point estimate weights paths but stderr weights replicate means equally. Qualify nondivisible/small budgets or require equal replicate sizes; there is no power-of-two constraint. Test estimator and interval coverage independently. Adopt replicate count and target error/coverage criteria in addition to the path allocation policy. Records: [03](models-monte-carlo/03-randomized-qmc.md).

- **Observed — Control variate can conceal invalid uncertainty inputs.** All negative adjusted variances floor to zero and beta is used only above variance 1e-10; impossible covariance inputs may report zero uncertainty. Adopt admissible moments, threshold and estimated-beta uncertainty treatment. Resolve rejection versus flooring, the absolute 1e-10 variance cutoff, analytic control expectation provenance and product-specific observed variance reduction. Records: [27](models-monte-carlo/27-control-variate.md).

- **Observed — Drift schedules and coefficient knots depend on stepper.** Generic GBM/Heston Euler ignores cumulative drift schedules used by exact/QE paths. Piecewise GBM does not split at coefficient knots, skips nonincreasing bootstrap checkpoints and does not check callback-vol finiteness. Define scheme equivalence and input guards, including whether out-of-order bootstrap checkpoints may be skipped and the required coefficient-knot alignment contract. Records: [04](models-monte-carlo/04-lognormal-diffusions.md), [09](models-monte-carlo/09-heston-qe.md).

- **Observed — Greek estimator input and interval contracts differ.** Low-level LRM delta indexes shocks and vega zip-truncates mismatched arrays; high-level callers alone ensure shape. FD omits expected-step-count validation and uses normal 1.96 intervals versus generic Student-t estimation. Agree standalone validation and CI conventions. Adopt finite-difference bump convergence/error budgets and simulated-path count semantics; define density-score applicability and convergence with independent Greeks reference evidence. Records: [25](models-monte-carlo/25-finite-difference-greeks.md), [26](models-monte-carlo/26-likelihood-ratio-greeks.md).

- **Decision — Capture limits and subset risk statistics.** Hash capture is expected-count sampling and can exceed nominal/requested count; pricing has no points-times-dimension cap. Subset quantiles are not full-population risk measures. Adopt resource limits and result labels. Records: [28](models-monte-carlo/28-capture-and-simulation-contracts.md).

- **Investigate — Hull–White nonfinite variance and empty theta fit.** The variance helper can return NaN subsequently suppressed by max(0) in stepping; empty theta knots with constant sigma fit a flat initial forward. Reproduce failure domains and adopt error propagation/fitting restrictions. Adopt theta-grid conventions and curve repricing tolerances, including the extrapolated final theta interval. Records: [07](models-monte-carlo/07-hull-white.md).

- **Evidence — Simulation acceptance budgets.** Adopt paths, seeds, time grids, clipping/repair and optional-stopping CI coverage by model. Establish independent price and convergence targets instead of relying only on same-kernel comparisons, round trips or seeded reproducibility. Specify allowed process/payoff pairings and low-level parameter validation responsibilities; qualify CIR Feller-failing and zero-mass regimes and their parameter-wide weak error, local-volatility surface provenance/step convergence, and simulation defaults separately from implementation presence. Records: [01](models-monte-carlo/01-engine-and-estimation.md), [02](models-monte-carlo/02-random-streams.md), [03](models-monte-carlo/03-randomized-qmc.md), [05](models-monte-carlo/05-generic-steppers.md), [07](models-monte-carlo/07-hull-white.md), [08](models-monte-carlo/08-cir-qe.md), [09](models-monte-carlo/09-heston-qe.md), [10](models-monte-carlo/10-lmm.md), [12](models-monte-carlo/12-schwartz-smith.md), [13](models-monte-carlo/13-fractional-noise.md), [23](models-monte-carlo/23-lsmc-exercise.md), [25](models-monte-carlo/25-finite-difference-greeks.md), [27](models-monte-carlo/27-control-variate.md).

- **Decision — Seed and generic-stepper domains.** Adopt the reproducibility scope, seed namespace and FNV collision policy. For Gaussian/generic steppers, decide negative mean-reversion admissibility, the small-state Milstein cutoff and convergence budgets by use case. Stream statistical tests do not establish universal independence, and discrete OU moment checks do not establish continuous-time accuracy. Records: [02](models-monte-carlo/02-random-streams.md), [05](models-monte-carlo/05-generic-steppers.md).

- **Decision — Dividend-jump conventions.** Adopt depleted-stock cash flooring, coexistence with continuous yield and prior application of time-zero events in low-level use. Independently qualify floor-sensitive scenarios; ordinary deterministic forward checks do not establish those cases. Records: [06](models-monte-carlo/06-cash-dividend-jumps.md).

- **Decision — LMM repairs and numeraire scope.** Adopt skipped near-zero accrual denominators, unchanged nonpositive displaced states, zero corrected drift for invalid predictions, displacement bounds and terminal-numeraire conventions. Qualify nonterminal options across supported grids beyond current caplet/terminal-forward checks. Records: [10](models-monte-carlo/10-lmm.md).

- **Evidence — Commodity calibration and risk-premium provenance.** Establish curve calibration and risk-premium provenance before treating the Schwartz–Smith expectation as a market futures curve, and obtain independent calibrated commodity benchmarks. Records: [12](models-monte-carlo/12-schwartz-smith.md).

- **Decision — Terminal and Asian payoff domains.** Adopt strict digital equality/payout conventions, historical aggregate domains, count-normalized scaled observations, partial-average snapshots, cached-weight mutation responsibilities and zero/nonpositive geometric treatment. Independently accept contract schedules and fixing multipliers. Records: [17](models-monte-carlo/17-vanilla-and-asian.md).

- **Decision — Barrier and lookback monitoring contracts.** Adopt endpoint hit-time rebate settlement, rebate rate/discount consistency and monitoring-start conventions. Specify node versus bridge sampling for lookbacks: the default continuous label alone does not enable the bridge. Qualify constant-volatility bridge scope, no-fired-observation handling and any local/stochastic-volatility use with independent price evidence. Records: [18](models-monte-carlo/18-barrier-monitoring.md), [19](models-monte-carlo/19-lookback-monitoring.md).

## Credit models and valuations

[Scope inventory](models-and-valuations-credit/README.md).

- **Observed — Intensity specification does not determine the advertised process.** IntensityProcess discards mean reversion, uses a stateless lognormal factor, ignores seasoning and computes expected MDR from expected intensity rather than averaging conditional MDR. Resolve model identity, active parameters and expected-rate definition. Correct or explicitly rename the mean-intensity MDR approximation; agree endpoint clipping and failure-to-zero handling and normalize correlation accessor semantics. Records: [07](models-and-valuations-credit/07-pool-defaults.md).

- **Observed — CDS-index reporting cashflows differ from priced cashflows.** The public schedule uses midpoint protection, half-period accrual-on-default and payment-date survival, while price delegates the single-name CDS kernel. Adopt an explicitly informational approximation or reconcile reporting PV. Define the consuming API contract for the informational schedule explicitly. Records: [12](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md), [14](models-and-valuations-credit/14-index-valuation.md).

- **Observed — CDS-index maturity cutoff drops adjusted unpaid coupons.** Index pricing exits at premium maturity; the single-name engine can retain unpaid adjusted final coupons after accrual end. Reconcile lifecycle ownership across routes. Records: [12](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md), [14](models-and-valuations-credit/14-index-valuation.md).

- **Observed — Tranche public projection loses timing and maturity state.** Public conversion replaces within-period protection discount timing with coupon payment date. Public project_cashflows can be empty at maturity while pricing retains rolled unpaid rows or upfront. Define reporting/lifecycle equivalence. Validate index-hazard protection timing for heterogeneous and high-hazard portfolios. Records: [18](models-and-valuations-credit/18-tranche-projection.md).

- **Observed — Tranche specialized risks revert to Gaussian pricing.** Spread DV01, par spread, expected loss, correlation and JTD instantiate the default Gaussian pricer despite a selected Student-t, RFL or multi-factor base. Recovery01 uses instrument-native value. Resolve selected-model propagation and label risk definitions. Establish an explicit JTD sign and mark-to-market convention. Records: [19](models-and-valuations-credit/19-tranche-risk.md).

- **Observed — Tranche recovery risk can omit active issuer exposures.** Recovery01 bumps index recovery and optionally index hazard, omitting issuer recovery overrides/hazards that quote CS01/DV01 use. Adopt aggregate/per-name and frozen/quote-preserving recovery experiments. Establish boundary-aware scaling for clipped correlation bumps alongside the recovery-risk contract. Records: [19](models-and-valuations-credit/19-tranche-risk.md).

- **Decision — Tranche domain, discretization and repair.** Detachment<=1% is rejected as a unit guard, excluding valid thin tranches. Large heterogeneous pools use a 0.001 fraction loss grid with linear splitting despite exact-language diagnostics; negative tranchelets/nonmonotone paths repair before stricter maturity checks. Correlation clipping retains the central denominator. Adopt domains, repair limits and grid accuracy. Agree Gaussian/base-correlation default and alternative intended uses, calibration requirements, settlement overrides and index-name rules. Distinguish interim/maturity loss floors, recovery-side floors and outstanding-notional repairs; define standalone-versus-registry provenance and host exposure. Records: [0031](models-and-valuations-credit/0031-tranche-loss-model-selection.md), [17](models-and-valuations-credit/17-tranche-loss-distribution.md), [18](models-and-valuations-credit/18-tranche-projection.md), [19](models-and-valuations-credit/19-tranche-risk.md).

- **Decision — Default exposure and margin lifecycle.** CDS expected loss can include pre-protection default time for forward starts; index JTD lacks an as-of maturity gate. Failed repriced CS01 can make SIMM use a standard maturity after residual time is nonpositive. Define exposure and fallback semantics. Verify quote-space units and dependency replay consistently across parallel and bucketed risk. Records: [13](models-and-valuations-credit/13-cds-and-index-risk.md).

- **Decision — CDS option screen Greeks are different experiments.** Spread Delta is a Black ratio while PV is annuity-aware quadrature; Gamma is an unnormalized +/-5bp Delta difference, Vega is +1-vol-point P&L, and Theta freezes calibrated mean/curves/dates/DF while reducing diffusion time by 1/365.25. Clean-price Delta is symmetric rebootstrap; underlying-spread DV01 is one-sided. Adopt names, units and independent derivatives. Adopt one-sided versus symmetric underlying spread-risk interpretation and add independent strike/factor cases and bump-size convergence evidence. Records: [15](models-and-valuations-credit/15-cds-option-quadrature.md), [16](models-and-valuations-credit/16-cds-option-risk-and-inversion.md).

- **Observed — CDS option lifecycle and replay differ by API.** PV rejects post-expiry and physical-at-expiry cases, IV returns zero at/before as-of, and several risks return zero at expiry. Price-strike Greeks require a provider and replay without some doc-clause/convention overrides used by generic CS01. Reconcile operation domains and convention propagation. Records: [15](models-and-valuations-credit/15-cds-option-quadrature.md), [16](models-and-valuations-credit/16-cds-option-risk-and-inversion.md).

- **Decision — Structural credit failures and model interpretation.** First-passage NaN becomes zero PD; jump truncation reports no tail-mass bound; fixed volatility scanning cannot establish between-node uniqueness. Approximate par spread is not contractual CDS calibration, and CreditGrades asset simulation omits the uncertain barrier. Adopt failure/domain claims and independent numerical benchmarks. Establish model-specific domains, NaN policy, Poisson accuracy limits, CDS approximation error and calibration uniqueness criteria; validate CreditGrades barrier simulation separately before interpreting asset paths as its default process. Records: [04](models-and-valuations-credit/04-structural-default.md).

- **Decision — Empirical credit assumptions and synthetic policies.** Agency-style PD scales contain library assumptions and recovery tables approximate historical moments. Adopt RFL/correlation fallbacks, fixed 60/40 factor decomposition, zero-loading recovery dependence and logistic-normal center-versus-mean interpretation. The liquidity model fixes 20bp/square-root impact and tender model a 2% hurdle without independent rationale. Approve table provenance/vintages and scenario interpretation; decide central-PD bin constraints and final-bound coverage, with a separate current jurisdiction-specific assessment for regulatory claims. Adopt recovery dilution exponents, costs/discount timing, regulatory preset labels and estate/collateral double-count prevention at integration boundaries. Resolve checked/unchecked copula endpoint/shape and comonotonic-tail reporting, factor representation consistency and recovery-center/volatility meaning. Establish liquidity volume/risk-aversion units and tier cutoffs, distinguishing proxy costs from execution predictions. For liability management, adopt breakeven-recovery naming, nonfinite-EBITDA handling and the intentionally excluded legal/economic effects. Records: [02](models-and-valuations-credit/02-pd-scales-and-rating-data.md), [06](models-and-valuations-credit/06-recovery-and-lgd.md), [09](models-and-valuations-credit/09-copula-kernels.md), [10](models-and-valuations-credit/10-portfolio-loss-and-correlated-recovery.md), [11](models-and-valuations-credit/11-liquidity.md), [20](models-and-valuations-credit/20-liability-management.md).

- **Evidence — Credit calibration, repair and settlement benchmarks.** Establish copula/tranche-grid tolerances, migration repair budgets, recovery/calibration provenance, CDS settlement-calendar cases and CDS-option strike/factor/front-end-protection examples. Formula, invariant and the independent single-name AoD regression evidence do not qualify every model or empirical assumption. Agree migration reconstruction/repair-size budgets, homogeneous-time assumptions and unrated-state treatment, and independently validate difficult spectra. Establish sampling-error and tail-convergence evidence for portfolio loss and independent generic copula quadrature convergence. Qualify factor quadrature separately from heterogeneous convolution, including all copulas, grid boundaries and resource limits. Establish CDS tolerances across interpolation, settlement lags, curves, calendars and mixed day counts, independent fixture provenance and host parity. For CDS options, adopt forward-annuity/stub conventions, distressed domains and quadrature tolerances; independently validate non-knockout front-end loss, factor/strike decomposition and settlement. Records: [03](models-and-valuations-credit/03-rating-migration.md), [04](models-and-valuations-credit/04-structural-default.md), [06](models-and-valuations-credit/06-recovery-and-lgd.md), [09](models-and-valuations-credit/09-copula-kernels.md), [10](models-and-valuations-credit/10-portfolio-loss-and-correlated-recovery.md), [12](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md), [15](models-and-valuations-credit/15-cds-option-quadrature.md), [17](models-and-valuations-credit/17-tranche-loss-distribution.md), [18](models-and-valuations-credit/18-tranche-projection.md), [19](models-and-valuations-credit/19-tranche-risk.md).

- **Decision — Distress-score population and classification scope.** Agree supported populations, vintage adjustments, zone rationale and any mapping from Altman scores to PD; retain the distinction between formula reproduction and calibrated default forecasting. Records: [01](models-and-valuations-credit/01-financial-distress-scores.md).

- **Decision — Leverage hazard and PIK exercise contract.** Agree caps, missing-state rules, time-grid accuracy and coefficient calibration. Specify whether monthly first-passage monitoring and one-action equity comparison meet the intended PIK valuation contract. Records: [05](models-and-valuations-credit/05-hazard-and-toggle.md).

- **Decision — Prepayment response and pool-correlation use.** Approve simplified refinancing/burnout response, baseline-expectation semantics, cap behavior and factor units. Quantify quadrature error and ensure consuming callers validate bumped matrix positive semidefiniteness. Records: [08](models-and-valuations-credit/08-prepayment-and-pool-correlation.md).

- **Decision — CDS default and convention boundaries.** Agree the default convention and intended reference-system scopes, exact clean/par/accrual conventions, same-day cash handling, date-inversion failure behavior and settlement approximation. Records: [0030](models-and-valuations-credit/0030-cds-survival-and-integration.md), [12](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md).

- **Observed — CDS par-spread denominator descriptions disagree with the kernel.** Source prose describes the default as excluding accrued-on-default, or the full-premium variant as only adding it. Both Bloomberg denominator branches include accrued-on-default; the clean branch subtracts accrued and the full-premium branch does not. Reconcile the source descriptions with the calculation, without treating the documentation discrepancy as proof of a pricing defect. Records: [12](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md).

## Shared valuation framework

[Scope inventory](valuations-framework/README.md).

- **Observed — Overnight engines and derivative paths differ.** Valuations projects a next-business-day interval; cashflows projects one day and carries the rate, so nonflat curves can differ. Whole-coupon cutoff is allowed here but rejected by cashflows; unrepresentable cutoff is ignored. Same-day observations project, unlike some TRS paths. Telescoping returns zero derivatives unless exposures force explicit calculation. Adopt parity, cutoff and derivative contracts, including whether product-specific publication timing justifies the same-day difference. Reject or explicitly bound oversized cutoff inputs and qualify holiday/lookback/cutoff behavior and derivatives with independent finite-difference comparisons. Records: [04](valuations-framework/overnight-projection.md).

- **Observed — TRS constructor, serde and reporting boundaries differ.** The constructor infers overnight compounding; omitted JSON chooses Simple and can fail validation. Internal empty-calendar fallback cannot rescue public validation. Annuity excludes by payment date despite accrual-end prose, and standalone par spread lacks currency-equality validation. Reconcile construction and calculation contracts, correct the annuity documentation, agree same-day fixing/payment policies, preserve annuity reconciliation across overnight conventions, and establish explicit currency validation for standalone par-spread callers. Records: [06](valuations-framework/trs-legs.md).

- **Observed — Generic volatility risk does not cover every pricing source.** Pricing resolves cube, ordinary surface then FX-delta surface; surface-ID discovery probes only ordinary surfaces. Consumer-specific support is required for complete risk. Scalar vol overrides can create node-vega residuals and make market-surface implied-vol breakeven ineffective. Define dependency/source-aware risk and override behavior, including repair or explicit rejection of scalar-vol overrides for iterative vol breakeven. Adopt first-scalar roles, the fixed FX01 shock, relevant-strike footprint, zero-vol policies and residual threshold. Establish independent bump/path convergence around exercise, barrier and volatility boundaries. Records: [01](valuations-framework/pricing-lifecycle.md), [11](valuations-framework/option-sensitivities.md), [13](valuations-framework/carry-theta-breakeven.md).

- **Decision — Partial rate/credit dependencies and fallback errors.** DV01 silently omits unresolved declared curves when at least one resolves; quote CS01 takes first credit/discount dependencies. Direct-hazard down-bump treats every error as permission for one-sided risk. Absent/inapplicable cross factors can return zero. Adopt missing-data/applicability policy and multi-curve coverage, including partial rate dependencies when a quote override avoids missing market access. Agree risk definitions for direct-built hazards and recovery bounds, signs, reporting units, curve-role selection and boundary handling. Establish independent calibrated quote-bump, direct-hazard approximation, recovery-stencil and cross-partial convergence evidence, including both orders of rate/credit recalibration. Records: [09](valuations-framework/finite-difference-rates.md), [10](valuations-framework/credit-sensitivities.md), [12](valuations-framework/cross-factor-risk.md).

- **Observed — Metric identities and tiny bump widths can lose information.** Rounded z-spread cash-band labels and duplicate series/matrix labels overwrite flattened scalar keys. sensitivity_central_diff debug-asserts on tiny bumps but returns zero in release, unlike checked width validation. Adopt unique identity and consistent width-error contracts. Reject or explicitly define duplicate qualified series/matrix labels and arbitrary fractional-year grids, and qualify key-label uniqueness. Records: [08](valuations-framework/metric-orchestration.md), [09](valuations-framework/finite-difference-rates.md), [10](valuations-framework/credit-sensitivities.md).

- **Decision — Historical risk estimator and horizon.** Directional Taylor uses jointly shifted markets, domain-aware symmetric/forward stencils, interpolated VaR and the integral of its quantile for ES; both report negative loss P&L. Under 20 scenarios only warns, dates/window are metadata, and valuation stays as-of. Adopt signs, estimator/horizon, low-sample acceptance and the metadata-only history window. Establish independent stencil/convergence comparisons near zero volatility and option exercise boundaries. Records: [14](valuations-framework/historical-risk.md).

- **Observed — Cashflow export validation and missing metadata.** Direct export sets trusted validation after eligibility alone despite an already-validated precondition. Balance replay failures become absent metadata and missing ratios remain null despite logging prose. Adopt validation/reporting boundaries, same-day exclusion, first-curve selection, metadata omission and the max(1e-6,1e-6*abs(PV)) reconciliation tolerance with as-of FX/per-row Money conversion; malformed-input effects remain untested. Records: [15](valuations-framework/cashflow-export.md).

- **Observed — Model controls depend on entry path.** Zero path override means default to the resolver but is rejected by ModelConfig. HW accepts monetary numeric scalars where Heston requires unitless. GBM near-zero terminal cumulative log-DF falls back to scale one rather than forcing exact terminal match. Reconcile helper/public domains and scalar units; agree volatility metadata/override precedence, explicit shift mismatch tolerance and zero-vol domains. Adopt drift rescaling with independent nonflat-curve/date-clock tests, document which pricer consumes each simulation override, and establish path/grid convergence for product defaults/caps independently of resource limits. Records: [02](valuations-framework/model-resolution.md).

- **Evidence — Convention registry authority and effective dates.** Qualify North-American fallback for unknown CDS currency, first-explicit-noncustom family selection, case-dependent ANY overwrite, major-zero/arbitrary-namespace parsing and uneven calendar/lag checks. Broad commodity calendars and bond presets need primary venue/effective-date provenance. Agree fallback families, default values and effective dates and establish primary-source provenance for every registry row. Records: [16](valuations-framework/convention-selection.md).

- **Observed — Inflation interpolation and lag controls are conditional.** The supplied interpolation governs monthly-anchor mode only; other historical lookup uses index interpolation and future lookup uses curve dates. Unrepresentable curve-month lag becomes no lag. Adopt explicit publication/lag and override boundaries. Decide whether unrepresentable curve lag and the invalid-DF effective-rate accessor should error instead of returning no lag/zero rate. Independently benchmark partial publication of adjacent months, leap years and contract-level overrides. Records: [17](valuations-framework/inflation-references.md).

- **Observed — Shared contract validation and defaults need reconciliation.** Local Bermudan price>0 accepts positive infinity; stochastic mode default 5,000 differs from helper minimum 50 and enum/schema minimum two. Result constructors include zero placeholders and usd_jpy helper stores JPY base/USD quote. Establish intended domains/names without assuming downstream failures. Adopt default thresholds and construction-path differences, and close or explicitly constrain positive-infinite call prices and placeholder-result ambiguity. Records: [19](valuations-framework/shared-contract-controls.md).

- **Decision — Margin bridge failure, bucket and identity semantics.** Any pricing failure becomes None/empty for product proxies. Tenors round up, supplied curve IDs are not deduplicated and NaN/infinity maps to the last bucket. Entity inference strips at most two suffixes and returns unknown suffixes silently; explicit credit classification is required. Adopt product-specific proxy, bucket and identity assumptions against the intended margin methodology/version. Establish independent tenor-boundary, duplicated-curve and pricing-failure behavior evidence. Records: [18](valuations-framework/margin-bridge.md).

- **Observed — Generated builder defaults and validation are not uniform.** Required defaults evaluate eagerly even when supplied; annotations on Option fields or non-Option attributes can parse but be ignored. Custom validators replace validate_invariants. Define structural-versus-pricing validation, custom-validator replacement and default-evaluation contracts. Add dedicated consumer/macro evidence, including compile-fail cases for unsupported attributes/type shapes; assess eager fallible/panicking default expressions at actual call sites before classifying a product defect. No resulting mispricing was reproduced. Records: [20](valuations-framework/builder-validation.md).

- **Decision — Carry and breakeven naming and fallbacks.** Normal metric dependency resolution can skip missing/inapplicable YTM and activate carry fallback, while actual YTM errors propagate. Approximate yield breakeven uses sensitivity conventions; iterative yield breakeven shifts discount curves rather than reinverting Street yield. Adopt these economics plus payment boundaries, cash taxonomy, funding/reinvestment assumptions, clocks and observable-state rules per product. Distinguish approximate targets from actual quote-space solves and establish a canonical reference for the precise constant-yield carry split. Records: [08](valuations-framework/metric-orchestration.md), [13](valuations-framework/carry-theta-breakeven.md).

- **Decision — Pricing lifecycle and enrichment contracts.** Agree equivalent-instrument metric semantics, duplicate-measure/seed precedence, instrument-replacement cache ownership, trusted-validation entry boundaries and the acceptability of raw-versus-Money repricing for small finite differences. Present request provenance is not a complete replay artifact; qualify any stronger claim against retained market/model/calibration state. Records: [01](valuations-framework/pricing-lifecycle.md), [08](valuations-framework/metric-orchestration.md).

- **Decision — Listed future-option lifecycle and tree controls.** Adopt the fixed-date cash-American European approximation, expiry-date Greek/PV asymmetry, settlement-date inclusion and whether reference quotes belong in each reporting quantity. Establish an accuracy tolerance and independent Greek/grid stability for the 401-step price versus 201-step risk defaults and additive normal tree. Confirm post-trading quote requirements and contract specification provenance/effective dates. Records: [03](valuations-framework/listed-products.md).

- **Evidence — Rates-credit grid and floating-reset alignment.** Agree accrual-start versus reset-date descriptor alignment for fixing-lag products, omitted incomplete descriptors and deterministic zero-parameter defaults. Independently qualify date interpolation and recovery integration/convergence for negative rates, mismatched day counts, stubs and lagged resets. Records: [05](valuations-framework/rates-credit-grid.md).

- **Decision — Observation-grid boundary contracts.** Adopt or tighten reversed daily horizons, one-date close-to-close sample fraction at maturity and contractual rather than realized-spacing annualization. Establish independent month-end deduplication, holiday, one-point and future-monitoring-window evidence; an enum named Continuous does not establish exact crossing or reconstruct prior breach state. Records: [07](valuations-framework/observation-grids.md).

## Rates valuations

[Scope inventory](valuations-rates/README.md).

- **Observed — Dependency reports omit forward curves actually used.** FRA with observed_fixing can still prefer an available forward for today/future reset while omitting forward/fixing dependencies. IRS same-ID compounded OIS and overnight caps can prefer an existing forward object although ID equality removes it from dependencies. Reconcile active-source reporting and generic risk coverage. Records: [02-FRA](valuations-rates/02-fra.md), [03-INTEREST-RATE-SWAPS](valuations-rates/03-interest-rate-swaps.md), [05-CAP-FLOOR-PRICING](valuations-rates/05-cap-floor-pricing.md). For IRS, agree lag-sentinel/EOM inference and additive spread conventions and disclose quote-versus-curve risk bases.

- **Observed — LMM sparse-ITM exercise uses future path information.** At ITM count<=basis_degree+3, the adapter chooses max(immediate, realized future chosen payoff), including out-of-sample mode. HW instead skips a sparse exercise date. Adopt a stable stopping policy and independently qualify low-ITM/OOS bias beyond MC standard error. Records: [08-HULL-WHITE-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md), [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Observed — Bermudan contract gates vary by engine.** HW tree rejects NonCoTerminal; HW LSMC ignores the enum and values a common terminal maturity. LMM enforces physical/co-terminal schedules for future exercise but can price exercise today through HW before full validation. Reject unsupported contracts consistently or adopt actual implemented cashflows. Records: [08-HULL-WHITE-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md), [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Observed — LMM controls and reported risks do not match all advertised inputs.** The adapter fixes two constant factors/common displacement max(0,1%-min forward), needs lmm_base_vol and ignores instrument mc_paths. Basis degrees alter thresholds without the expected regressors; HwSigmaVega is not base-vol risk and expected exercise time is not published. Reconcile controls and qualify parameter calibration. Records: [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Decision — HW LSMC basis, integration and exercise-time meaning.** PolynomialBasis ignores auxiliary annuity, defaults are in-sample and trapezoidal bank integration is not made exact by exact marginal steps. Tree exercise time conditions on positive future exercise, omits the separate exercise-today maximum and can be zero despite immediate value. Adopt basis/grid/sample and metric definitions. Records: [08-HULL-WHITE-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md).

- **Observed — Quoted futures risk can fail after clearing a usable quote.** Model eligibility accepts any adjustment, any surface ID or every non-Simple compounding mode without confirming inputs. DV01 clears the live quote and propagates missing-HW/wrong-or-missing-surface errors, with no restoration. Define the intended selection/error contract and test quoted overnight cases. Records: [10-RATE-FUTURES](valuations-rates/10-rate-futures.md).

- **Observed — Inflation price, inverse and reporting domains differ.** Observed finite end CPI can be nonpositive: price/flows validate starts but not all ends, while zero-coupon par inversion rejects nonpositive ratio. YoY raw flows keep pay==as-of although PV excludes it. Align data/price/inverse validation and adopt filtering/reconciliation; shared observation roots are in core market data. Records: [13-INFLATION-SWAPS](valuations-rates/13-inflation-swaps.md).

- **Observed — CMS risk and cashflow export can mix methods.** Convexity risk subtracts a zero-correction Black value from a selected replication base; projected cashflows use the approximation. Default Vanna differentiates only local Black plus convexity-vega terms, omitting other smile/forward effects. Adopt method-consistent labels and independent full-derivative evidence.
