Records: [11-CMS-CONVEXITY](valuations-rates/11-cms-convexity.md), [12-CMS-REPLICATION](valuations-rates/12-cms-replication.md).

- **Observed — Repo collateral ratios discard currency.** Collateral Money becomes an amount while required collateral is cash-currency amount; coverage and implied return divide without FX or same-currency checks. Implied collateral return is annualized static excess rather than modeled investment return. Define admissible currency and interpretation. Records: [15-REPO](valuations-rates/15-repo.md).

- **Decision — IRS and repo margin proxies.** IRS accepts any nonempty repriced bucket set then otherwise substitutes duration/notional with warning; completeness depends on shared extraction. Repo assigns duration proxy wholly to 3M/6M even for long maturities. Adopt approximation eligibility, complete-risk tests and calibration evidence. Records: [03-INTEREST-RATE-SWAPS](valuations-rates/03-interest-rate-swaps.md), [15-REPO](valuations-rates/15-repo.md). Confirm open-repo effective-maturity scope separately from unmodeled termination optionality.

- **Decision — XCCY reset dates and deterministic reset convexity.** Initial historical/same-day FX uses raw common start; future initial CIP uses constant-leg adjusted start even if resetting dates differ. Subsequent reset/rebalancing uses raw resetting-period start. Adopt calendar/same-day conventions and independently quantify omitted stochastic FX/rate reset convexity. Records: [16-CROSS-CURRENCY-SWAPS](valuations-rates/16-cross-currency-swaps.md). Adopt collateral/basis assumptions and spread-outside-compounding for each supported contract.

- **Decision — Deposit and FRA economic domains.** Deposits can emit nonpositive redemption for very negative rates although inversion rejects it; FRA rejects only a near-zero settlement denominator, not a substantially negative one. Establish supported rate/denominator domains and consistent price/quote checks. Records: [01-DEPOSITS](valuations-rates/01-deposits.md), [02-FRA](valuations-rates/02-fra.md). Include deposit no-calendar and valuation-date conventions, FRA same-day fixing policy, and disclosure of quote-replayed versus direct-curve DV01.

- **Decision — Rate-option Greek, premium and tenor conventions.** Swaption quote Greeks freeze cash annuity/smile and use month-difference/12 tenor ignoring day-of-month. HW cap surface vega can be ineffective with fixed sigma and require Normal surface despite explicit pricing needing none; inflation vega can ignore an override. Caplet IV targets gross option value while PV may include premium. Adopt distinct model/quote experiments and units. Records: [06-CAP-FLOOR-RISK](valuations-rates/06-cap-floor-risk.md), [07-EUROPEAN-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/07-european-swaptions.md), [14-INFLATION-OPTIONS](valuations-rates/14-inflation-options.md). Agree caplet inverse brackets/domains, theta fixing-crossing behavior and complete overnight derivative coverage. Adopt swaption cash-annuity/stub approximations and post-expiry lifecycle; qualify exact-expiry and independent cash-settlement benchmarks. Distinguish quote-replayed from direct-curve risks.

- **Evidence — HW caps and Bermudan numerical qualification.** Benchmark Gaussian coupon moment matching for high vol, long/partially fixed periods and observation rules; qualify mid-coupon clipping, grids, bases, samples and OOS policy for HW/LMM. Shared-process numeraire tests are not independent accuracy evidence; add Longstaff–Schwartz and product LMM calibration provenance. Records: [05-CAP-FLOOR-PRICING](valuations-rates/05-cap-floor-pricing.md), [08-HULL-WHITE-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md), [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md). Adopt aggregate overnight quote interpretation, schedule defaults, scalar/schedule sigma precedence, prepared-tree reuse and each engine's contract/metric availability. Set model-domain, calibration-target and numerical acceptance criteria under [ADR 0029](valuations-rates/0029-rate-option-exercise-and-volatility.md); distinguish slow/ignored Bermudan cases and local-formula/broad-range tests labelled QuantLib parity from independently reproduced vendor evidence.

- **Decision — CMS approximation and negative-rate scope.** Adopt flat-yield/payment-delay weighting and index mappings. The replication error recommends negative-rate Black/Hagan/Bachelier despite the implemented Black branch requiring positive rates; correct that suggestion and define supported regimes. Records: [11-CMS-CONVEXITY](valuations-rates/11-cms-convexity.md), [12-CMS-REPLICATION](valuations-rates/12-cms-replication.md).

- **Evidence — CMS replication tail and normalization.** Adopt normalized expectation, clamped smile tail, finite width and adaptive 16-node tolerance 1e-11/depth 16; qualify skew, high vol, delay and deep strikes. Normalization does not establish smile arbitrage-freedom. Records: [12-CMS-REPLICATION](valuations-rates/12-cms-replication.md).

- **Decision — CMS spread model identity.** StaticReplication selects conditional two-lognormal integration using fixed 20-node Gauss–Hermite, flat-strike marginals, Gaussian correlation and approximate marginal convexity. Qualify naming, joint-measure assumptions and convergence near correlation boundaries. Records: [17-CMS-SPREAD](valuations-rates/17-cms-spread.md). Validate correlation ±1, the low-volatility floor, near-expiry states and skew rejection against independent integration or Monte Carlo evidence.

- **Evidence — Futures convexity and mapped option DV01.** Qualify fixing+two-calendar-day default, Normal-vol approximation/5% ceiling, explicit-adjustment scaling and overnight Gaussian assumptions. Independently validate quoted-value/model-risk distinction and delta-plus-premium-discount option DV01. Records: [10-RATE-FUTURES](valuations-rates/10-rate-futures.md).

- **Decision — Inflation deterministic scope and contract conventions.** Projected CPI ratios are not stochastic ratio expectations. Adopt publication/reference expiry, OneOne stubs and YoY conventions; qualify CPI rebase warning and unpublished-start option approximation with independent jurisdiction cases. Records: [13-INFLATION-SWAPS](valuations-rates/13-inflation-swaps.md), [14-INFLATION-OPTIONS](valuations-rates/14-inflation-options.md). Include CPI lag/publication policies, OneOne partial-year handling, nonannual option coupons and quote-convention domains. Preserve shared flow filtering in YoY cashflow/PV reconciliation tests.

- **Decision — Basis-swap contract conventions and reconciliation.** Adopt per-leg discounting, explicit same-curve/weekends-only fallback opt-ins, additive spread and absolute/incremental par-spread bp units. Qualify cashflow/PV valuation-date filtering, low-notional inversion boundaries and per-curve risk-vector aggregation. Establish primary market references for supported contract combinations rather than inferring them from generic FloatLegSpec acceptance. Record: [04-BASIS-SWAPS](valuations-rates/04-basis-swaps.md).

- **Observed — Cap/floor Auto prose disagrees with executable quote selection.** Reconcile the [Auto variant prose](../../../finstack-quant/valuations/rates/src/cap_floor/types.rs) and [test introduction](../../../finstack-quant/valuations/rates/tests/cap_floor/validation/auto_vol_type.rs), which promise negative-rate conversion and no source-metadata inspection, with the [resolver](../../../finstack-quant/valuations/core/src/instruments/common_impl/vol_resolution.rs) and assertions that preserve quote conventions and reject invalid Black coordinates. Record: [05-CAP-FLOOR-PRICING](valuations-rates/05-cap-floor-pricing.md).

## Bond valuations

[Scope inventory](valuations-bonds/README.md).

- **Investigate — Merton step-up and custom coupon economics.** The adapter takes only step-up initial rate and custom dates/accruals, not custom coupon amounts, into a scalar-coupon engine. Reproduce deterministic no-default schedule PV and decide supported domain versus full coupon preservation. Records: [08](valuations-bonds/08-merton-simulation.md).

- **Investigate — Merton calibration and final discount basis differ.** Calibration precedes automatic curve-DF population, so absent supplied knots it uses a flat horizon-implied rate while final pricing uses curve knots. The residual stays the low-path calibration result. Reproduce curved-term targets and adopt consistent discount/final-residual acceptance. Records: [09](valuations-bonds/09-merton-calibration.md). Agree cash-equivalent target semantics, tolerance/budget suitability and final-price diagnostics after reproducing both discount and carry discrepancies.

- **Investigate — Merton settlement target misses carry terms.** Dirty target converts to valuation PV by a DF without canonical detached/intervening cash; YTM and other target types can use different entitlement. Reproduce coupon/ex-coupon events within settlement lag and reconcile calibration cash basis. Records: [09](valuations-bonds/09-merton-calibration.md).

- **Investigate — Dynamic joint floating coupons may bypass ex-coupon entitlement.** Replay builds dynamic groups from raw metadata while static cash uses the holder-entitlement filter; no corresponding dynamic exclusion was found. Compare zero/positive-vol FRNs at ex-coupon boundaries before classifying numerical impact. Records: [06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md).

- **Investigate — Joint and hazard recovery balances use different dates.** Joint replay applies principal-effective events; hazard/JTD retain cash repayment exposure through actual cash dates. Reproduce lagged amortization and adopt separate coupon/recovery balance semantics where needed. Records: [06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md), [17](valuations-bonds/17-default-risk.md).

- **Decision — Asset-swap path conventions.** Custom/workout annuity uses ID-derived SOFR annual ACT/360 and EURIBOR-6M semiannual conventions; ordinary settlement uses forward tenor. Carry/ex-coupon/settlement and Floating-versus-Fixed-placeholder requirements differ. Adopt intended domains and independent convention examples. Records: [13](valuations-bonds/13-asset-swaps.md).

- **Observed — CS01 comment confuses absolute and relative finite-difference error.** Source prose claims forward/central agreement around 1e-8 of CS01 magnitude; the usual expansion gives O(h²) absolute price difference and O(h) relative first-order sensitivity. Correct the claim and specify measured tolerance; this alone is not a pricing defect. Records: [16](valuations-bonds/16-spread-credit-risk.md).

- **Decision — Native bond dispatch, credit recovery and HW inputs.** Adopt model selection, daily fractional recovery of par without accrued recovery, credit treatment of detached cash and scalar-only HW bond inputs. Sigma schedules are rejected; source presence elsewhere does not provide bond support. Records: [02](valuations-bonds/02-settlement-and-clocks.md), [03](valuations-bonds/03-pv-models.md), [04](valuations-bonds/04-rates-tree-exercise.md). Agree quote-pinned PV semantics, daily integration resolution, ACT/365L rejection domains and the public callable-bond default and rates-only domains under [ADR 0028](valuations-bonds/0028-callable-bond-model-selection.md).

- **Decision — Exercise grid and event ordering.** Adopt window/endpoints/payment/month-end opportunities, put then friction-threshold call on continuation with current cash added outside the exercise comparison, omitted put make-whole and deterministic-reference make-whole in the rates-only path. Records: [04](valuations-bonds/04-rates-tree-exercise.md). Specify friction units, supported put make-whole semantics, reference-curve treatment and approximation acceptance criteria.

- **Decision — Return floors create additional issuer opportunities.** MOIC/XIRR floors can create calls absent from the original schedule, exclude fees/negative coupons/prepayments from protected distributions and do not guarantee maturity return. Historical distributions rebuild from current schedule/market. Adopt contractual eligibility and history provenance. Records: [05](valuations-bonds/05-return-floors.md).

- **Decision — Joint floating basis and LSMC error budget.** The floating index is fixed basis plus conditional discount-forward shock, without an independent basis factor. All-path standardized quadratic continuation uses independent stages with 20,000 default paths each; truncated SVD may retain full degree. MC intervals omit regression/model error. Adopt model, sampling and convergence criteria. Records: [06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md), [07](valuations-bonds/07-lsmc-policy.md). Include conditional make-whole regression and bias qualification in the acceptance criteria.

- **Decision — Merton toggle, barrier and output semantics.** EndogenousHazard only affects toggle state; structural default remains. Asset drift uses a separate risk-free rate, PIK does not raise configured debt barrier, coupons process at halfsteps after default checks, tail statistic is price rather than loss, and effective spread may return the last unconverged iterate. Adopt each contract and failure policy. Records: [08](valuations-bonds/08-merton-simulation.md).

- **Decision — Workout yield and inverse fallback.** YTW minimizes over voluntary puts, uses a distinct candidate grid and represents immediate cash with infinities. I-spread inverse can choose the lowest price when workout consistency fails. Adopt exit eligibility, grid and fallback behavior. Records: [11](valuations-bonds/11-workout-yield.md).

- **Decision — Bond spread inversions and meanings.** OAS uses trial-error sentinels without an independent final residual gate; DM freezes projection. G spread uses a par benchmark rather than sovereign cash-bond interpolation. Adopt convergence/failure policy and quote names. Records: [12](valuations-bonds/12-spread-quotes.md).

- **Decision — Yield and curve DV01 are different experiments.** Price quotes or exercise rights make default parallel DV01 yield-based while buckets remain curve risk. CallableOas YieldDv01 uses effective duration times yield-basis price; quoted option curve risk bumps one tree curve while other routes combine dependencies. Adopt labels, units and reconciliation. Records: [14](valuations-bonds/14-yield-risk.md), [15](valuations-bonds/15-curve-risk.md). Include convexity display scale, bucket aggregation, curve dependency selection and shock convergence requirements.

- **Decision — Bond CS01 fallback and value basis.** Credit CS01 recalibrates par quotes; no-credit fallback uses forward Z/OAS with settlement-value Z versus valuation-value OAS. Spread duration always uses Z, and bucket fallback is a parallel instrument-key result. Adopt intended risk decomposition and price basis. Records: [16](valuations-bonds/16-spread-credit-risk.md).

- **Decision — Recovery01 depends on curve provenance.** Recovery risk recalibrates or freezes hazard according to stored calibration provenance, potentially changing sign. Adopt experiment selection, disclosure and independent quote-preserving benchmarks. Records: [17](valuations-bonds/17-default-risk.md).

- **Decision — Bond vega is embedded-option model risk.** Vega is optional-minus-straight sigma sensitivity, not total-bond or market-vol risk; one-vol-point units differ for normal/lognormal sigma. Define output interpretation and parameter calibration domain. Records: [18](valuations-bonds/18-embedded-option-risk.md). Set noise/bump acceptance criteria and unit comparability across normal/lognormal models.

- **Decision — Bond life, carry and lifetime-return ownership.** Bond WAL uses cash dates/amounts and limited repayment kinds; schedule WAL uses economic/par balance semantics. Carry begins at valuation while YTM is settlement quoted. MOIC excludes negative holder flows, XIRR includes them; projected schedule is not verified lifetime ledger and early-exit XIRR errors are omitted. Adopt date/sign/failure contracts. Records: [19](valuations-bonds/19-carry-and-life.md), [20](valuations-bonds/20-lifetime-returns.md). Qualify broader lagged/amortizing carry reconciliation and consumer expectations for custom-schedule WAL distinctions.

- **Evidence — Independent bond validation and references.** Establish curved-credit, lagged principal, dynamic ex-coupon FRN, Merton cash-calibration, ASW and stochastic-risk benchmarks. Add precise BDT/BK, Longstaff–Schwartz and Moosmüller irregular-schedule provenance; internal routing/round-trip/seed tests do not establish these accuracy domains. Records: [02](valuations-bonds/02-settlement-and-clocks.md), [04](valuations-bonds/04-rates-tree-exercise.md), [06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md), [07](valuations-bonds/07-lsmc-policy.md), [08](valuations-bonds/08-merton-simulation.md), [09](valuations-bonds/09-merton-calibration.md), [10](valuations-bonds/10-quoted-yield.md), [13](valuations-bonds/13-asset-swaps.md), [15](valuations-bonds/15-curve-risk.md), [16](valuations-bonds/16-spread-credit-risk.md), [17](valuations-bonds/17-default-risk.md), [18](valuations-bonds/18-embedded-option-risk.md). Establish exact security/market applicability for quoted-yield conventions, plus adoption evidence for each option/default-sensitive method; located tests do not complete these tasks.

- **Decision — Bond construction and custom-schedule conventions.** Adopt registry lookup fallback, currency/default constructor conventions and custom frequency inference; establish consistency between custom cash amounts, coupon-spec metadata and supported model domains. Record: [01](valuations-bonds/01-schedule-and-holder-cashflows.md).

## Other fixed income

[Scope inventory](valuations-other-fixed-income/README.md).

- **Observed — Convertible model identity differs from residual terminology.** Total and cash components share DF*survival continuation plus fractional recovery; surviving stock drift includes hazard. This is not split-discount Tsiveriotis–Fernandes. Reconcile model/risk names and qualify the intended credit formulation independently. Records: [02](valuations-other-fixed-income/02-convertible-lattice.md), [03](valuations-other-fixed-income/03-convertible-quotes-risk.md).

- **Decision — Convertible dilution and soft-call timing.** All validated dilution events immediately alter conversion ratio without as-of filtering. Soft-call days-above requirements use BGK trigger uplift rather than historical observation counts. Adopt future/event/history semantics and approximation accuracy, including already-passed mandatory dates, dividend protection, terminal split smoothing, soft-call approximation provenance and grid convergence. Records: [02](valuations-other-fixed-income/02-convertible-lattice.md).

- **Observed — Convertible sensitivity contracts can alter validation/model paths.** Conversion01 bumps only ratio when ratio and price coexist, potentially violating consistency. Vega treats any down-reprice Validation error as one-sided permission; OAS shifts hazard when credit exists and discount otherwise. Define intended validation/fallback and economic risk experiment, reconcile synthetic-credit/OAS naming and set economically appropriate inverse-quote residual tolerances. Records: [03](valuations-other-fixed-income/03-convertible-quotes-risk.md).

- **Decision — Inflation-bond floor, yield and ownership.** Unknown protected CPI uses one Black ratio even with hybrid observed/projected anchors; published index-only data has zero projection risk. Breakeven compares nominal zero yield with real coupon-bond yield; real-yield same-day ownership differs from nominal PV. Horizon conversion has one-year error fallback and rounded frequency. Adopt hybrid publication fallback, floor-vol quote interpretation, jurisdiction contracts, unnormalized convexity units, same-day ownership, nominal breakeven reference and failure policy. Establish independent market/quote vectors. Records: [04](valuations-other-fixed-income/04-inflation-indexation.md), [05](valuations-other-fixed-income/05-inflation-yields-risk.md).

- **Decision — Loan EIR fee eligibility and inverse policy.** Default EIR can include commitment, LC and fronting fees without accounting eligibility/staging. IRR may be nonunique and margin bounds are checked after solving. Define quote units, accounting scope, multiple-root policy and currency-scaled residual tolerances. Establish independent OID schedules with delayed draws and PIK. Records: [06](valuations-other-fixed-income/06-loan-quotes-eir.md).

- **Decision — Term-loan principal and fee timing.** Over-amortization caps to funded balance; floating margin changes snap to accrual boundaries unlike fixed steps. Commitment-fee integration grids draws/commitment changes but needs other-principal-event cases. Adopt absolute OID allocation after draw stops and contractual timing. Records: [07](valuations-other-fixed-income/07-term-loan-cashflows.md).

- **Investigate — Term-loan curve anchor and final-payment cutoff.** Rates-only lattice fit/corrections use settlement-relative time with disc.df(t); reproduce nonflat curves based before settlement. Legal-maturity zero cutoff may differ from adjusted unpaid final-cash ownership. Establish consistent clock/lifecycle behavior. Records: [08](valuations-other-fixed-income/08-term-loan-pricing.md).

- **Decision — Term-loan exercise and workout candidates.** Soft/Hard calls share rules, friction is a threshold rather than cash fee, and floating calls between reset/payment are disabled. YTW has fewer candidates than standing tree calls; as-of-selected n-year/call dates can precede settlement. Adopt model-specific rationale, approximation/grid/tolerance limits and supported domains, including reset-boundary exercise policy and the finite workout candidate set. Records: [08](valuations-other-fixed-income/08-term-loan-pricing.md), [09](valuations-other-fixed-income/09-term-loan-risk-yields.md).

- **Decision — Term-loan selected model and credit risk.** Quote risk fits/holds OAS or z-spread. Hazard CS01/default risk require Tree plus hazard; explicit Discounting may ignore an attached hazard curve. Confirm selected versus native repricing, especially Recovery01, adopt the signed callable-minus-straight option-value convention and disclose hazard-versus-z-spread risk scope. Records: [09](valuations-other-fixed-income/09-term-loan-risk-yields.md).

- **Decision — Revolver anchor and cash timing.** Current drawn is anchor state, not origination history. Stochastic accrual averages endpoint utilization and funds changes at period midpoint; subinterval overnight averaging can use whole-period compounding. Adopt timing error budgets, LC fee inheritance and fee floors, and establish independent seasoned-state cashflow examples. Records: [10](valuations-other-fixed-income/10-revolver-cashflows.md).

- **Observed — Revolver zero-vol and Constant modes change economics.** Tiny utilization sigma also freezes drift/spread response. Constant credit spread is a CIR process with kappa 0.01 and sigma 0.001 rather than constant state; draw-option attribution forces Constant to zero. Reconcile names, limits and counterfactual semantics. Records: [11](valuations-other-fixed-income/11-revolver-monte-carlo-credit.md), [12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md).

- **Evidence — Revolver calibration, discretization and maturity.** MarketAnchored fits current/average hazard, not the complete survival curve; spread vol/correlation default to 0.3. Weekly simulation retains only contractual endpoints for rate/hazard integration; recovery stops at legal maturity and hazard freezes after final observation. Establish empirical parameter provenance, survival-calibration accuracy, statistical/grid convergence and unpaid-payment policy. Agree absent-flow versus present-rate averaging semantics for expected schedules. Records: [11](valuations-other-fixed-income/11-revolver-monte-carlo-credit.md).

- **Observed — Revolver expected loss omits some configured credit and draw effects.** Without credit_curve_id the metric is zero even for explicit CIR spread. With hazard, the no-credit rerun removes spread/contingent funding; same seed does not preserve utilization if spread affects draws. Define exposure/counterfactual coverage and correct identical-path claims. Records: [12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md).

- **Decision — Revolver quote, accrual and EIR history.** MC quotes use anchor drawn at settlement/expected flows; accrued prorates the full coupon instead of elapsed balances with default day-count context. EIR rebuilds issue funding from current drawn. Adopt seasoned-state, history and day-count domains. Records: [12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md).

- **Decision — MBS amortization and delayed ownership.** WAM amortizes but legal maturity independently stops, potentially truncating residual balance. Negative/tiny WAC uses straight-line principal, sub-cent balances retire and seasoning uses days/30.4375. Holder in-flight P&I differs from purchaser quote ownership. Require coherent inputs and servicing/jurisdiction evidence; adopt the sub-cent cutoff, seasoning approximation, coupon/fee consistency tolerance, supported prepayment domains and custom-delay interpretation. Records: [13](valuations-other-fixed-income/13-mbs-projection.md).

- **Observed — MBS OAS and effective-risk prepayment experiments differ.** Effective rate risk scales CPR/PSA exponentially; MC OAS scales SMM. Buckets compress curve movement to a WAM-endpoint rate. Defaults kappa 0.05, sigma 0.01 and 512 paths are uncalibrated; monthly left-point/delay approximation has no MC error report and zero-vol comparison allows 0.5% PV. Adopt model accuracy/cost budgets, CPR-versus-SMM consistency, endpoint-reduced bucketed-risk scope, default parameter provenance and borrower calibration. Establish MC convergence and economically scaled OAS acceptance. Records: [14](valuations-other-fixed-income/14-mbs-risk-oas.md).

- **Decision — TBA assumptions, delivery and dollar-roll reporting.** Generic collateral fixes factor one, 25bp servicing, 25bp guarantee (6bp GNMA), PSA one; only part of delivery eligibility is checked and no delivery option is priced. Front-pool carry and newly assumed back-pool PV differ. TBA rejects invoice-only delivery export, while roll cash legs omit collateral. Adopt scope, assumptions, shared front/back refinancing adjustment and reporting reconciliation. Establish exchange/dealer valuation vectors before broader adoption. Records: [15](valuations-other-fixed-income/15-tba-and-dollar-roll.md).

- **Decision — Fixed-income index TRS is a carry/proxy model.** Exponential-yield carry omits constituent price, roll-down and credit MTM. DurationDv01/SIMM are signed N*duration*1bp rather than PV derivatives; one IR bucket omits financing/index-credit risk while VM uses carry PV. Negative finite duration is permitted. Adopt valuation/analytics/margin uses, yield day-count convention, fixing/initial-level precedence, negative-duration domain and missing-risk policy; establish an independent constituent-index reconciliation. Records: [16](valuations-other-fixed-income/16-fi-index-trs.md).

- **Evidence — Bond-future exchange scope.** CTD and factor are caller-supplied; a CME-style factor helper does not cover every exchange. No basket optimization, delivery option or convexity is modeled. Establish exchange-rule provenance, contract-specific factor fixtures and accuracy limits/fair-value scope for the selected-CTD proxy; no independent delivery-option benchmark is established. Records: [01](valuations-other-fixed-income/01-bond-futures.md).

## Equity and composition

[Scope inventory](valuations-equity-and-composition/README.md).

- **Investigate — Private-fund partial catch-up may misstate the next hurdle.** CatchUp returns LP cash without updating the within-distribution LP accumulator later used by PromoteTier and row IRR. Reproduce partial catch-up followed by another hurdle against an independent fund-agreement fixture; existing paid-share/conservation tests do not establish that sequence. Resolve the LP accumulator discrepancy before relying on downstream hurdles or row IRR; retain residual tolerances in the independent fixture. Records: [18](valuations-equity-and-composition/18-private-fund-waterfall.md).

- **Observed — Continuous Dividend01 bypasses selected-model repricing.** Continuous yield risk calls instrument.value, while discrete-dividend and principal Greeks use context dispatch. A selected alternative PV can therefore carry native-model continuous-dividend risk. Agree consistent selection and evidence. Agree the one-day backward theta branch near expiry, higher-Greek time scenarios, finite-difference units and native-model IV interpretation alongside an alternative-model PV. Records: [08](valuations-equity-and-composition/08-selected-risk-and-implied-vol.md).

- **Decision — Future model sensitivities differ from mark-driven PV.** Listed equity/total-return future marks may be quoted or settled while Delta/spread risk differentiates model fair quote; a frozen quoted PV can have nonzero model Delta. Quanto Delta also freezes sampled ATM vols. Define mark versus model exposure and labels. Agree settlement-date reporting and qualify an independent quanto benchmark. Adopt the total-return future spread sign and simple accrual against intended contract specifications, with independent contract examples. Records: [03](valuations-equity-and-composition/03-equity-future.md), [04](valuations-equity-and-composition/04-total-return-future.md).

- **Observed — Property capitalization assumes annual NOI without a frequency guard.** Direct cap uses the first future individual NOI, terminal cap the last flow grown one year, and cash-on-cash is not annualized, although monthly/quarterly schedules are accepted. Specify schedule units/frequency or normalization and add independent cases. Qualify first-period return interpretation and near-zero cap-rate shocks. Records: [20](valuations-equity-and-composition/20-property-valuation.md).

- **Observed — Basket constituent risk is scenario P&L with overlapping factors.** ConstituentDelta is 1% quote-shock P&L rather than a divided derivative. Shared market IDs cause duplicated overlapping component shocks, while embedded instruments replace only the selected constituent with a synthetic mark. Adopt naming, units and unique-factor attribution. Records: [22](valuations-equity-and-composition/22-basket-nav-and-risk.md).

- **Decision — Composite history is fixed-capital financed P&L.** Period return divides P&L by configured capital and resets PV after rebalancing; the compounded index starts at 100. No cash/funding/transaction/slippage ledger exists. Adopt that interpretation and use a separate ledger benchmark if self-financing wealth returns are intended. Define cashflow-completeness requirements and explicit financing assumptions; independently reconcile a coupon-paying, cross-currency, rebalanced fixture with a cash ledger before treating output as a self-financing strategy return. Records: [24](valuations-equity-and-composition/24-composite-valuation-history.md).

- **Observed — Composite schema example uses a different index scale.** The example reports 1.0125 for 1.25% return, while runtime starts at 100 and implies 101.25. Correct the schema example without treating it as a runtime return-calculation error. Preserve the distinction between host display units and formula units when correcting the example; no host-wrapper parity or runtime gate was executed in this audit. Records: [01](valuations-equity-and-composition/01-contract-dispatch.md), [24](valuations-equity-and-composition/24-composite-valuation-history.md).

- **Decision — Equity option lifecycle, Black76 name and dividends.** Expiry/exercise observations are required and settlement-day cash remains due; theta synthesizes expiry from current/rolled spot. Native Black76 selects spot Black–Scholes for European and LR for early exercise; European cash-dividend pricing deducts present values from spot; early-exercise pricing passes timed dividends into an [escrowed-reserve tree](models-numerical/dividends-and-lattice-exercise.md) that restores dividend reserves at exercise. Adopt naming, lifecycle and approximation conventions. Qualify physical post-exercise Greeks and cash-payment lag against intended contracts. Adopt tree step/stencil choices and obtain an independently sourced LR reference plus expiry, dividend and exercise-date convergence evidence. Records: [06](valuations-equity-and-composition/06-option-lifecycle.md), [07](valuations-equity-and-composition/07-black-tree-and-direct-greeks.md), [08](valuations-equity-and-composition/08-selected-risk-and-implied-vol.md).

- **Evidence — PDE, Heston and rough-volatility domains.** Qualify fixed Black grid/domain, American exclusions with future discrete dividends and Heston ADI bounds. Fourier/QE/rough adapters require fitted scalars without calibration. Independent grid/transform/path-bias budgets are needed; rough-Bergomi forward variance rejects nonpositive intervals and nonfinite stderr can become zero. Adopt allowed parameter regions and separate 1D/2D error budgets; establish independent evidence for American dividend-free and Heston tail regimes. Agree required Heston parameter-key scope and the surface dependency despite separate model parameters, integration/simulation budgets and independent reference tolerances. Agree rough ATM-forward strip use, rejection and diagnostic policies; qualify transform convergence and MC bias when variance-strip breaks do not align with the simulation grid. Records: [09](valuations-equity-and-composition/09-equity-pde.md), [10](valuations-equity-and-composition/10-heston-adapters.md), [11](valuations-equity-and-composition/11-rough-volatility-adapters.md).

- **Decision — Autocall and cliquet term-sheet state.** ATM term GBM uses discrete observations and pinned initial spot for risk. Memory coupons need not release at maturity; same-day fixing requirements differ from cliquet spot fallback. Adopt exact payoff, seasoning, duplicate-fixing and memory conventions with independent cases. Adopt ATM strip dynamics, grid/budget/confidence-interval rules and pinning conventions; resolve duplicate fixing handling where validation only checks positive fixings. Qualify barrier-risk convergence. For cliquets, adopt local/global clipping order and long-option scope, acceptable MC risk uncertainty and the distinct theta observation interval. Records: [12](valuations-equity-and-composition/12-path-gbm.md), [13](valuations-equity-and-composition/13-autocallable.md), [14](valuations-equity-and-composition/14-cliquet.md).

- **Evidence — Variance swap history, strip tails and vega.** Finite strike strips have no ATM fallback; weights use scheduled counts and OHLC estimator selection. Analytical Vega is not full smile-surface repricing. Qualify adjusted-history provenance, tail coverage, estimator and risk interpretation. Adopt the flat-volatility override and forward-start threshold as well as estimator/sample weighting. Records: [15](valuations-equity-and-composition/15-variance-swap.md).

- **Decision — Equity TRS dividend income and margin proxy.** Dividend carry is simple continuous yield rather than reinvested compounded income; SIMM equity exposure is signed notional*1%, not modeled Delta. Adopt intended valuation and margin uses. Adopt dividend-payment conventions and curve-role assumptions. Qualify the notional-based SIMM proxy separately from valuation delta and define intended margin-use limits. Records: [16](valuations-equity-and-composition/16-equity-trs.md).

- **Decision — Corporate DCF clocks, terminal flow and dilution.** DCF fixes ACT/365.25/configured valuation date, spacing-based mid-year adjustment with annual-terminal exception and active-set treasury-stock dilution. Adopt annual terminal-flow guard, WACC/growth domains, bridge/discount and dilution assumptions. Establish independent dilution boundary cases and suitability criteria for each terminal model/valuation discount; preserve the distinction between explicit cashflow exports and complete equity valuation. Records: [17](valuations-equity-and-composition/17-corporate-dcf.md).

- **Decision — Private-fund residual value and shocks.** Without a curve, future LP cash sums undiscounted. NAV excludes future distributions/proceeds but can coexist with commitments; Nav01 scales historical/future realizations and NAV, and zero-paid-in DPI/TVPI becomes zero. Reconcile these definitions with fund agreements. Agree clawback timing, allowed-carry replay definition and residual tolerances. Reconcile future commitments and metadata day-count differences; performance adoption depends on resolving the partial-catch-up allocation discrepancy above. Records: [18](valuations-equity-and-composition/18-private-fund-waterfall.md), [19](valuations-equity-and-composition/19-private-fund-valuation.md).

- **Decision — Property valuation and leverage metrics.** DCF excludes purchase price but deducts acquisition costs; direct cap omits those costs and appraisal overrides model value. Exit payoff uses absolute outstanding principal without fees/accrual/breakage. LTV is debt PV/property PV, origination LTV uses first future draw, and DSCR depends on interval/kind tags. Adopt each measure and cashflow scope. Determine acceptable financing products and exit conventions; qualify DSCR period alignment and OriginationLTV for seasoned assets, using the annual-NOI policy established above. Records: [20](valuations-equity-and-composition/20-property-valuation.md), [21](valuations-equity-and-composition/21-levered-property.md).

- **Observed — Basket fee and notional boundaries.** expense_ratio is not finite/economically bounded at the contract boundary, one day of fee applies per valuation, notional multiplies monetary weighted NAV and the declared discount curve is unused. Adopt units, fee economics and validation. Agree the semantic role of the unused discount-curve dependency. Records: [22](valuations-equity-and-composition/22-basket-nav-and-risk.md).

- **Decision — Composite sizing, metric omission and rebalancing.** Volatility weights use unit-total-P&L volatility and one annualizer, not return/covariance risk parity. Unsupported primitive metrics are omitted if any primitive supports them; aggregate FX conversion creates no separate translation risk. Sparse rebalance dates execute at the next observation. Adopt exposure completeness and history policy. Adopt eligible weighting metrics and their units/signs, lookback/annualization rules, sparse-history treatment and expression availability. Establish expected hedge residuals for cross-currency/nonlinear legs and explicit treatment of placeholder cashflow exports. Records: [23](valuations-equity-and-composition/23-composite-sizing.md), [24](valuations-equity-and-composition/24-composite-valuation-history.md).

- **Decision — Cash-equity dividend precedence and quote exposure.** Adopt nonempty dividend-list precedence and the zero-horizon forward-metric fallback. Reconcile contractual dividend-cashflow expectations and the meaning of delta for embedded quotes. Record: [02](valuations-equity-and-composition/02-spot-and-carry.md).

- **Decision — Volatility-index risk and listed-option presets.** Agree index-point risk and lifecycle behavior, and obtain independent exchange specifications for production contract presets. Example premiums and volatilities are not calibrated inputs. Record: [05](valuations-equity-and-composition/05-vol-index-and-listed-options.md).

- **Decision — Vanilla option selection and adoption scope.** Agree the default model/exercise mapping, supported input/lifecycle domains, dividend approximations and accuracy targets for equity and FX options; registry availability does not establish method adoption. Assess registered alternatives within their own restrictions. Resolve Heston/rough parameter provenance and market-fit requirements separately from selection, and requalify independent exercise-date/tree-convergence and vendor-fixture evidence. The specific lifecycle and numerical-budget work remains in the related entries above and the [FX scope](#fx-commodities-and-exotics). Record: [0027](valuations-equity-and-composition/0027-vanilla-option-model-selection.md); dispatch context: [01](valuations-equity-and-composition/01-contract-dispatch.md).

## FX, commodities and exotics

[Scope inventory](valuations-fx-and-exotics/README.md).

- **Observed — FX linear price and risk semantics.** SpotRate equals PV/base notional and InverseRate its reciprocal, so optional discounting changes them from market spot/inverse and settlement can make them zero/error. Adopt semantic names or reconcile calculations. Decide explicitly whether the spot instrument represents a quote-currency receipt and whether default settlement should remain valuation-date-relative. Preserve the distinction between scenario-dependent at-market templates with omitted contract rates and the risk of fixed booked trades. For listed contracts, keep contractual point delta distinct from model spot delta and qualify coordinated family behavior when the shared listed kernel changes. Records: [FX-DELIVERY-CIP](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-delivery-cip.md), [FX-LISTED-CONTRACTS](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-listed-contracts.md).

- **Evidence — NDF projection horizon and inverse payoff.** Projected FX uses settlement maturity rather than fixing date, and inverse quotes invert that projected rate in payoff. Obtain fixing-measure/horizon benchmarks and adopt quote/settlement conventions, including projection on the fixing date, observed-fixing input before its scheduled date and missing-strike templates. Records: [NDF-FIXING-SETTLEMENT](valuations-fx-and-exotics/ndf-fixing-settlement.md).

- **Investigate — Asian drift differs between analytic branches.** Positive-effective-strike Turnbull–Wakeman uses terminal-implied flat drift; geometric/GBM and negative-effective-strike linear paths use every fixing-date DF. Benchmark moments under nonflat curves and adopt the approximation domain. Validate estimator bias and error treatment for the arithmetic GBM control variate whose coefficient uses the pricing sample and whose paired values come from two seeded runs. Records: [ASSET-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-asian.md).

- **Observed — Range-accrual digital width and reference spot are unit-sensitive.** Static digital width is absolute 0.0025 despite a 25bp comment, without commodity/equity price scaling; clipped probabilities can hide smile effects. RelativeToInitialSpot uses current valuation spot, moving barriers with shocks. Define unit scaling, stored reference and arbitrage/error policy. Records: [ASSET-RANGE-ACCRUAL](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-range-accrual.md).

- **Observed — Commodity delivery marks and delta differ from contract state.** Post-delivery/pre-payment forward value uses current spot without required delivery fixing. Analytic coefficient Delta remains nonzero when omitted strike makes at-market PV identically zero. Invoice-only cash export needs asset delivery for economic reconciliation. Adopt fixing, risk and reporting contracts. Records: [COMMODITY-DELIVERY-LISTED](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-delivery-listed.md).

- **Decision — Monitoring history and terminal claim ownership vary by product.** Asset continuous barrier/lookback permits absent monitoring start/history as unseasoned; FX barrier/touch requires explicit seasoned state. Asset Asian/barrier/lookback retains post-expiry realized claims, while FX options/commodity Asian return zero; rate notes drop final-payment-day claims while FX retains maturity cash. Adopt explicit lifecycle policies, including monitoring inclusion, retained post-expiry claims, paid-at-hit versus unpaid-at-expiry rebates and rebate units. For lookbacks, resolve the absent continuous history-start/extremum contract and residual-claim treatment. Records: [ASSET-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-asian.md), [ASSET-BARRIER](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-barrier.md), [ASSET-LOOKBACK](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-lookback.md), [FX-BARRIERS](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-barriers.md), [FX-TOUCH](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-touch.md), [COMMODITY-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-asian.md), [TARN-TARGET](valuations-fx-and-exotics/tarn-target.md).

- **Evidence — Schwartz–Smith defaults and selected-model risks.** Registered coefficients kappa 1, sigma_x 0.30, sigma_y 0.15, rho 0.30 and zero drifts/risk premium are drift-pinned to expiry forward, not locally calibrated. Vega/Vanna/Volga are rejected. Establish calibration/domain evidence and sigma-risk meaning; decide numerical risk support for early-exercise options. Add dedicated acceptance cases for near-expiry carry fallback and physical-settlement exposure, keeping single-expiry forward pinning distinct from joint forward-curve calibration. Records: [COMMODITY-SPOT-OPTION](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-spot-option.md).

- **Evidence — Callable range state and issuer stopping.** LSMC regresses short rate without in-range count despite path-dependent accrual; default is in-sample/single-curve HW. Dropping elapsed calls does not establish past call/redemption state. Qualify augmented-state, seasoning and independent exercise benchmarks; residual coupon subtraction alone does not establish Markov sufficiency. Adopt the single-curve and in-sample defaults explicitly. Validate payment-date/exercise ordering, relative-rate bounds and missing-state fallback paths against independent notes. Records: [CALLABLE-RANGE-HW](valuations-fx-and-exotics/callable-range-hw.md).

- **Decision — Snowball recursion and supported state.** Gearing applies only to inverse floater; recursive snowball is previous+fixed-floating. Both MC note types reject seasoned input, callable snowballs error despite representability, and strict pre-fitted scalar HW inputs are not calibrated from optional surfaces. Adopt payoff/domain and surface-risk expectations, including deterministic additive basis and the cap/floor convexity approximation in inverse-floater discounting. For TARN, agree accrual-fraction target units, exact coupon truncation and first-coupon zero-target redemption; qualify target-boundary, event-timing and stochastic-discounting accuracy beyond the existing non-knockout internal parity case. Records: [SNOWBALL-HW](valuations-fx-and-exotics/snowball-hw.md), [TARN-TARGET](valuations-fx-and-exotics/tarn-target.md).

- **Evidence — FX variance discrete forecast.** Forecast combines smile cumulative variation with carry-squared/partial-return terms as an interval squared-log approximation. OHLC supports fully observed settlement only and failed replication errors without ATM fallback. Qualify wings, calendar/discrete sampling, partial-return treatment, boundary carry terms, annualization and estimator policy; agree the lognormal interval mean/second-moment approximation separately from static replicated variation. Records: [FX-VARIANCE](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-variance.md).

- **Decision — Commodity averaging and same-day fixings.** Average swaps use midpoint fallback when no business observation exists, include final endpoint, apply calendar-day lag and equal quantity per stub. Asians require same-day fixing while swaps/futures project it. Adopt exchange/contract examples and state rules, including raw full-history cash schedules requiring fixings for already-paid periods while PV filters paid periods first. Records: [COMMODITY-AVERAGE-SWAP](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-average-swap.md), [COMMODITY-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-asian.md), [COMMODITY-DELIVERY-LISTED](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-delivery-listed.md).

- **Evidence — Constant-effective-rate versus datewise-drift accuracy.** FX barriers and asset analytic/PDE methods use terminal-effective rates while asset MC uses datewise drift. Qualify the fixed 200x100 PDE grid, domain truncation, Rannacher/parity tolerances, discrete observation projections and nonflat-curve behavior independently of seeded or same-kernel comparisons. Include near-barrier and sparse-discrete-monitoring cases plus continuous-extremum sampling convergence beyond lookback monotonicity tests. Records: [FX-BARRIERS](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-barriers.md), [ASSET-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-asian.md), [ASSET-BARRIER](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-barrier.md), [ASSET-LOOKBACK](valuations-fx-and-exotics/asset-lookback.md).

- **Decision — FX vanilla quotation and degenerate Greeks.** Agree the quotation/hedging delta among spot, forward and premium-adjusted variants; the exposed four measures do not select a desk convention. Adopt zero-strike, zero-volatility and exact-ATM Greek branches, and the fixed-surface smile movement under spot risk. Resolve the supported domain, alternatives and tolerances before accepting the draft. Record: [FX-VANILLA-DELTA](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-vanilla-delta.md).

- **Decision — FX digital settlement and Greek boundaries.** Confirm strict expiry equality and zero-volatility distribution-limit semantics for cash and asset digitals. Qualify boundary derivatives independently of a finite bump through a payoff jump; distinguish frozen-volatility Greeks from unimplemented smile-density/local-volatility alternatives. Record: [FX-DIGITAL](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-digital.md).

- **Evidence — FX touch negative-rate and hit-state domain.** Agree the distinction between a settled recorded hit and a newly hit payout, plus rejection when the at-hit formula has negative lambda-squared. Establish an independent hitting-time benchmark across the supported negative-rate domain and qualify the one-sided zero-volatility risk convention. Record: [FX-TOUCH](valuations-fx-and-exotics/fx-touch.md).

- **Decision — Quanto payoff scale and correlation convention.** Agree the fallback payoff scale `notional/strike` and the equity–FX correlation sign/quote convention. Establish the intended calibration/domain and tolerance basis for frozen correlation and one ATM-forward FX volatility; current scaling/regression evidence does not qualify a calibrated joint-market model. Record: [QUANTO-DRIFT](valuations-fx-and-exotics/quanto-drift.md).

- **Evidence — Commodity Asian moment matching and fallbacks.** Agree arithmetic moment-matching tolerances for skew, long averaging windows and seasoned effective strikes under the shared-forward covariance assumption. Expand domain cases for ill-conditioned moments and deterministic fallbacks; keep exact geometric pricing distinct from the arithmetic approximation. Record: [COMMODITY-ASIAN](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-asian.md).

- **Decision — Commodity swaption volatility and annuity.** Agree calibration of the scalar swap-rate volatility and whether equal quantity per period is the intended annuity contract. Qualify the positive-lognormal domain and rejection of invalid annuity after the helper arithmetic-mean fallback; normal/shifted alternatives are not locally implemented. Record: [COMMODITY-SWAPTION](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-swaption.md).

- **Evidence — Kirk correlation, smile risk and limit benchmark.** Agree the correlation source and smile movement when forward bumps also change each leg's sampled volatility strike. Qualify the denominator/positive-forward approximation domain and add an independent zero-strike exchange-option limit benchmark. Preserve the distinction between leg-one delta and simultaneous spread risk. Record: [COMMODITY-SPREAD](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-spread.md).

## Structured credit

[Scope inventory](valuations-structured-credit/README.md).

- **Observed — Ordinary stochastic dispatch discards richer dependence presets.** Scenario construction starts SingleFactor/kappa zero, copies scalar asset correlation and changes mean reversion only for intensity. Preset prepay/default -0.30/-0.20 does not select private TwoFactor support, and Matrix/Sectored dependence collapses to scalar. Adopt public model selection and qualify actual correlation; distinguish stochastic discounting from stochastic credit/prepayment in caller-facing provenance. Record: [15](valuations-structured-credit/15-stochastic-waterfall.md).

- **Investigate — OAS forward clock and stochastic settlement normalization.** monthly_forwards begins curve time at zero while paths begin at as-of. Deterministic DFs are settlement-relative, but OU factors integrate from as-of without division by stochastic settlement DF. Reproduce non-base/nonzero-settlement valuations and define coherent clocks before claiming general curve consistency. Agree separate OAS model provenance, zero-path promotion, numerical parameter domains, monthly integration error and rate/credit calibration. Record: [17](valuations-structured-credit/17-oas-scenarios.md).

- **Observed — Three-month excess spread means three payment periods.** min_excess_spread_3m averages payment periods, making quarterly and monthly deals use different horizons. Reconcile the field name and contractual horizon; resolve regime composition order, reversible step-down versus permanent early amortization, and scheduled/unscheduled principal classification. Validate combined-rule examples against dated legal waterfalls, not only isolated predicates. Record: [13](valuations-structured-credit/13-principal-regimes-and-amortization.md).

- **Decision — Floating rates and statistical collateral timing.** Observed index applies its floor plus spread without final zero floor; absent entire series uses stored all-in rate, absent required observation errors, and projection floors all-in at zero. Adopt historical/projected negative-rate and data-fallback contracts, half-period default interest, default-before-prepay ordering and irregular-period hazard aggregation. Qualify cached level payments and term resets for modifications and extended slices, heterogeneous borrower timing and lagged-recovery accuracy beyond the inspected limiting vectors. Record: [03](valuations-structured-credit/03-statistical-collateral-projection.md).

- **Observed — Coupon claims and accrued metadata use different loss balances.** Actual claims use balance after current realized-loss writedown; quote accrued retains pre-writedown opening balance. Adopt loss timing/quote convention and avoid describing both as one opening balance. Records: [11](valuations-structured-credit/11-waterfall-claims-and-rounding.md), [20](valuations-structured-credit/20-quotes-and-inverse-pricing.md).

- **Decision — Loss policy, calls and ordinary terminal recovery.** Calls immediately add pending recoveries to proceeds. Ordinary completion pays at adjusted max(default+full lag,last payment), potentially after legal final, and writes unpaid face down at final recovery settlement. Adopt family loss-policy defaults, call value/period timing, cleanup solvency, absolute 100-unit stop, residual identification and legal entitlement to those recoveries. Qualify structures with no explicit equity or unusual residual placement; booking tests alone do not establish the legal or economic appropriateness of either terminal policy. Record: [14](valuations-structured-credit/14-loss-recognition-and-terminal-cash.md).

- **Evidence — Assumed collateral and preset economics.** Missing CMO collateral creates WAC 4.5%, WAM 360, servicing/guarantee 25bp and PSA 100%, with maturity valuation+WAM. Define allowed generic, family and assumed-collateral uses and disclosure, including missing-calendar behavior and deterministic/stochastic entry-point provenance. Establish empirical provenance, sample dates and intended collateral populations for every embedded constructor, stochastic and fee profile; source coverage alone does not approve these material JSON dependencies. Records: [01](valuations-structured-credit/01-dispatch-and-assumptions.md), [18](valuations-structured-credit/18-agency-cmo-and-pac.md).

- **Decision — Borrowing-base dimensions, cap composition and fees.** Missing metadata avoids dimension caps; overlapping caps apply sequentially against a pre-exclusion denominator. Commitment fees are appended lender receipts rather than collateral-waterfall claims. Adopt metadata eligibility, concentration order/denominator, fee payer/funding, readvance policy, term-out liquidation and IRR investment basis. Qualify an independent borrowing-base/legal facility reference beyond the inspected event-unit tests. Record: [19](valuations-structured-credit/19-borrowing-base-facility.md).

- **Decision — Structured-credit risk units and actual perturbations.** Fixed-flow modified duration differs from full-reprojection effective risk; bucket CS01 time-allocates rather than independently bumps curves. Recovery01 is per percentage point versus default/prepay bp. Two-sided risk uses achieved widths, vector default/prepay uses maximum width and constant upward bumps can fail at bounds. Adopt names, signs, units, vector normalization and boundary experiments. Verify breakeven monotonicity with calls/reinvestment/coverage discontinuities and whether asset overrides should survive global scenario shocks; obtain convergence and independent economic risk references. Records: [20](valuations-structured-credit/20-quotes-and-inverse-pricing.md), [21](valuations-structured-credit/21-risk-bumps-and-scenarios.md).

- **Decision — Quote/inverse units and post-solve limits.** YTM follows note frequency/day count (deal default annual ACT/365F) with compounding extension. Standalone z-spread is bp versus registry decimal; DM decimal versus summary bp. DM is fixed-flow z-spread, not coupon reset each trial, and registry plausibility limits apply after root solving. Adopt output units, names, solver bounds, clean-price accrual around payment delay and multiple-IRR handling. Qualify future-draw sign/accounting treatment in each helper and independent quote/yield references beyond self-consistent price/spread roundtrips. Record: [20](valuations-structured-credit/20-quotes-and-inverse-pricing.md).

- **Decision — Collateral beyond final and dependency/history closure.** Instrument cashflows beyond deal final enter the last payment bucket; choose sale/termination versus retained entitlement. Agree history replay, hazard-versus-deal-model precedence, default-draw liquidity priority and unfunded-path meaning. Confirm nested-instrument market-dependency closure and qualify an independent joint contract/default/funding benchmark. Review historical scheduled-draw replay against the future-only payment schedule as a targeted question, not an established defect. Records: [08](valuations-structured-credit/08-instrument-collateral.md), [10](valuations-structured-credit/10-reinvestment-reserves-and-hedges.md).

- **Evidence — Stochastic clamps and conditional diagnostics.** Adopt and qualify 50%-monthly rate clamps, stationary zero-kappa horizon-persistent default factors, WAL conditional on repaid paths and discrete 95% ES rounding. Obtain independent nonlinear-tail accuracy, convergence and empirical preset-calibration evidence; limiting vectors and RNG/reproducibility checks do not establish that scope. Record: [15](valuations-structured-credit/15-stochastic-waterfall.md).

- **Decision — Pool normalization and reported statistics.** Agree representative-line accuracy, missing-rating and zero-statistic policies, original-balance reconstruction and inverse-HHI terminology. Validate heterogeneous aggregation against loan-level reference pools and establish agency parameter licensing, provenance and fit beyond the inspected hand-formula WARF/WAS checks. Record: [02](valuations-structured-credit/02-pool-normalization-and-statistics.md).

- **Decision — Delinquency and advancing conventions.** Agree default-input reinterpretation, timing-curve inversion, modification aggregation, arrears forgiveness and advance seniority. Obtain general transition calibration and independent mixed cure/modification and servicing references beyond the 100%-roll/no-cure hand schedule. Record: [04](valuations-structured-credit/04-delinquency-and-advancing.md).

- **Decision — NPL resolution and reperformance.** Agree resolution-date anchoring, whether reperformance starts next period, carrying-cost conventions and expectation-before-waterfall bias. Establish independent workout datasets and parameter provenance. Record: [05](valuations-structured-credit/05-npl-resolution.md).

- **Decision — CMBS servicing and debt-service coverage.** Agree expectation-before-waterfall extension/workout, annual-grid yield maintenance, appraisal reductions and partial-NOI DSCR. Establish external deal/vendor accuracy evidence beyond the located extension, replacement-rate, premium, discounting and fee tests. Record: [06](valuations-structured-credit/06-cmbs-servicing.md).

- **Decision — Card master-trust allocation and metric meaning.** Agree revolving/frozen allocation, seller funding assumptions and differing annual-input/cumulative-ratio metric labels. Obtain independent master-trust cashflow references and qualify correlated payment/default behavior. Record: [07](valuations-structured-credit/07-card-master-trust.md).

- **Decision — Collateral exercise policies.** Agree call-before-put priority, minimum-successful-XIRR selection, candidate-solve exclusion and refinance/step-up thresholds. Establish adversarial negative/multiple-IRR cashflow and simultaneous call/put references before adoption as a pricing exercise model. Record: [09](valuations-structured-credit/09-collateral-exercise.md).

- **Decision — Reinvestment, reserves and hedge cash accounts.** Agree purchase-yield eligibility, unrated new-cohort metadata, recovery exclusions, account priority and final reserve release. Qualify assumed reinvestment prices and hedge economics independently of cash-conservation checks; historical scheduled-draw replay is tracked in the dependency/history item above. Record: [10](valuations-structured-credit/10-reinvestment-reserves-and-hedges.md).

- **Decision — Waterfall allocation, fees and rounding.** Agree template fee/interest/principal ordering, principal support, PIK and AFC treatment, zero-weight fallback and silent omission of invalid standard fees. Qualify independent monetary rounding and incentive-IRR fixtures, including multiple-sign-change equity histories and legal-document references beyond hand-built golden waterfalls. Record: [11](valuations-structured-credit/11-waterfall-claims-and-rounding.md).

- **Decision — Coverage numerators and cures.** Agree collateral numerator membership, CCC/discount interaction, missing-rating treatment, IC fallback, placement/hysteresis and maximum-versus-summed cure. Require contract-specific reference waterfalls and tests for new instrument/reinvestment metadata so omitted ratings cannot silently improve coverage. Establish stress calibration for expected collateral valuation and forward coupon estimates. Record: [12](valuations-structured-credit/12-coverage-and-cures.md).

- **Decision — Revolver utilization and option attribution.** Agree clipped utilization, volatility-freeze boundary, shared-factor sign/loading, relative fair-spread anchoring and actual-minus-counterfactual sign. Qualify draw-option decomposition under nonlinear trigger/call changes beyond zero-option-cost and allocation tests. Record: [16](valuations-structured-credit/16-revolver-utilization-and-option-cost.md).

- **Decision — PAC targets and CMO cash conventions.** Agree endpoint-only PAC construction, persistent dates, broken-PAC principal sweep, accrued/price basis and accrual funding. Obtain external deal/PAC benchmark provenance and qualify intermediate/path-varying speed protection. Assumed-collateral use and disclosure are tracked in the preset-economics item above. Record: [18](valuations-structured-credit/18-agency-cmo-and-pac.md).

- **Decision — Reporting and economic metric interpretation.** Agree paid/noncash classification, draw-sensitive WAL fallback, current-capital equity-return basis, zero/null policies, ratio denominators and stochastic credit-duration naming. Qualify economic metric interpretations independently of diagnostic and hand-formula accounting identities; stochastic WAL conditioning and ES rounding are tracked in the stochastic-diagnostics item above. Record: [22](valuations-structured-credit/22-diagnostics-and-reporting.md).

## Calibration: orchestration, solvers and replay

[Scope inventory](calibration-framework/README.md).

- **Observed — Plan residual ratios reuse a physical-magnitude penalty filter.** Plan summaries form absolute residual/tolerance ratios, then omit values at or above 5e5 when a smaller valid value exists. Ratios 1e6 and 0.1 can therefore report a maximum of 0.1, although step failure still makes overall success false. This is a source-derived example, not an executed regression. Define explicit penalty classification and qualify extreme-ratio reporting; failure text also always cites excess residual even for validation or clamp failure. Include mixed-unit, extreme-ratio and invalid-row host-serialization cases; document the distinct objective and iteration/evaluation-counter meanings. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-014](calibration-framework/14-fit-reports.md).

- **Decision — Pooled fit statistics mix native units and penalty populations.** Raw plan maxima/RMS combine PV-per-notional, volatility and other residual units; observation counts can include penalty rows excluded from step RMSE. Agree unit-aware or normalized reporting and the population represented by each statistic before interpreting pooled errors as comparable fit quality. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-014](calibration-framework/14-fit-reports.md).

- **Investigate — Parameter clamping and analytical Jacobians have different derivatives.** Residual evaluation clamps parameters, while the analytical path evaluates the target derivative at the clamped point without the clamp derivative; numerical derivatives see the clamped closure. Constructor failures can create flat penalties and clamping can suppress target bound penalties, although final bound violation forces failure. Qualify bounded paths and reproduce the numerical effect near or outside bounds. Establish the intended boundary behavior and qualify in-domain pricing-failure recovery as well as near-bound solutions. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-012](calibration-framework/12-weighted-global-solve.md).

- **Observed — Failed sensitivity perturbations can look like zero risk.** Bootstrap returns zero when both bumps fail. Global diagnostics omit the entire Jacobian and return all-zero quote sensitivities when any up bump fails, even if its down bump succeeds. Successful nonfinite bumps can publish a Jacobian without a condition estimate. Define unavailable versus zero results and symmetric bound-aware fallback; the condition estimate is the normal-equation eigenvalue ratio, not the Jacobian condition. Agree diagnostic units and add explicit boundary and nonfinite-bump cases. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-015](calibration-framework/15-sensitivity-diagnostics.md).

- **Observed — Hull–White adapter labels differ from dated runtime selection.** Schema/model prose says Jamshidian, but the envelope always supplies schedules and selects contractual Gaussian integration. Piecewise prose says expiry knots, while the implementation uses live-fixing support. Reconcile the adapter descriptions with the model-owned algorithm records. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-018](calibration-framework/18-hull-white-market-adapter.md).

- **Observed — Hull–White adapter accepts controls it does not apply.** Piecewise initial guesses are parsed but ignored, and global plan solver/diagnostics controls are not forwarded into the HW helpers. Define the effective control surface and reconcile the exposed settings rather than implying every accepted control changes the fit. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-018](calibration-framework/18-hull-white-market-adapter.md).

- **Decision — Hull–White adapter has explicit coupon and date conversions.** The first cap coupon is unconditionally omitted, overnight cap indices are rejected, and persisted sigma times become base plus round(365t) calendar days. Agree these supported-contract and persistence conventions and qualify boundary effects without duplicating the underlying rate-model choices. Include term-specific first-coupon conventions, sigma persistence at noninteger-day support, and independent schedule/price fixtures distinguishing contractual integration from the synthetic direct-calibrator path. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-018](calibration-framework/18-hull-white-market-adapter.md).

- **Observed — Tranche materialization uses caller-specific date defaults.** Initial spot and None-roll maturity use registry calendar/BDC before schedule overrides. Direct build overrides default to CdsImm, while envelope parameters default to RollRule::None. Define which date roles overrides own and whether the caller-specific split is intended; holiday-boundary price effects were not executed. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-008](calibration-framework/08-tranche-materialization.md).

- **Observed — Residual-key uniqueness differs between solvers.** Sequential bootstrap collects a map without the global solver’s duplicate-key guard, so a custom target collision can overwrite a row. Public envelope quote-ID validation limits reachability. Define whether the shared target contract must enforce uniqueness in bootstrap and add a custom-target collision case. Cover generic target and replay callers and independently test final-interpolation changes to earlier fits before claiming complete observation coverage. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-010](calibration-framework/10-sequential-bootstrap.md).

- **Decision — Rate replay preserves shape using fresh calibration defaults.** Replay uses default controls and source-knot discount DF-ratio or forward additive overlays rather than losslessly restoring the original fit policy. Intermediate fits are gated, but final overlay/resampled linked-OIS curves receive no new complete quote-fit report. Agree whether exact bumped quote repricing or source-shape preservation takes precedence. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-019](calibration-framework/19-rate-quote-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-020](calibration-framework/20-linked-ois-replay.md).

- **Investigate — Rate replay mixes approximate pillar selection and rounded clocks.** Shock selection uses base plus raw tenor or futures expiry rather than builder spot/adjusted/payment/reference-end pillars. Linked projection uses rounded dates in discount factors and the supplied time interval in its denominator. Qualify grids, interpolation, clocks, negative-rate policy and off-knot identity with independent replay fixtures. Reconcile futures reference-period-end pillars with expiry-based bump selection; include coupon intervals, tails, short maturities and non-Actual clock inversion. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-019](calibration-framework/19-rate-quote-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-020](calibration-framework/20-linked-ois-replay.md).

- **Decision — Hazard replay has separate quote-risk and finite identity contracts.** It preserves stored policy and checks zero-shock knot identity; recovery/spread actions use par-risk quotes and recovery leaves par spreads unchanged. Tenor shocks affect every pillar within 0.1 years, deal overrides require exact pillar matching, and horizon replay validates rolled conditional-survival identity only at knots/midpoints with 1e-8 absolute tolerance. Adopt these conventions and obtain independent CDS/horizon evidence. Distinguish quote-space from hazard-space sensitivity; qualify independent CDS price/CS01, off-knot survival, coupon dates/tails and roll/accrual discontinuities at standard CDS dates. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-021](calibration-framework/21-hazard-quote-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-022](calibration-framework/22-hazard-deal-and-horizon.md).

- **Investigate — Scalar search success does not establish a local root certificate.** The custom scan/refinement/minimization solver is not Brent and has phase-specific budgets and validity filters. Some final sign-change checks omit the scan magnitude filter and can certify a best near-zero point using a sign change elsewhere. Qualify discontinuities, penalties, flat regions and multiple roots; numerical false acceptance was not reproduced. Decide tangent near-zero acceptance, disconnected feasible domains and strict work budgets, then use independent adversarial functions to qualify the exact certification branches. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-011](calibration-framework/11-scalar-root-search.md).

- **Decision — Multistart minimization and fit acceptance optimize different criteria.** Restart selection minimizes weighted L2, while acceptance uses maximum unweighted residual and final validation. A lower weighted-error restart can displace a candidate that satisfied the acceptance gate. Agree candidate-selection policy and qualify near-bound, hard-fit and multimodal cases independently. Set the supported dimensionality and quantify restart scale/count sensitivity on independent difficult surfaces. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-012](calibration-framework/12-weighted-global-solve.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-013](calibration-framework/13-deterministic-multi-start.md).

- **Decision — Finite validation grids do not certify continuous arbitrage absence.** Discount checks add knots/midpoints; other curves use fixed grids and may extrapolate. Surface calendar/convexity checks are finite-node tests, some nonfinite protection relies on builders, and invalid discount pairs are skipped by the arbitrage loop. Define the supported guarantee and independently qualify interpolation/extrapolation between sampled points. Agree the intended role of each heuristic, price versus total-variance tolerances and coverage; include dense-grid pricing, interior oscillation, extrapolation and finite-value failure examples. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-016](calibration-framework/16-curve-validation.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-017](calibration-framework/17-surface-validation.md).

- **Decision — Search bounds and final validation domains differ.** Search permits negative rates while default final policy forbids them; the default hazard search maximum is 10/year versus the validator’s 0.5/year. Agree the intended search versus acceptance domains and distinguish these framework policies from target-specific construction limits. Pin search versus final acceptance policy for negative/high-rate and distressed-credit regimes before attributing failures solely to the optimizer. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-016](calibration-framework/16-curve-validation.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-017](calibration-framework/17-surface-validation.md).

- **Decision — Staged loading and dependency reports require caller acknowledgment.** Strict request loading returns semantic errors in a report rather than rejecting the envelope. Result loading checks nested market shape without recomputing fit, quote-side metadata labels rather than transforms quotes, and graph IDs are checked before runtime typed prerequisites. Define caller obligations and the meaning of loaded versus executable or validated artifacts. Explicitly resolve staged API and default-convention adoption, ID/replacement policy and dry-run versus numeric-preflight documentation, and add independent wire-to-financial examples. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-001](calibration-framework/01-wire-and-authoring.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-002](calibration-framework/02-controls-and-market-context.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-003](calibration-framework/03-dependencies-and-execution.md).

- **Observed — Extreme freshness ages can change comparison meaning.** Freshness uses caller-supplied timestamp metadata and casts unsigned age seconds to signed i64. Extreme ages can invert the intended comparison. Constrain the arithmetic/domain and reproduce boundary cases; no executed runtime failure or external timestamp verification is claimed. Decide the permitted age range and whole-second truncation semantics. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-002](calibration-framework/02-controls-and-market-context.md).

- **Decision — Recalibration cache correctness depends on identity and lifetime.** Successful values persist without eviction, failures retry, and convention-registry versions are not explicit key inputs. Hazard caching assumes the source hazard/recipe and discount dependency capture pricing inputs. Agree registry pinning, provider lifetime and growth policy, and qualify concurrency/dependency completeness beyond selected key-change tests. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-023](calibration-framework/23-recalibration-cache.md).

- **Evidence — Quote construction and replay need independent dated market fixtures.** Builder records document registry-defined date, pillar and quote-unit conversions, but inspected convention JSONs and version labels do not certify market-standard compliance. Same-kernel replay, direct-fit and quote-repricing checks establish consistency. Obtain independent instrument/convention fixtures and solver stress evidence scoped to the adopted methods. Pin convention-data versions and retain deposit/FRA settlement/reset-holiday examples; listed-futures sizing, reference-period calendars and final settlement; swap holiday/EOM/stub/payment-delay/lookback/shift/cutoff/spread and override-domain cases, including pillar effects on interpolation and coupled fits; CDS standard-date boundaries, negative upfront, accrual rebates, step-in and cash settlement; an external tranche term sheet; and XCCY quote orientation, exchange type and joint-calendar examples. Reconcile XCCY accrual-end support with final lagged cashflows and final discount-curve validation. Records: [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-004](calibration-framework/04-deposit-fra-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-005](calibration-framework/05-futures-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-006](calibration-framework/06-swap-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-007](calibration-framework/07-cds-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-008](calibration-framework/08-tranche-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-009](calibration-framework/09-xccy-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-011](calibration-framework/11-scalar-root-search.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-012](calibration-framework/12-weighted-global-solve.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-013](calibration-framework/13-deterministic-multi-start.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-019](calibration-framework/19-rate-quote-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-020](calibration-framework/20-linked-ois-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-021](calibration-framework/21-hazard-quote-replay.md), [CALIBRATION-FRAMEWORK-022](calibration-framework/22-hazard-deal-and-horizon.md).

## Calibration: quotes and curve/smile targets

[Scope inventory](calibration-targets/README.md).

- **Observed — Discount pricing aliases differ between trial and final curves.** Solver curves use pricing_discount_id while final curves use output curve_id; residual insertion uses the actual curve ID and prepared instruments retain the pricing-role ID. A distinct-ID path can read stale trial state during final repricing or overwrite the external pricing dependency. The default equal-ID path avoids this.
