Define alias semantics and add a distinct-ID returned-artifact repricing case before asserting numerical impact. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-003](calibration-targets/03-discount-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Configured hazard maxima are not forwarded to curve construction.** The target accepts hazard_hard_max values above 10/year, but never forwards them to the core builder, whose cap remains 10/year. Trials can therefore fail despite satisfying target controls. Reconcile cap propagation and add custom-bound construction/fit cases, separately from the framework’s post-fit validation policy. Qualify distressed and near-zero-LGD fits, irregular/IMM dates, objective smoothness and upfront/par conversion against independent data. Decide matured-input policy while preserving the distinction between original quote recovery and calibrated curve recovery. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-005](calibration-targets/05-hazard-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Inflation seed bounds can form an inverted clamp interval.** The target forms lo=max(C0×0.9^t,hard_min) and hi=min(C0×1.5^t,hard_max) without checking their order before clamping. Public preflight accepts positive finite C0 outside those bounds: C0=20000 and t=1 give lo=18000 above default hi=10000. This is an unexecuted, panic-capable source path. Define seed/domain errors for extreme CPI units and rates; a finite rate below -1 can also invalidate a fractional-power seed. Qualify actual reference-month/publication fixtures, mixed ZC/YoY identification, nonlinear interpolation and seasonal observed-versus-projected continuity. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-006](calibration-targets/06-inflation-calibration.md).

- **Observed — XCCY trial and final curve validation differ.** Trials accept nonmonotone raw discount factors without a floor, while final construction uses default DiscountCurve validation and lacks the discount target’s currency policy. A negative-rate candidate can fit and then fail final construction. Align trial/final negative-rate policy and qualify final-artifact admission, preserving the distinction from framework validation. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-007](calibration-targets/07-xccy-calibration.md).

- **Decision — XCCY calibration has separate input, shape and result conventions.** Rates-only, XCCY-only and mixed quotes are accepted despite obsolete rate-anchor commentary. The method uses a fixed 1.5 DF cap, lacks discount-target nonlocal interpolation polish and attaches no rate replay sidecar. Its derived spread is continuous foreign-minus-domestic zero spread, not quoted coupon basis. Agree supported outputs, interpolation/fit accuracy and replay requirements. Validate pair-specific spot/basis orientation and date conventions against independent benchmark curves. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-007](calibration-targets/07-xccy-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Swaption SABR forwards do not apply all resolved observation conventions.** Resolved float compounding and reset-lag data survive preparation, but projection uses accrual-endpoint DF-implied growth without lookback/shift/cutoff/reset fixings. The HW adapter separately rejects nonzero observation transforms. Define supported compounding/reset conventions and quantify the SABR contract difference before treating every resolved field as applied. Add independent broker ATM/par-forward fixtures with payment lag and nontrivial overnight observation conventions. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-013](calibration-targets/13-swaption-forwards.md).

- **Decision — Quoted and interpolated swaption nodes use different forward schedules.** Quoted nodes retain exact quote-specific contractual dates, while interpolated cube nodes use index defaults and rounded-month dates. Adopt the intended synthetic forward/schedule reconstruction and qualify node/interpolation consistency and cross-calendar parity independently. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-013](calibration-targets/13-swaption-forwards.md).

- **Decision — Residual weights do not specify measurement accuracy.** Discount/forward use PV per notional weighted by an inverse-squared positive-rate annuity proxy and a time scheme, defaulting to SqrtTime. Forward deposits instead use undiscounted accrual-growth error; hazard/inflation omit annuity weighting and parametric fits default to equal weights. Acceptance uses native unweighted maximum residual. Agree economic units and weighting across rate signs, maturities, day counts and cash/notional rounding, including the `1e-3` floor, cross-tenor comparisons, near-duplicate/matured inputs and nonfinite controls; reproduce sensitivity to those choices. For the forward target, qualify control placement/collisions for overlapping FRA/future/swap sets, finite-difference conditioning, holiday/stub grids, negative-rate accrual-factor domains, deposit versus discounted residual weights and off-benchmark interpolation. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-002](calibration-targets/02-rate-preparation-and-residuals.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-003](calibration-targets/03-discount-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-004](calibration-targets/04-forward-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-005](calibration-targets/05-hazard-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-006](calibration-targets/06-inflation-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-008](calibration-targets/08-parametric-calibration.md).

- **Observed — Discount method labels do not identify the full numerical path.** Nonlocal Bootstrap performs global polish, and the analytical-Jacobian flag selects target central finite differences with local-causality skips. Parameter clipping and seed adoption also affect the method. Reconcile naming and qualify bounds, scan resolution and Jacobian step convergence/boundaries without an analytic-adjoint or test-pass claim. Under ADR 0032, agree direct-construction versus calibration defaults, negative-rate/floor regimes, interpolation-dependent polishing, seed adoption, smoothing, extrapolation, fit budgets and bounds for each intended use. Trace host-builder defaults before promising equivalent omission behavior. Independently reconcile full cashflow dates and off-pillar zeros/forwards, including tails and smoothing. Alias, residual-scaling and control-grid tasks retain their separate owners above. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-003](calibration-targets/03-discount-calibration.md), [ADR 0032](calibration-targets/0032-curve-interpolation-and-calibration.md).

- **Decision — Equity carry selection and dividend units need an explicit contract.** Yield override then yield scalar take precedence over a unique cash schedule; absent carry errors. Cash dividends use ex-date PV and numeric amount extraction discards Money currency. SVI without discount requires yield and assumes a zero discount rate. Agree supported currencies, dividend currency responsibility, ex-date versus pay-date economics, yield/schedule precedence, no-discount SVI assumptions, clock conventions and near-zero prepaid-forward errors. Reconcile against independent dividend/forward-market data. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-009](calibration-targets/09-equity-forward-inputs.md).

- **Decision — Equity volatility surfaces have different tail policies.** Equity SABR and SVI materialize fixed-absolute-strike total variance using each source expiry’s forward. SABR defaults to out-of-range Error; SVI hardcodes flat-total-variance Clamp, implying volatility proportional to T^-1/2 outside the range. Adopt the fixed-strike interpolation domain, source ordering diagnostics, short-end limiting behavior and SVI tail-policy configurability. Qualify continuous/interpolated arbitrage independently of finite-grid fit checks, including parameter stability, independent smile data, finite-scan misses, target-grid repricing and cross-expiry crossings. The carry entry above owns the no-discount assumption. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-010](calibration-targets/10-total-variance-materialization.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-011](calibration-targets/11-equity-sabr.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-012](calibration-targets/12-equity-svi.md).

- **Decision — Swaption cube bucketing and interpolation are approximate choices.** Rounded 1e-4-year keys can merge nearby contractual coordinates and exact-key lookup can snap a nearby target. Interpolation uses log alpha/nu with a 1e-16 nu floor, linear clipped rho and inherited beta/shift; deterministic missing-corner substitution requires the lower-left corner. Agree these policies and independently validate market smiles/forwards, quoted-vol tolerances by convention and cross-node arbitrage. Qualify interpolation near zero nu, rho bounds, sparse grids, convention boundaries and rounded-key collisions; there is no global no-arbitrage projection. Delivered-cube quote rechecks and failed-bucket report failure are already implemented. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-014](calibration-targets/14-swaption-cube.md).

- **Decision — Base-correlation inversion needs identification and tolerance qualification.** The target enforces nondecreasing parameters, searches nearest-first, accepts approximate knots within a hardcoded 0.001 upfront fraction and fabricates a flat second knot for a single quote. Monotone correlation does not establish complete no-arbitrage or unique roots. Agree the tolerance, monotonicity, one-quote synthetic extent and bracketing policies for intended market use. Independently benchmark tranche prices/loss quadrature, root shape, attachment interpolation and constrained-fit residuals. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-015](calibration-targets/15-base-correlation.md).

- **Decision — Student-t calibration fits one parameter under a fixed correlation.** The target fits df with flat correlation held fixed, accepts the initial df within 0.001, maps pricing failures to infinity and uses curve base date as valuation date. Agree the quote, as-of, tolerance and initial-acceptance contract. Qualify parameter sensitivity, multiple roots and integration error against independent tranche benchmarks; preserve causal pricing error diagnostics. Confirm curve-base as-of and scalar output naming fit consuming risk/replay flows. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-016](calibration-targets/16-student-t.md).

- **Investigate — NS/NSS parameter repair and dense maturity grids need qualification.** The adapter uses all calendar dates to the last nondeposit pillar plus deposit endpoints. Tau clamping/separation repair is discontinuous, and fixed beta bounds and a tight PV budget can affect the fit. Qualify parameter identifiability, repaired-bound behavior, overdetermined fits, extrapolated shapes, numerical derivative accuracy, daily-grid coverage, workload and independent final-curve repricing. Decide whether fit tolerance should reflect market noise and whether returned curves require model/solver provenance. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-008](calibration-targets/08-parametric-calibration.md).

- **Decision — Rate and hazard recipes preserve different replay provenance.** Rate recipes omit original quote IDs, numerical settings and interpolation/extrapolation fields and generate replay IDs. Hazard recipes retain parameters/configuration, rebuilt pillar bindings and original plus par-risk quotes. Agree reproducibility, ID preservation and convention/version-lineage requirements across the two contracts. Reconcile policy omissions through the existing [framework](calibration-framework/README.md); test curve transforms, custom day counts, upfront par conversion and regenerated-ID diagnostics. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-017](calibration-targets/17-calibration-replay.md).

- **Decision — Quote admission and target domains need explicit boundaries.** General quote validation permits zero volatility, while SABR/SVI fitting requires positive observations. Define target-specific growth-rate and zero-vol domains and explicit admission errors rather than implying every schema-valid quote has a fit. Agree whether direct bump helpers reject invalid input immediately and standalone leaf validators enforce nonblank IDs. Obtain independent broker/source fixtures and authoritative convention-version mapping. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-001](calibration-targets/01-quote-contracts.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-011](calibration-targets/11-equity-sabr.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-012](calibration-targets/12-equity-svi.md).

- **Evidence — Calibration references do not yet establish independent market qualification.** The Bloomberg-named USD OIS fixture is attributed in source to 2025-12-10, but its provenance and a current run were not independently verified. Other inspected tests mainly use hand formulas, same-kernel quotes, round trips and invariants. Obtain independent dated curves, surfaces and credit benchmarks for the selected target formulations. For equity SABR, qualify sparse/extreme strikes, beta near the normal cutoff, tiny quoted vol, multistart reproducibility, displaced-domain boundaries, source calendar ordering and post-materialization smile accuracy. Under ADR 0032, review independent inputs, provenance and tolerances for linked Hagan–West formula, failed-fit and attributed Bloomberg OIS cases, then run the relevant scoped suites. Records: [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-003](calibration-targets/03-discount-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-005](calibration-targets/05-hazard-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-008](calibration-targets/08-parametric-calibration.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-011](calibration-targets/11-equity-sabr.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-012](calibration-targets/12-equity-svi.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-014](calibration-targets/14-swaption-cube.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-015](calibration-targets/15-base-correlation.md), [CALIBRATION-TARGETS-016](calibration-targets/16-student-t.md), [ADR 0032](calibration-targets/0032-curve-interpolation-and-calibration.md).

## Calibration: Hull–White and LMM

[Scope inventory](calibration-rate-models/README.md).

- **Observed — Scalar cap search documentation overstates optimality.** The fixed-κ helper claims monotone option prices imply a unique unimodal basket SSE, while its implementation searches three golden-section brackets and acknowledges local minima. Narrow that claim and qualify within-bracket/global search behavior before treating the returned point as a global least-squares optimum. Reproduce pathological multi-quote, low-σ and weak-vega baskets before asserting global optimality. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-007](calibration-rate-models/07-fixed-kappa-cap-fit.md).

- **Observed — HW control descriptions have inconsistent units.** The vega floor is numerically 1e-8 but described as one basis point of annuity-year; restart half-width 0.5 is additive in log parameters, implying natural factors about 0.61–1.65 rather than literal ±50%. Reconcile the source descriptions with the implemented controls. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Observed — Cap diagnostic prose differs from calculation.** Market vega is evaluated at each quote's actual strike, although metadata commentary calls it ATM. Moneyness excludes nonfinite or |F|<1e-12 forwards but includes negative forwards, contrary to commentary that nonpositive forwards are skipped. Clarify these diagnostics before using them to assess fit quality. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md), [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-007](calibration-rate-models/07-fixed-kappa-cap-fit.md).

- **Observed — HW construction is not a numerical arbitrage certificate.** Swaption source prose says model construction eliminates the need for output arbitrage checks; actual gates inspect individual prices, roots and residuals. Narrow the guarantee and distinguish mathematical-model assumptions from the computed surface and synthetic decomposition. No arbitrage failure was reproduced. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-004](calibration-rate-models/04-jamshidian-decomposition.md).

- **Observed — LMM calibration prose omits contractual accrual and loading history.** Private slice comments identify accruals with tenor differences and describe a live `i/N` loading index. The public helper actually receives contractual accruals and preserves original contract indices after trimming elapsed periods. Reconcile the descriptions with the pricing-owned structure. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md).

- **Evidence — LMM formulation citation points to a different book.** The module names a 2002 Rebonato book/chapter but links the reference catalogue's different 2004 title. Establish the exact primary attribution and relevant approximation assumptions rather than treating the existing anchor as verified provenance. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md).

- **Decision — Fixed and free κ select different cap objectives and domains.** Fixed-κ fits minimize raw premium SSE, while free-κ/σ fits divide premium errors by market vega. Fixed-κ σ starts at 1e-8 versus 1e-5 for LM; supplied κ endpoints are accepted while fitted parameters near either bound are rejected. Fixed-κ fitting still applies the vega admission gate before branching despite its unweighted objective. Agree these differences explicitly. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md), [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-007](calibration-rate-models/07-fixed-kappa-cap-fit.md).

- **Decision — Mixed swaption quote units need an economic weighting rule.** Normal and Black observations use absolute-rate and relative-volatility residuals under one numerical objective and tolerance. Agree how mixed conventions are weighted and which quote-space accuracy is intended; equal numerical volatility errors do not establish equal economic importance. Narrow source descriptions of a common implied-volatility scale so they distinguish Normal and Black units. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-003](calibration-rate-models/03-atm-swaption-quotes.md), [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Evidence — Quote count and parameter bounds do not establish identification.** The two-parameter gate accepts two observations including duplicates, without a separate information-rank acceptance rule. Bound pinning is not proof of weak economic identification. Qualify the identification policy with independent noisy-market baskets, restart sensitivity and parameter stability. Agree supported parameter boxes, restart coverage and boundary diagnostic interpretation as part of that policy. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Observed — Final HW reports retain optimizer-scale metadata.** Main residuals are replaced with exact reconstructed quote-vol errors, but inherited final_* optimizer statistics still describe vega-linearized residuals. Preserve that distinction in field interpretation and inspect consumers before treating mixed-scale metadata as a numerical defect. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Observed — Scalar and piecewise iteration counts are summary labels.** Fixed-κ cap reports set iterations to one; piecewise reports use pillar count rather than actual golden-section/Brent iterations or pricing evaluations. Define their reporting meaning before consuming them as convergence or workload measures. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-007](calibration-rate-models/07-fixed-kappa-cap-fit.md), [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-008](calibration-rate-models/08-piecewise-sigma-bootstrap.md).

- **Decision — Piecewise HW bootstrapping needs a stability and boundary policy.** The method freezes earlier segments, rejects duplicate maturities, aligns intervals to last live fixing and extrapolates the last σ flat without smoothing. Upper-bound solutions are rejected while lower-bound solutions are accepted; direct σ bounds may exceed the engine's bound of two. Agree these policies and qualify noisy or near-duplicate pillar sensitivity. Include bound sensitivity and quote-space conditioning, and decide regularization if attainable pillars imply abrupt σ changes. Reconcile source headings/enum prose that locates intervals at quote expiry/payment with live-fixing support, coordinating engine-schema wording with its owner. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-008](calibration-rate-models/08-piecewise-sigma-bootstrap.md).

- **Decision — LMM calibration is one explicit ATM scale fit.** The helper targets only the earliest future slice with frozen weights, a fixed shape and displacement, clamped surface lookup and a positive unshifted Black swap rate. It returns a scalar without a fit report or persistence. Agree target-slice and edge policies and preserve provenance without implying full-surface or Bermudan calibration. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md).

- **Investigate — Synthetic HW decomposition needs a signed-coupon envelope.** Negative-rate Normal quotes can produce negative fixed-bond coupons, but the synthetic kernel does not explicitly verify a unique exercise region. Newton convergence is step-only and individual/total prices are floored. Reproduce signed-coupon, root-residual and clamp cases against an independent payoff calculation before claiming exactness across that domain. Define permitted coupon-sign/negative-rate domains, a root-residual requirement, fallback bracket policy and permitted price clamping. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-004](calibration-rate-models/04-jamshidian-decomposition.md).

- **Investigate — Contractual Gaussian integration needs broader error qualification.** The kernel combines a tilted-tail bound of 1e-13 with panel quadrature budgets totaling 9e-13. Narrow exercise regions, signed-term cancellation, large-volatility horizons and notional/vega scaling still need independent checks; the one-coupon covariance comparison is a narrow special case. Include multiple exercise regions, long horizons and near-zero prices against independent high-precision integration; assess fixed absolute price error against admitted vega and fit tolerances, and document original reference generation separately from production-kernel agreement. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-005](calibration-rate-models/05-contractual-gaussian-integration.md).

- **Investigate — Cap quote inversion has conditioning and admission differences.** Normal strip inversion uses tolerance 1e-14 and a finite doubling search whose last upper bound can exceed 100. Scalar and piecewise methods apply different vega admission rules. Qualify low-vega/near-intrinsic, negative-forward and long-delay cases against independent premiums and agree the intended inverse domain. Include irregular periods, distinguish factor-curve calibration from contractual projection accuracy, and establish the provenance of the fixed numerical cap references. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-002](calibration-rate-models/02-cap-schedules-and-quote-inversion.md).

- **Evidence — LMM same-formula round trips do not qualify multi-forward pricing.** Frozen-formula round trips and the one-forward analytical premium case do not establish multi-forward Monte Carlo agreement. The small-path pricing test verifies override consumption only. Obtain independently converged multi-forward European comparisons and preserve calibration provenance before using the fitted scale as Bermudan accuracy evidence. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md).

- **Decision — Direct and contractual HW calibration domains require explicit adoption.** Agree the direct synthetic versus contractual schedule domains and how callers preserve fit provenance. Qualify irregular calendars and curve interpolation/extrapolation independently of model-time invariance, including delayed-payment par-strike conventions and near-Black-bound inverse behavior. An initial guess is a numerical seed, not evidence of parameter identification. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-001](calibration-rate-models/01-curve-entry-and-quote-domains.md), [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-003](calibration-rate-models/03-atm-swaption-quotes.md).

## Statements: capital structure

[Scope inventory](statements-capital/README.md).

- **Observed — Residual balance scaling is not clamped.** Source prose describes a [0,1.10] clamp, but the period-flow implementation applies the raw opening/scheduled ratio and warns when deviation exceeds 1e-6; negative opening is not explicitly rejected there. Reconcile the prose and agree rejection or repair separately from documenting the current ratio. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-003](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md).

- **Observed — PIK thresholds lack a finiteness gate.** Evaluated metrics are checked for finiteness, but the threshold is not. A directly constructed NaN threshold makes the strict comparison false, infinities select extreme behavior, and hysteresis can retain prior mode; JSON admissibility may differ. Define the typed/wire contract and reproduce boundary behavior without treating the already-validated metric as missing validation. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-008](statements-capital/08-pik-and-carry-state.md).

- **Observed — Reported period order follows insertion order.** The cashflow API describes ascending periods but returns IndexMap insertion order when totals are nonempty. Agree or reconcile chronological output guarantees and qualify callers that assume sorted periods. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

- **Observed — CashflowDate prose conflicts with the agreed period-close FX boundary.** The helper accepts a caller-supplied date, while the evaluator aggregates cashflow buckets and passes period.end.previous_day(); individual flow dates no longer survive that boundary. Preserve the agreed period-close capital/waterfall FX direction and reconcile flow-date wording; this is not an optional redesign. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

- **Observed — A sweep without targets is capacity-based across staged rows.** Field prose says all term loans, but runtime allocation is not filtered that way and supported prepayment targets can include revolvers. Reconcile the supported contract and avoid treating local LPA/S&P wording as universal legal compliance. Qualify mixed loan/revolver target allocation against the adopted contract. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-007](statements-capital/07-prepayments-and-ecf.md).

- **Investigate — New-funding closes mix economic and cash-period clocks.** A zero-opening/new-funding close combines economic-dated funding with cash-period PIK and repayments, whereas ordinary close uses economic outstanding. Reproduce newly issued or redrawn debt with early/late settlement and PIK dates to measure closing-balance and capacity differences. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-003](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md).

- **Investigate — Class allocation can direct floating remainder to an ineligible final row.** Class vectors contain zeros for other rows, but pro-rata remainder goes to the final array entry, potentially zero/ineligible, before available cash becomes zero. Independently bound or reproduce the residual across scales and row orders before concluding a material cash loss; ordinary conservation fixtures do not cover every case. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md).

- **Evidence — Residual coupons need independent debt-ledger reconciliation.** Qualify arbitrary cash/PIK shares, zeroed-coupon reactivation, irregular day counts and multiple dated advances/arrears against an independent schedule. Existing residual regressions primarily establish internal ledger invariants. Include tolerance scaling while preserving the established period-close balance-change and FX/date policy. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-005](statements-capital/05-residual-coupons.md).

- **Decision — Capital instrument defaults and prepayment scope need explicit contracts.** Convenience bond/swap defaults and placeholder rejection are implemented, while full-model validation rejects bonds, convertibles and derivatives whenever any prepayment rung exists, including default Sweep without ECF. Preserve the unsupported-domain gate and clarify caller ergonomics and intended defaults. Document product-level convention presets and default-stack rejection, and independently qualify each supported instrument schedule. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-001](statements-capital/01-instrument-construction.md).

- **Decision — Debt and hedge flow classification has distinct sign and clock rules.** Coupon flows net within an instrument/period; debt and hedge perspectives differ; principal, PIK and fees use absolute amounts; credit flows are ignored with a warning. Cash buckets use half-open payment boundaries while balances use economic dates, and hedge debt balances/accrual/funding are zero. Agree these reporting/classification conventions. Include gross-versus-net hedge reporting, signed custom-cash domains, distressed cash treatment and uniform invalid-period rejection; use independent borrower/hedge ledgers spanning period boundaries. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-002](statements-capital/02-cashflow-classification.md).

- **Decision — Funding face and accrual do not establish EIR accounting.** Residual mismatch warns while applying a scale ratio; default schedule accrual lacks ICMA/ISMA frequency context and fails closed when it is needed. Preserving OID face is not effective-interest accounting. Agree the supported accrual context and separate face-ledger from accounting measures. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-003](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md).

- **Decision — Dated debt claims separate capacity, arrears and reserves.** Economic capacity, past-effective settlement liability and future-paid reserves are separate; carried claims precede current claims and closing can include unpaid settlements. Aggregate fallback cannot express separated dates or advances. Agree the supported event/settlement contract and its fallback scope. Independently reconcile long sequences of arrears, new drawings and early/late settlements, and define absolute tolerance and debt-balance disclosure across currency scales. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-004](statements-capital/04-dated-principal.md).

- **Decision — Residual rebuilding preserves earned coupons without creating a new pricing model.** Balance changes accrue from the day after a period-close snapshot and preserve earned coupons and cash/PIK shares. The method does not introduce floating reset dynamics or recalculate embedded bond options. Agree those boundaries when applying capital actions. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-005](statements-capital/05-residual-coupons.md).

- **Decision — Waterfall conservation controls depend on currency and cash-source assumptions.** The waterfall is single-currency, uses mandatory cash-consuming categories and transactional state, but its substring guard is not a transitive proof of cash-source independence. All residual cash becomes equity even without an Equity rung. Agree the currency-agnostic 1e-6 shortfall tolerance and 0.005 dust floor and the formula dependency policy. Specify retained-cash/equity semantics and document the cash-pool dependency limitation. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md).

- **Decision — ECF sweep deductions and prepayment rights are limited.** ECF deducts earlier actual paid fees, interest and scheduled principal, not independent mandatory/voluntary buckets; explicit cash-interest override floors negative values to zero, optional deductions default to zero and later rungs reserve no cash. At-par prepayment omits premiums, penalties and legal rights. Adopt the intended lender definition and priority/rights scope. Pin contract-specific ECF definitions and input sign conventions. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-007](statements-capital/07-prepayments-and-ecf.md).

- **Decision — PIK toggles transfer arrears and can operate after paydown.** The rule is strict metric<threshold with evaluated-period hysteresis; carried interest arrears transfer fully to PIK. Toggle PIK is posted at close even after full paydown, separately from dated contractual PIK, without instrument-specific PIK capability or rate-stepup rules. Agree those claim and capability conventions. Independently reconcile state across changing reporting periods and toggle specifications, preserving the accepted period-close timing. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-008](statements-capital/08-pik-and-carry-state.md).

- **Decision — Capital reporting retains first-currency and merge assumptions.** Gross cash-plus-PIK expense excludes income, principal audit components are breakdowns rather than extra cash, and mutable merge retains the first reporting currency and omitted old rows. Component lookup/export performs no FX. Agree merge, completeness and presentation semantics. Independently reconcile native and reporting ledgers across dated FX changes. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

- **Evidence — Capital and waterfall policy lacks independent contractual reconciliation.** Selected tests cover formulas, boundaries, routing, state and internal conservation, but no independent debt ledger, lender ECF definition, legal waterfall, accounting-standard or currency/FX qualification was established. Obtain evidence scoped to the adopted contracts rather than treating test names as compliance. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-004](statements-capital/04-dated-principal.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-007](statements-capital/07-prepayments-and-ecf.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-008](statements-capital/08-pik-and-carry-state.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

## Margin: collateral and initial margin

[Scope inventory](margin-collateral-im/README.md).

- **Observed — SIMM parameter validation leaves divisor and correlation gaps.** Qualifying-credit concentration thresholds permit zero although concentration divides by them; `ir_inter_currency_correlation` is used without range/PSD validation, and missing cross-class pairs default to 1.0 and can pass PSD. Maintained embedded thresholds are positive. Reproduce adversarial inputs and define completeness/domain rules before inferring financial impact. Include concentration-adjusted within-bucket matrices and reproduce invalid IR intercurrency overrides in the IR calculator, rather than relying on normal embedded parameters. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-002](margin-collateral-im/02-simm-parameter-validation.md).

- **Observed — Registry loading bypasses some direct-type validation.** Wire registry records bypass direct Serde guards and do not check schema/version tags; monetary, MPOR, haircut, limit, maturity and rating domains are partly deferred or unchecked. Define one admitted input contract and inspect downstream consequences instead of assuming populated embedded tables certify all custom inputs. Resolve duplicate-cell overwrite policy and constructor-specific overlay propagation alongside schema/version enforcement. In particular, schedule registry rates above one are accepted before consumer rejection and zero schedule MPOR is not rejected here; align those boundary contracts across registry, direct structs and wire inputs. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-015](margin-collateral-im/15-collateral-eligibility-and-haircuts.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-017](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md).

- **Decision — Default margin amounts need currency and group-allocation semantics.** Defaults are copied numerically into the selected currency without EUR-equivalent conversion or group allocation. Agree jurisdiction/agreement-specific amount interpretation and caller responsibility rather than treating the numerical defaults as globally applicable thresholds. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-017](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md).

- **Observed — Collateral concentration is checked per row rather than per class.** Each allocation row is compared with the first matching class limit, without grouping or finite/nonnegative validation. Two 20% rows can each pass a 30% limit while their class total is 40%; this is a source-derived example, not an executed test. Define aggregation and input-domain requirements. Define negative, zero, NaN and mixed-unit input behavior, automatic-enforcement ownership and issuer-level scope. Add an independent contractual reference for the intended concentration covenant. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-016](margin-collateral-im/16-collateral-concentration.md).

- **Observed — Haircut helper and strict calculator select different eligibility rules.** Lightweight lookup/is_eligible ignores some metadata and permits default_haircut, while the strict calculator enforces maturity/rating and ignores that default. Base plus FX haircut is uncapped, overlap uses first-match inclusive tolerance, and the Treasury schedule does not identify issuer/country. Agree helper scope, boundary precedence and total-haircut domain. Include missing-metadata behavior, ordered maturity-range/default-limit validation across construction paths, and independent qualification of every schedule cell and actual security eligibility. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-015](margin-collateral-im/15-collateral-eligibility-and-haircuts.md).

- **Observed — External CCP inputs lack amount and MPOR validation.** External IM and direct/provided MPOR are not checked for nonnegative amount, expected currency or positive horizon. Define the external-source contract and reproduce invalid values at public calculator boundaries. Specify source-date provenance and qualify the empirical meaning of the proxy being conservative; no bound or CCP-reference accuracy has been established. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — CCP fallback retains an external horizon override.** An external MPOR override persists even when the external IM amount is absent and the proxy supplies the result. Agree whether proxy/external metadata may mix and add a targeted source-fallback fixture. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — ICE name resolution selects different clearing houses.** for_ccp("ICE") selects ClearUS, while the registry alias ice selects ClearCredit. Reconcile identity/default routing before applying clearing-house-specific parameters. Also document and adopt unknown-name routing explicitly. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — Margin settlement-day reporting uses calendar days.** MarginCall::days_to_settle subtracts dates as elapsed calendar days despite business-day Rustdoc. Agree the intended displayed metric and reconcile the documentation or implementation with calendar-aware evidence. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-019](margin-collateral-im/19-margin-dates-and-replay.md).

- **Investigate — Repo excess returns can exceed previously posted cash.** After the first observation, excess returns are not capped by prior cash margin, so the projected margin balance can become negative. Reproduce declining exposure/collateral paths and establish whether negative balances represent an intended receivable or an unsupported return. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md).

- **Decision — Repo margin projection has a narrow event contract.** All active repo types share one ratio algorithm; call_frequency and substitution fields do not drive projection, and no initial excess return or terminal unwind is synthesized. Interest uses caller-provided balances and a fixed 0.01-major-unit filter. Agree chronology, parameter domains, currency precision and omitted-event treatment. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md).

- **Observed — Sensitivity-table loading is not uniform container validation.** Ordinary SIMM/FRTB from_rows does not run final container validation, while SIMM curvature rows do; detached tables omit base currency/units and can ignore populated unused columns. Agree validation boundaries, table context and data-loss reporting. Include duplicate-row policy and the ordinary-versus-curvature credit tenor difference; define sensitivity sign/units and validation timing at instrument adapters. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-003](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Observed — SIMM FX and currency-scaling admission differs by path.** Same-currency ordinary FX vega is accepted unlike curvature. scaled_to_currency ignores a supplied rate when target equals base and otherwise accepts any scalar, leaving positivity to callers. Define consistent currency/scaling contracts and adversarial parity cases. Agree pair-order normalization and the retained expiry collapse; independently assess concentration-adjusted dependence for adversarial currency combinations. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-003](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md).

- **Observed — Margin provenance descriptions overstate or lag the implementation.** Version and file-count corrections belong to the [central methodology-pins follow-up](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#methodology-pins). Schema prose asserts GMRA compliance, and an ImResult example marks SIMM non-approximate although the calculator marks it approximate. Reconcile those scope/status descriptions without replacing provenance with compliance claims. Correct the registry module’s broad decimal-rate units comment, and qualify both GMRA and ISDA schema prose against the actual scope. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Decision — Approximate SIMM dimensional scope must remain explicit.** The USD-only historical model lacks ordinary IR subcurve/full expiry-underlying dimensions and product classes, uses residual equity/nonqualifying-credit treatment, and fully nets ordinary commodity risk within bucket. Curvature expiry scaling, squared correlations, component/class aggregation and the ten-day diagnostic basis need scoped adoption, not a full-SIMM equivalence claim. Adopt raw-net IR concentration under offsetting tenors; CQ sector/classification provenance; CNQ same-name multi-tenor concentration/correlation; equity residual-only coverage; prescribed source-volatility and per-bp units at each adapter; commodity full intra-bucket netting and absent ordinary concentration cross-adjustment; and residual/fixed-day curvature conventions. Decide square-root MPOR scaling outside ten days, approximation propagation through parts-based callers and omitted zero diagnostic rows. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-004](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-005](margin-collateral-im/05-simm-qualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-006](margin-collateral-im/06-simm-nonqualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-007](margin-collateral-im/07-simm-equity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-008](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-009](margin-collateral-im/09-simm-commodity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md).

- **Evidence — Approximate SIMM needs an independent portfolio and MPOR comparison.** Hand formulas and literal parameter transcriptions do not establish complete portfolio agreement or model-specific MPOR scaling. Obtain an independent licensed/reference comparison covering the implemented dimensional limitations, concentration and curvature aggregation. Cover high-concentration/mixed-sign CQ books, same-name multi-tenor and residual-plus-nonresidual credit, concentrated multi-name equity, FX dependence and mixed curvature factors/tenors/buckets. Independently verify all 289 commodity correlation cells and mixed-bucket vectors, including HVR diagnostic replay. Hand vectors do not settle omitted product dimensions or establish current-version/licensed SIMM certification. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-004](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-005](margin-collateral-im/05-simm-qualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-006](margin-collateral-im/06-simm-nonqualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-007](margin-collateral-im/07-simm-equity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-008](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-009](margin-collateral-im/09-simm-commodity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md).

- **Decision — Schedule and CCP margin leave rescaling and netting to callers.** Standalone outputs carry MPOR metadata without time scaling; portfolio callers rescale. Schedule trait defaults use five-year IR unless configured, and mixed-book NGR uses all supplied rows with a 40% floor. Agree legal netting partitions, maturity boundaries and applicable parameter versions. Adopt exact maturity-boundary, custom/missing-rate and all-negative/zero-gross-notional behavior, with model-versus-consumer MPOR provenance. Compare independent schedule portfolios and reverify the schedule version’s applicability. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-012](margin-collateral-im/12-schedule-notional.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-013](margin-collateral-im/13-schedule-net-gross.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Decision — IM agreement elections and group allocation are separate from VM.** One-way IM threshold/MTA and custody flags are distinct from variation-margin rules; group allocation remains caller-owned. Confirm the actual agreement elections and responsibility for group or regulatory thresholds. Specify per-side account scope and consumer enforcement of segregation/timing; reconcile the unsupported InternalModel selection and distinguish model MPOR from contractual MPOR in result metadata. Constructor names alone do not establish legal eligibility or model approval. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-017](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md).

- **Decision — VM replay models agreed collateral and does not enforce every schedule term.** VM uses symmetric thresholds, one additive nonnegative IA, MTA before rounding, delivery-up/return-down rounding, input-order replay and immediate agreed-collateral updates without frequency enforcement. Periodic/on-demand end boundaries differ. Agree pending versus settled scope, timing and legal elections. Include the lack of a post-round MTA recheck, nonnegative-IA domain, unsorted and same-day observation policy, calendar provenance and intraday terms. Qualify actual bilateral elections with independent call examples; unmapped currencies use weekend-only calendars. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-018](margin-collateral-im/18-variation-margin.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-019](margin-collateral-im/19-margin-dates-and-replay.md).

- **Decision — Collateral metrics are static proxies with special zero cases.** Utilization defines 0/0 as one and positive/zero as infinity; excess percentage is zero for nonpositive requirement. Haircut01 is positive collateral times one basis point and funding cost is undiscounted posted collateral times annual spread. Agree these result meanings without equating them to dynamic XVA or full-PV repricing. Adopt accepted negative-input domains and validation of externally supplied derived fields, as well as the Haircut01 sign/name; preserve those meanings in consuming reports. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-021](margin-collateral-im/21-collateral-metrics.md).

- **Evidence — Margin reference provenance needs qualification beyond local metadata.** Inspected production_simm_csa_audit, simm_schedule_parity and audit_regressions cases provide hand-formula, literal-transcription and invariant evidence. The recorded historical PDF hash/retrieval was not independently refreshed, and round trips do not establish economic or legal agreement. Qualify the adopted version, primary source and fixture provenance. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-002](margin-collateral-im/02-simm-parameter-validation.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Investigate — Margin aggregation under extreme and offsetting inputs.** Quantify overflow, cancellation and insertion-order effects in ordinary sensitivity sums, concentration-adjusted SIMM quadratics and schedule gross-by-class/notional totals. Include large cancelling IR/FX inputs and floored quadratic sums; deterministic serialization alone does not establish numerical accuracy or invariance. Qualify affected outputs against independent scaled/offsetting cases before drawing an impact conclusion. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-003](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-004](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-008](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-013](margin-collateral-im/13-schedule-net-gross.md).

## Statements: model, formula and check contracts

[Scope inventory](statements-contracts/README.md).

- **Observed — Bundled earnings conflict with the agreed reported-cost D&A convention.** Current EBITDA is revenue minus COGS and OPEX; EBIT, operating income, EBT, net income and return numerators then deduct D&A separately. Source/tests explicitly assume ex-D&A OPEX. The previously agreed primary convention uses reported COGS/OPEX already containing D&A, so those inputs can omit the EBITDA add-back and double-deduct D&A below EBITDA. Reconcile formulas, examples, bindings and fixtures against that existing direction; do not reopen it as an optional policy. Existing formula assertions pin the current behavior and are not evidence that it matches the agreed convention. Include documentation and an independently specified accounting example separating D&A inclusion from add-backs; retain no compatibility heuristic for interpreting costs. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-007](statements-contracts/07-earnings-and-margins.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-008](statements-contracts/08-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-009](statements-contracts/09-returns-on-capital.md).

- **Observed — Return and earnings tax identifiers can diverge.** Return numerators read `taxes`, while net income reads `tax_expense`; no alias or equality check reconciles them. Establish whether distinct meanings are intended or consolidate the input contract. The different lookup is confirmed statically; no financial discrepancy was executed. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-009](statements-contracts/09-returns-on-capital.md).

- **Observed — Cumulative/statistical DSL arity differs across documentation and execution.** `cumsum`, `cumprod`, `cummin`, `cummax`, `std`, `var` and `median` are documented as unary but compilation only rejects zero arguments. Surplus arguments participate in compilation, typing and dependency discovery, while evaluation consumes only argument zero. Establish one arity contract and test ignored erroring arguments; the evaluator record tracks the same cross-layer discrepancy. Define trusted versus untrusted caller-built AST resource limits as part of the same function/compiler boundary. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-004](statements-contracts/04-dsl-syntax-and-lowering.md).

- **Observed — Registry dependency closure does not discover qualified references.** Load-time closure intersects raw AST names with unqualified same-namespace IDs, so `fin.a` does not match `a`; qualified and cross-namespace references are not automatically included or covered by registry cycle checks. Explicitly define supported inclusion semantics and test supplied-versus-omitted dependencies; later model/evaluator resolution is a separate boundary. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-006](statements-contracts/06-registry-and-dependencies.md).

- **Investigate — Textual namespace qualification permits collisions.** Namespace validation requires only nonempty text, despite ASCII grammar checks on metric identifiers. Qualification replaces standalone text, including a function-call name if a metric shares it, and builder namespace derivation uses the first dot. Reproduce function-name and dotted-namespace collisions before selecting stronger naming constraints or AST rewriting. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-006](statements-contracts/06-registry-and-dependencies.md).

- **Observed — Published schema examples and ordering comments have drifted.** `financial_model_examples` claims a one-value/one-formula model but constructs an empty node map. `ARTIFACTS` is not path-sorted despite its comment; shared rendering owns final ordering. Reconcile these source descriptions without classifying them as financial-output defects. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-016](statements-contracts/16-wire-schema-and-errors.md).

- **Decision — Model acceptance must be distinguished from evaluation readiness.** Unknown-reference graph errors warn while semantic validation succeeds; direct scalar values can be nonfinite, and unresolved dimensions, empty models, period gaps and noncanonical PeriodId/date combinations are accepted here. Raw constructors/direct Serde do not perform the full named-loader validation. Specify the boundaries requiring closed references, resolved units and complete data, preserving existing strict-validation requirements rather than implying loader success establishes readiness. Also define duplicate-observation provenance, full identifier grammar, publication-date ownership, numeric Scalar scales, required strict-load limits and supported irregular timeline/authoring states. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-001](statements-contracts/01-model-timeline-and-values.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-002](statements-contracts/02-authoring-and-availability.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-005](statements-contracts/05-currency-dimensions.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-016](statements-contracts/16-wire-schema-and-errors.md).

- **Decision — Forecast wire shape is not forecast-domain validation.** Typed decoding rejects unknown fields/nulls and checks seed representation, but method ranges, history, conditional parameters and min/max ordering are deferred. Make API error/stage guarantees explicit and qualify invalid configurations at the stage that actually consumes them. Ensure caller-selected method controls are documented at their consuming stage and are not silently ignored. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-003](statements-contracts/03-forecast-parameter-contract.md).

- **Decision — Squared monetary units and descriptive registry metadata have limited authority.** Monetary variance remains Unknown because full squared-unit algebra is absent. Registry `requires`/`unit_type` do not control dependency discovery or numerical unit inference; existing node IDs may intentionally shadow built-ins, and unqualified self-lag references count as registry cycles although the model supports temporal recurrence. Define publication, recurrence and override contracts without treating metadata as enforcement. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-005](statements-contracts/05-currency-dimensions.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-006](statements-contracts/06-registry-and-dependencies.md).

- **Decision — Hashes and supplied results need an explicit provenance boundary.** Semantic validation mutates inferred type fields, while `content_hash` hashes the current state without validation. `run_model(Some results)` uses supplied results without recomputation or model-hash proof, although identity/formula checks validate unit consistency. Establish validation-before-hash and model/result identity requirements. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-014](statements-contracts/14-comparison-and-suite-policy.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-016](statements-contracts/16-wire-schema-and-errors.md).

- **Decision — Bundled credit ratios and returns need a bounded financial meaning.** Coverage is EBITDA-based TTM rather than a cash-only covenant calculation; PIK, add-backs and debt definition remain caller-owned. ROE/ROA/ROIC/ROCE divide current-period flows by endpoint balances without annualization or averaging; ROIC subtracts reported taxes and uses debt plus equity without cash netting. The 3x/1.5x/1.25x prose thresholds are neither runtime policy nor independently qualified universal guidance. Preserve the already agreed rejection of ambiguous partial TTM. Qualify denominator admissibility, cash-versus-PIK treatment, each intended threshold, time horizon, average-versus-endpoint capital and operating-tax treatment explicitly. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-008](statements-contracts/08-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-009](statements-contracts/09-returns-on-capital.md).

- **Decision — Passing accounting checks does not establish complete observation coverage.** Missing/nonfinite identity inputs warn and skip, gaps produce informational roll-forward findings, and first periods have no roll-forward. Data-quality checks are composed explicitly; empty or all-skipped suites can pass. Materiality filtering preserves errors. Require explicit evaluated/completeness coverage where publication demands it, without reviving the already-corrected historical materiality concern. Define opening anchors, required cash adjustment lines, mandatory node/period coverage and treatment of advisory findings for publication. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-010](statements-contracts/10-balance-sheet-articulation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-011](statements-contracts/11-cash-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-012](statements-contracts/12-retained-earnings-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-013](statements-contracts/13-data-quality-checks.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-014](statements-contracts/14-comparison-and-suite-policy.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-015](statements-contracts/15-formula-checks.md).

- **Decision — Account membership and dividend-input enforcement need qualification.** Duplicate IDs can double-count and checks do not establish a complete chart of accounts. [ADR 0044](conventions/0044-financial-perspectives-and-boundary-conversions.md) already accepts the MagnitudePositive dividend default and explicit InflowPositive alternative; those sign choices are not reopened. Wrong-sign magnitude dividends currently warn but retain signed arithmetic, so enforcement remains unreconciled. Unconfigured dividends become zero, whereas configured missing dividends or adjustments skip. Generic adjustment labels do not establish retained-earnings eligibility. Define the remaining membership and missing-input contracts and qualify enforcement of the accepted signs. Specify eligible retained-earnings adjustment accounts and duplicate-ID handling, including suite/check identities. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-010](statements-contracts/10-balance-sheet-articulation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-011](statements-contracts/11-cash-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-012](statements-contracts/12-retained-earnings-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-013](statements-contracts/13-data-quality-checks.md).

- **Decision — Formula-check preflight can prevent fallback evaluation.** Syntactic finite-input preflight examines all direct inputs, including `coalesce` and untaken branches, so recovery can be skipped before evaluation. Afterwards, nonfinite output without a DivisionByZero warning becomes insufficient-data Warning, including finite-input domain/overflow cases. Define missing-data versus invalid-domain classification and required coverage; a concrete runtime example remains unexecuted. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-015](statements-contracts/15-formula-checks.md).

- **Evidence — Accounting arithmetic and tolerances need scale-specific qualification.** Ordinary f64 sums have finite-intermediate guards and overflow tests, but these do not establish cancellation accuracy across scales. Interpret default absolute 0.01 and relative 1e-9 tolerances in base units and business materiality; overrides are absolute-only. Hand-calculated identities, embedded-formula assertions, schema/hash checks and properties establish intended behavior at selected inputs, not independent accounting-method adoption. Qualify reporting scales, acceptable residuals and absolute-versus-relative materiality for each intended reporting use. Records: [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-010](statements-contracts/10-balance-sheet-articulation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-011](statements-contracts/11-cash-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-012](statements-contracts/12-retained-earnings-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-014](statements-contracts/14-comparison-and-suite-policy.md).

## Statements: evaluation, forecasts and reporting

[Scope inventory](statements-evaluation/README.md).

- **Observed — Dated CAGR still uses the wrong accepted clock.** Daily/weekly `growth_rate` uses Actual/Actual between included period-end dates, while accepted ADR 0008 requires Actual/365 Fixed for explicitly dated CAGR. Its regression constructs observations with the same Actual/Actual helper and therefore checks wiring. Reconcile code/tests with the accepted fixed-365 direction; frequency annualization remains distinct and ambiguous/incomplete TTM rejection is preserved. Use independent fixed-365 leap-window fixtures rather than regenerating expectations with the same implementation clock. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-003](statements-evaluation/03-temporal-operators-and-period-windows.md).

- **Observed — Aggregate capital FX is period-close even under CashflowDate.** All aggregate `cs.*` conversions receive the inclusive period-end snapshot; individual cash dates are unavailable at that boundary. Preserve the agreed period-close waterfall allocation convention and reconcile capital-builder prose promising cash-date conversion. This is shared with the capital/contracts audit and is not a proposal to change the agreed FX policy. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-012](statements-evaluation/12-capital-state-orchestration.md).

- **Observed — Selected accumulation paths still use ordinary sums.** Rollup Sum and trend/seasonal fitting use ordinary floating-point sums, while formula n-ary and normalization paths use compensated kernels. Reconcile applicable monetary/exposure accumulation with accepted ADR 0042 and qualify cancellation/extreme-value requirements; no impact magnitude or new numerical failure has been reproduced. Specify supported magnitude/history length and reproducibility tolerances for trend fitting, and independently qualify rollup cancellation and extreme seasonal factors. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-009](statements-evaluation/09-trend-extrapolation.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-010](statements-evaluation/10-seasonal-decomposition.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-014](statements-evaluation/14-period-rollup.md).

- **Observed — Surplus cumulative/statistical arguments are ignored at runtime.** Seven nominally unary functions accept extra arguments through the compiler but evaluate only argument zero. Reconcile this with the compiler/dependency behavior tracked in the contracts scope, including an ignored erroring second argument when defining rejection versus evaluation. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-004](statements-evaluation/04-historical-statistics-and-ewm.md).

- **Observed — Forecast correlation is not represented in the dependency DAG.** A peer must generate shocks before its follower, yet peer declarations do not add graph edges. A present peer can therefore fail through an absent or not-yet-generated shock cache. Establish dependency-order and replay coverage before selecting peer-graph enforcement or joint covariance generation. Include declaration/order permutations, explicit or hidden peers, and independent sampling-error tolerances. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-008](statements-evaluation/08-correlated-shocks.md).

- **Decision — Zero correlation does not preserve the independent random stream.** Correlated paths use XOR seed mixing and uncorrelated paths multiply-plus-hash, so adding a rho=0 configuration changes exact draws. This is a confirmed replay/comparison distinction, not distribution-bias evidence. Ordinary non-Monte-Carlo evaluation validates then ignores correlation with a tracing warning; define both mode and stream-identity contracts. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-008](statements-evaluation/08-correlated-shocks.md).

- **Observed — Normalization replacement can leave stale provenance and dependents.** Merging replaces the entire output node and clears checks while retaining existing `node_sources` and dependent node values. Define recomputation/provenance semantics; append-only was a proposal rather than an accepted policy. Preserve the previously agreed reported D&A in COGS/OPEX and avoid duplicated add-backs. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-015](statements-evaluation/15-normalization-adjustments.md).

- **Observed — Money conversion diagnostics do not fully describe exported values.** Finite values overflowing Money conversion remain numeric with no Money view but receive a NonFiniteValue diagnostic. Long exports select raw monetary values from type metadata, not successful Money conversion; dated node export preserves scalar/nonfinite values without cash-perspective conversion. Clarify representation and warning guarantees rather than imposing universal NaN rejection. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-013](statements-evaluation/13-result-units-and-exports.md).

- **Observed — Monte Carlo probability and persistence have domain gaps.** Breach probability does not reject a nonfinite threshold; NaN gives zero exceedances. Paths are serde-skipped, so quantile-only roundtrips cannot reproduce pathwise probability. Column restoration does not fully validate unique/complete path keys, and the path index narrows to u32 without an explicit configuration cap. Establish threshold, path-count and persisted-path contracts. Adopt default percentiles and permitted output shapes and independently qualify moments, quantiles and sampling error. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-011](statements-evaluation/11-monte-carlo-evaluation.md).

- **Observed — Forecast domain prose overstates sign restrictions.** Normal and mean-reverting kernels support negative bases, and growth rates below -100% can flip signs. Reconcile prose asserting rejected negative bases or preserved signs with the implemented kernels; no additional economic restriction is adopted by this audit. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-006](statements-evaluation/06-deterministic-forecasts-and-fades.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-007](statements-evaluation/07-stochastic-forecast-kernels.md).

- **Investigate — Geometric fade can overflow an otherwise representable path.** The implementation forms target/base directly. Finite same-sign endpoints can produce infinite or zero ratios, rejecting or collapsing the path even where intermediate levels could be represented. Define the extreme-domain requirement and compare a log-space reference before choosing a change. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-006](statements-evaluation/06-deterministic-forecasts-and-fades.md).

- **Evidence — Lognormal clamps and per-step stochastic parameters need qualification.** Exponent clipping at ±709 is a tail approximation and underflowed zero remains finite. Coefficients are per model step, without elapsed-time scaling or fitting. Obtain independent moment, tail and domain evidence rather than treating same-kernel replay or zero-volatility tests as statistical validation. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-007](statements-evaluation/07-stochastic-forecast-kernels.md).

- **Investigate — A finite normalization total can hide a nonfinite bridge row.** Only final base plus total is validated, not each stored intermediate adjusted_value. A finite 1e308 base with +1e308 then -1e308 adjustments can have an infinite intermediate and finite final result; this is a static illustration, not an executed reproduction. Eager reference-unit metadata validation is distinct from date-driven numeric checks. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-015](statements-evaluation/15-normalization-adjustments.md).

- **Decision — Period labels and numeric offset tolerances define temporal behavior.** QTD/FiscalYTD construct Gregorian month IDs from year/index labels rather than explicit model dates. Lag helpers allow abs(fract)<=1e-10 then truncate, whereas growth_rate uses a round-distance check and rejects positive bases below 1e-10. Qualify fiscal-ID geometry and these separate numeric domains. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-003](statements-evaluation/03-temporal-operators-and-period-windows.md).

- **Decision — Missingness and tolerance rules differ by operator.** Absolute equality/rank tolerances, nonfinite numeric Boolean/annualization flags, missing-versus-NaN EWM decay and finite-only aggregates are separate implemented policies. Pin scale, flag and missing-value contracts per operation rather than asserting a universal rule. Use independent scale-specific truth-table/aggregate-order cases, define infinite-frequency and compounding/correction flags, and qualify rank-tie and EWM correction tolerances. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-002](statements-evaluation/02-scalar-formulas-and-undefined-values.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-004](statements-evaluation/04-historical-statistics-and-ewm.md).

- **Decision — Explicit forecasts and bounds do not restart or constrain latent state.** Explicit forecast cells leave latent run/seasonal phase intact, output bounds do not feed back into recursion, and supplied seasonal/time-series history can differ from dated node history. Adopt these use-case semantics and establish predictive evidence beyond the existing phase/clipping regressions. Document the base requirement even for supplied-history methods, per-period versus elapsed-time use, allowed sign-changing growth, seasonal phase anchoring and extreme/degenerate factors. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-005](statements-evaluation/05-forecast-segments-and-bounds.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-006](statements-evaluation/06-deterministic-forecasts-and-fades.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-010](statements-evaluation/10-seasonal-decomposition.md).

- **Evidence — Monte Carlo reports samples without a statistical or check-suite certificate.** Every forecast cell must be finite, all samples are retained internally, ordinary model checks are not run, and exact finite-sample quantiles have no convergence error estimate. Native serial/parallel equality and deterministic debt replay establish consistency, not independent statistical/accounting accuracy. Qualify the one-pass post-waterfall dependency/snapshot contract using independent multi-period cash/debt bridges, issuance-at-boundary and dependent-reevaluation cases. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-011](statements-evaluation/11-monte-carlo-evaluation.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-012](statements-evaluation/12-capital-state-orchestration.md).

- **Decision — Rollup and normalization transform results under different contracts.** Rollup defaults to Sum without stock/ratio inference, rejects incomplete buckets and drops earlier provenance, capital state, checks and warnings. Normalization caps apply independently, default self-cap uses Reported, and numeric validation covers only inputs used at target dates. Adopt these distinctions with independent flow/stock/ratio and adjustment examples. Specify balance/ratio/trailing-node declarations, rollup provenance/diagnostic presentation, positive-base cap clipping and the agreement governing independent versus shared caps. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-014](statements-evaluation/14-period-rollup.md), [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-015](statements-evaluation/15-normalization-adjustments.md).

- **Decision — Prepared evaluation and cell precedence require an explicit contract.** Adopt mask-before-explicit-value precedence, inclusive availability equality and the prepared-plan mutation domain. The structural fingerprint excludes numeric values and some live-read model fields; do not present a match as complete financial-input identity. Records: [STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-001](statements-evaluation/01-graph-precedence-and-prepared-plans.md).

## Statements analytics: valuation, comparisons and templates

[Scope inventory](statements-analytics-valuation/README.md).

- **Observed — DCF sensitivity can leave the direct-DCF input domain.** Growth-up shocks are capped by WACC minus epsilon but not the separate default 5% stable-growth ceiling, so a valid baseline can produce a rejected shocked point. Statements are evaluated once without market/as-of inputs, unlike direct DCF. Reconcile cap and market-dependent statement support; numerical impact was not executed. Define bump/ceiling interaction, asymmetric-range presentation and independently reconciled sensitivity cases. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-02](statements-analytics-valuation/02-dcf-sensitivity.md).

- **Decision — LBO funding and returns are a bridge rather than an independent transaction gate.** Entry uses the first model period metric and sponsor equity is residual, constructing sources/uses equality. MOIC omits interim cashflows and dates, exit equity is not floored, and optional failed checks do not gate results. Adopt naming, negative-domain inputs and gating before treating this result as transaction validation. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-03](statements-analytics-valuation/03-lbo.md).

- **Observed — Analysis builder order and optional controls affect selection.** Statement-only analysis ignores a supplied check suite, a net-debt override called before DCF has no effect, and terminal value without WACC does not enable DCF. Reconcile these source-confirmed branches with the intended API contract. Include independent mixed-frequency reporting vectors in the agreed gating contract. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-04](statements-analytics-valuation/04-analysis-reports-units.md).

- **Observed — Report units and helper finiteness boundaries differ.** Text credit reports hardcode $M and may render raw nonfinite FCF while checked assessments filter it. Unit helpers reconcile typed Money and numeric projections, but `finite_ratio` validates only the quotient; operand guards depend on the caller. Establish the result/report unit and finite-value contract. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-04](statements-analytics-valuation/04-analysis-reports-units.md).

- **Decision — Comparable populations are not automatically normalized.** NaN market cap passes configured comparison bounds, peers are not deduplicated, and direct PeerSet retains subject entries. PeriodBasis labels data without normalizing currency, time or accounting basis. Define input-domain, subject-exclusion and comparable-population requirements. Specify minimum peer-quality requirements and independent ranked-data/regression reference vectors. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-05](statements-analytics-valuation/05-comps-population-statistics.md).

- **Decision — Relative-value scores and confidence have specific statistical meanings.** Regression signals divide subject residual by peer residual sample SD (n−1), not residual SE (n−2) or predictive uncertainty; raw-z eligibility precedes regression. Confidence is weighted R² across regression dimensions only and peer count covers the full population. Adopt score/confidence names, normalization and coverage reporting; formulas do not establish empirical ranking quality. Define economically appropriate directions/weights and duplicate-dimension policy. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-06](statements-analytics-valuation/06-comps-scoring.md).

- **Investigate — Goal-seek driver precedence and search domains need independent tests.** Actual or calculated drivers are allowed and inserted literals interact with evaluator precedence. Monetary probe-conversion failures become NaN without their cause; a sign-restricted search retries unconstrained. Default ±1e6 bounds and target-unit residual tolerance require use-specific qualification, including multiple roots and nonlinear cases. Include discontinuous/nonmonotone objectives and require explanatory propagation of monetary conversion failures. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-07](statements-analytics-valuation/07-goal-seek.md).

- **Decision — Forecast-error exclusions depend on measurement scale.** MAPE/sMAPE use an absolute 1e-10 denominator cutoff, so unit rescaling can change which observations remain; only MAPE reports retained count. Define scale and coverage reporting and obtain out-of-sample assessment beyond formula/arithmetic checks. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-08](statements-analytics-valuation/08-forecast-errors.md).

- **Decision — Scenario inheritance and comparison baselines need an explicit contract.** Separate-map merging lets an ancestor explicit cell override defeat a child model-wide value. Comparison tables choose named base or insertion-first, use baseline periods only, and do not strictly reconcile types/finiteness. Adopt hierarchy, baseline and missing-domain semantics. Require metadata sufficient for economic comparability between scenario outputs. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-09](statements-analytics-valuation/09-scenario-inheritance.md).

- **Decision — Tornado endpoints do not establish full response extrema.** `ParameterSpec.base_value` supports percentage construction/metadata but does not replace the source-model baseline. Tornado entries use minimum/maximum input endpoints, which can omit interior output extrema, and impact ranking across target units is raw. Adopt baseline, nonmonotonicity and ranking semantics. Use independent nonlinear cases to qualify display ranges and measurement semantics. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-10](statements-analytics-valuation/10-sensitivity-grids.md).

- **Decision — Variance bridges are arithmetic rather than causal attribution.** Validation compares node unit metadata, including None==None, without proving typed-amount consistency or all finiteness. Relative change divides by signed baseline; additive bridges sum raw driver deltas and accept duplicate drivers. Reconcile attribution labels and result invariants with the intended interpretation. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-11](statements-analytics-valuation/11-variance-bridges.md).

- **Decision — Dependency explanations need visible completeness and time context.** A missing component downgrades a nominally additive formula to an unsigned reference list. Nonlinear/lagged formulas show available current-period references, missing CS lookup errors are omitted, and absent cell provenance can default to formula interpretation.
