Define trace-completeness and temporal warnings. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-12](statements-analytics-valuation/12-dependency-explanations.md).

- **Decision — Corkscrew success is not an accounting validation certificate.** Tolerance is absolute 0.01 numeric units; account currency, types and duplicate membership are not checked. Lenient mode can return Success with warnings for a finite identity break while treating invalid input more strictly. Establish coverage, typing, materiality and status-gating requirements. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-13](statements-analytics-valuation/13-corkscrew.md).

- **Decision — Scorecard thresholds and evidence coverage need explicit constraints.** Threshold finiteness/order/gaps/overlap and unknown threshold rating names are not comprehensively validated; best-first inclusive boundaries select the better bucket. Missing evidence is omitted and surviving weights renormalized, and a rating may coexist with Failed status. Adopt minimum coverage, threshold domains and gating; reconcile Fitch introductory prose with the actual sp/moodys registry. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-14](statements-analytics-valuation/14-credit-scorecards.md).

- **Investigate — Recurrence and vintage templates lack scale-aware truncation guarantees.** Numeric inputs and node collisions are not prevalidated; nonfinite coefficients/opening values can generate invalid DSL. Vintage coefficients below 1e-10 are omitted without a volume-scaled tail-error budget or probability/decay constraints, and absent historical lags become zero. Define generic convolution versus survival-curve scope and quantify truncation error. Specify node-collision behavior, historical initialization and caller-owned economic constraints. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-15](statements-analytics-valuation/15-recurrence-templates.md).

- **Observed — Lease IDs and fixed template names can collide.** Duplicate lease IDs overwrite generated nodes while their identifiers repeat in totals; fixed aggregation names can also collide with caller nodes. Establish uniqueness and namespace rules and reproduce duplicate/collision outcomes before using generated totals as a lease-administration reconciliation. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-16](statements-analytics-valuation/16-real-estate-templates.md).

- **Decision — Lease projection conventions require independent contractual qualification.** Periods per year comes from the first period, with no day prorating or mixed-frequency guard; scalar currency is unenforced, negative finite rents and growth below -100% remain allowed. Full free rent is allocated before renewal-probability loss under an explicit identity-preserving convention. Adopt these domains and attribution semantics using independent lease examples. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-16](statements-analytics-valuation/16-real-estate-templates.md).

- **Evidence — Valuation and scorecard labels need independent method support.** DCF uses exact contiguous whole-period terminal years and valuation-date-specific opening balance sheets; preserve those observed boundaries rather than inventing stub annualization. Qualify WACC/tax-shield calibration, date ownership, annual basis and independently reconciled DCF/LBO/comps/lease examples. Scorecard registry points do not establish empirical PD or agency calibration, and no accounting/regulatory certification follows from method names. Include mixed-frequency and leap-year DCF reference cases. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-01](statements-analytics-valuation/01-corporate-dcf.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-03](statements-analytics-valuation/03-lbo.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-05](statements-analytics-valuation/05-comps-population-statistics.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-06](statements-analytics-valuation/06-comps-scoring.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-14](statements-analytics-valuation/14-credit-scorecards.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-16](statements-analytics-valuation/16-real-estate-templates.md).

## Statements analytics: credit checks and impairment

[Scope inventory](statements-analytics-credit/README.md).

- **Observed — Credit suite and cash-burn descriptions differ from implementation.** Suite prose promises leverage/coverage trends, but construction selects debt and EBITDA levels. CreditMapping describes monthly burn while runway treats it as per-model-period burn and converts nominal frequency to months. Reconcile selection and units and establish the supported period domain. Test irregular/date-specific periods if nominal-frequency runway conversion remains supported. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-01](statements-analytics-credit/01-suite-selection.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-07](statements-analytics-credit/07-liquidity-runway.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-08](statements-analytics-credit/08-sequential-credit-signals.md).

- **Observed — Interest fallback lacks the documented warning and period conversion.** Fallback interest multiplies average/current balance by the supplied rate without annual-to-period scaling. Current-balance substitution emits no promised warning, and a configured direct CS-interest node suppresses estimation even when its value is missing, causing a skip. Reconcile rate/period and precedence contracts; no numerical impact was executed. Add independent irregular-period, changing-balance and missing-prior cases, and define partial-pair coverage. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-03](statements-analytics-credit/03-interest-reconciliation.md).

- **Decision — A passed credit/reconciliation check can mean no usable observations.** Info/Warning usually preserve passed=true and missing/nonfinite operands often skip, without evaluated/completeness counts. Missing/nonfinite optional capex additions or PPE disposals become zero; the credit nonfinite sweep excludes cash/burn, growth excludes near-zero bases, and tax excludes nonpositive pretax income. Define required coverage and distinguish unavailable from evaluated passes under existing strict-validation policies. Specify suite-level disposals/other PPE bridge inputs, duplicated mapping behavior, zero-base growth handling, loss-period tax coverage and diagnostics for failed LTM helpers. Decide gap/equality/reset behavior for credit signals and their actual-to-forecast transition/cadence meaning. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-01](statements-analytics-credit/01-suite-selection.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-02](statements-analytics-credit/02-stock-flow-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-04](statements-analytics-credit/04-growth-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-05](statements-analytics-credit/05-tax-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-06](statements-analytics-credit/06-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-07](statements-analytics-credit/07-liquidity-runway.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-08](statements-analytics-credit/08-sequential-credit-signals.md).

- **Decision — Unit and finite-result enforcement varies by check.** Working-capital and current leverage/coverage checks enforce units, while capex, PPE, dividend, interest, growth, tax and runway paths lack equivalent cross-node unit/finite-result guards. Reconcile these boundaries with accepted numerical/validation requirements and qualify incompatible-unit and extreme-value cases; no relaxation is proposed. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-02](statements-analytics-credit/02-stock-flow-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-03](statements-analytics-credit/03-interest-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-04](statements-analytics-credit/04-growth-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-05](statements-analytics-credit/05-tax-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-06](statements-analytics-credit/06-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-07](statements-analytics-credit/07-liquidity-runway.md).

- **Decision — Check configuration and sign conventions require a complete contract.** FCF counts and trend lookback must be positive, but count ordering, leverage-band nesting, coverage/runway floor ordering, growth/tax bounds and several tolerances lack finite/ordered-policy validation. SignConventionPolicy only warns or suppresses warnings; InflowPositive does not transform equations. LBO setup overwrites the caller leverage warning band with [0,8]. Define invalid-input and override behavior, including the absolute-prior-value growth denominator and actual-to-forecast transition semantics. Establish threshold provenance and borrower/sector/cadence applicability, including absolute tolerances and runway horizons. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-01](statements-analytics-credit/01-suite-selection.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-02](statements-analytics-credit/02-stock-flow-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-03](statements-analytics-credit/03-interest-reconciliation.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-04](statements-analytics-credit/04-growth-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-05](statements-analytics-credit/05-tax-plausibility.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-06](statements-analytics-credit/06-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-07](statements-analytics-credit/07-liquidity-runway.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-08](statements-analytics-credit/08-sequential-credit-signals.md).

- **Decision — Credit ratio and maturity clocks represent different financial measures.** Leverage requires complete TTM earnings and skips when the helper is unavailable; coverage uses current-period flows. Capital summaries group whole seniority ranks before ratios, suppress incomplete earnings/interest windows, and group current nonzero balances by final maturity year rather than actual principal due. Define these distinct scopes without implying equivalent covenant measures; preserve agreed rejection of ambiguous partial TTM. Qualify fiscal/custom-period windows and incomplete-interest diagnostics, and specify gross/net and pari-passu reporting definitions. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-06](statements-analytics-credit/06-leverage-and-coverage.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-09](statements-analytics-credit/09-capital-structure-summary.md).

- **Observed — Credit summary exports and context completeness lose information.** Capital-summary numeric table export uses default metadata without the summary currency. Credit-context minima use available periods only; missing instruments yield empty output without marking every requested period skipped, and duplicate reference periods retain the last value. Define currency, coverage and duplicate-period reporting guarantees. Approve cash, cash-plus-PIK and fee-inclusive debt-service denominators, with explicit numerator definitions and currency provenance. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-09](statements-analytics-credit/09-capital-structure-summary.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-10](statements-analytics-credit/10-credit-context.md).

- **Observed — Custom CECL marginal-PD overrides can bypass probability validation.** `forecast_boundary_hazard` maps marginal PD below one to log hazard and every other value to infinity, so NaN or >1 from custom overrides becomes infinite hazard. Straddling forecast/reversion probabilities are clamped before final [0,1] validation. The default trait method validates cumulative endpoints but custom implementations can bypass it. Validate each conditional probability before transformation/composition, or justify and test a stronger trusted-source contract; this is confirmed static control flow without runtime reproduction. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-17](statements-analytics-credit/17-cecl-forecast-reversion.md).

- **Investigate — CECL forecast hazard changes with the integration segment width.** Linear reversion estimates its forecast anchor over the immediately preceding segment-width interval, or a forward interval at zero forecast horizon. Bucket width, a partial last bucket or a split bucket’s post-forecast segment therefore changes the anchor; midpoint integration also does not split at reversion end. Compare against fixed-anchor piecewise-hazard integrals and quantify grid dependence. Existing immediate/linear tests assert positivity rather than accuracy or convergence. Agree discounting/recovery assumptions and the scenario/policy evidence required for allowance audit. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-17](statements-analytics-credit/17-cecl-forecast-reversion.md).

- **Evidence — Impairment and WARM midpoint grids need knot and scale qualification.** Performing ECL multiplies unconditional cumulative-PD increments by midpoint EAD/discount without splitting at PD/EAD knots. WARM exposure-years is exact for a single linear profile but buckets can cross piecewise-linear knots. Allowance/scenario totals use ordinary sums; accepted ADR 0042 governs accumulation reconciliation rather than a new optional policy. Independent knot/dynamic-range benchmarks are needed beyond the two-bucket unconditional-PD oracle and constant/linear WARM cases. Include high EIR, long maturities, severe recovery delays, zero/one/invalid PD, zero maturity/forecast, impaired discount limits and arbitrary EAD-knot/grid placement. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-12](statements-analytics-credit/12-ecl-exposure-and-pd.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-15](statements-analytics-credit/15-ecl-bucket-measurement.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-16](statements-analytics-credit/16-ecl-scenario-weighting.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-17](statements-analytics-credit/17-cecl-forecast-reversion.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-18](statements-analytics-credit/18-cecl-warm.md).

- **Evidence — Accounting policy labels lack parameter-level adoption and output provenance.** Embedded defaults include 0.01 absolute PD increase, 2× relative PD, three notches, DPD30/90, cure3/12, quarterly buckets, a two-year CECL forecast and 2% historical PD. Source version/effective dates are descriptive, not date-driven selection. Results lack resolved policy/curve identity, and Exposure/EclResult monetary fields carry no Currency/Money identity. The 0.75 CCF label Basel IRB does not establish suitability for every accounting exposure. Attach institution-specific adoption, calibration and reproducibility evidence. Distinguish each numerical calibration from the accounting framework, decide immutable resolved-policy snapshots, and reconcile registry/runtime validation and embedded-data failure behavior. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-12](statements-analytics-credit/12-ecl-exposure-and-pd.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-14](statements-analytics-credit/14-ecl-policy-defaults.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-15](statements-analytics-credit/15-ecl-bucket-measurement.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-16](statements-analytics-credit/16-ecl-scenario-weighting.md).

- **Decision — Staging domain and cure fields need explicit meaning.** Curve SICR caps comparison at 30 years, unknown ratings skip notch assessment, and custom scale/DPD order/cure consistency are incompletely validated. Early Stage-3 curve branches precede PD-source checks while direct-PD calls validate first. Stage3-to2 with active SICR reports cured=false; no-trigger probation can retain Stage2/3 with NoTrigger. Adopt threshold/cadence and result-field semantics; the positive-current/zero-origination relative-PD error is already explicit, not a newly discovered defect. Independently qualify matched-window PD comparisons and institution-specific SICR/cure calibration. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-13](statements-analytics-credit/13-ecl-staging.md).

- **Decision — Impairment measurement is narrower than a full cash-shortfall model.** Stage3 equals EAD0 minus one discounted recovery yet requires a PD lookup at zero despite no future-PD dependence. Stage1 restricts default-event horizon to one year with supplied LGD. Raw cumulative-PD flat extrapolation produces no additional default increments beyond the last knot. Adopt these boundaries with independent reference cases rather than treating IFRS9 naming as complete approval. Clarify query-time validation, drawn schedule-at-zero behavior, negative-rate support, Stage-1 LGD interpretation and recovery timing, and qualify custom PD sources and tail-sensitive allowances. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-12](statements-analytics-credit/12-ecl-exposure-and-pd.md), [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-15](statements-analytics-credit/15-ecl-bucket-measurement.md).

- **Decision — Scenario weighting shares stage and exposure assumptions.** Weight sums within 1e-6 are accepted without renormalization, duplicate IDs are allowed, zero-weight scenarios are still priced, and caller-assigned stage, EAD and EIR are shared across scenarios. Define tolerance, identity, zero-weight failure and scenario-specific scope, including which economic variation belongs in supplied PD/LGD. Document full-config versus convenience reachability and independently reconcile differing PD grids and LGD override modes. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-16](statements-analytics-credit/16-ecl-scenario-weighting.md).

- **Decision — WARM bypasses supplied impairment and scenario controls.** WARM permits empty sources, ignores supplied PD/scenario weights/LGD overrides and computes annual loss rate times exposure-years without EIR, discount toggle, staging or impaired-asset recovery treatment. Common exposure/config validation still applies to ignored fields and allowance is not capped at current EAD. Establish eligible exposures, impaired-loan treatment, historical-loss-rate provenance and forward-looking adjustments before reporting-population adoption. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-18](statements-analytics-credit/18-cecl-warm.md).

- **Evidence — Statement-to-covenant integration needs independent qualification.** The adapter sends all periods including actuals, raw metrics and stored fiscal boundaries with inclusive-end conversion to the separate covenants crate. The adapter boundary and the [separate 14-file engine catalogue](covenants/README.md), including threshold/probability algorithms, have source-audit coverage. Independently qualify their combined period, metric, threshold and probability behavior; neither source audit establishes engine or integration accuracy. Agree all-period forecast selection and document inclusive-end conversion and the explicit-volatility requirement. Records: [STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-11](statements-analytics-credit/11-covenant-statement-adapter.md).

## Scenarios

[Scope inventory](scenarios/README.md).

- **Observed — Base-correlation detachment units differ from the owning curve.** The scenario adapter divides detachment basis points by 10,000, so 700 becomes 0.07; core curves and tranche pricing use percentage-of-pool coordinates, where 7% is 7.0. Scenario tests use fractional knots labeled as percentages and therefore mask the mismatch. A 700 bp request can miss a normal 7.0 knot and become a warning-qualified no-op. Reconcile the existing explicit-unit contract and add a normal-coordinate regression; the conditional impact was traced statically, not executed. Records: [010](scenarios/10-correlation-shocks.md).

- **Observed — Native and serial failure paths can leave different applied state.** Native runs containing at least two distinct ParCDS operations generate all ParCDS/inflation replacement effects before committing any run member; a later generation error leaves earlier members unapplied. Serial/WASM execution may already have committed them. The inspected first-error test has all missing hazards and does not cover success followed by failure. Reconcile the partial-mutation contract and qualify resulting state across dispatch modes; this does not dispute successful-value equivalence. Specify which warnings make an application unusable and whether success/completeness needs an explicit summary. Records: [002](scenarios/02-execution-and-partial-success.md).

- **Observed — Inventory P&L bypasses accepted execution and cache ownership.** Inventory P&L directly maps instrument.value at both dates and folds locally without engine PricingOptions, config or provider, while report metadata stamps the engine config. The engine also retains its cached recalibration provider across apply calls and documents immutable-batch sharing. Reconcile these paths with accepted ADR0022/INVARIANTS2.6 shared-executor and request-isolation requirements. Neither a stale-cache result nor a configuration-dependent price difference was executed. Records: [002](scenarios/02-execution-and-partial-success.md), [014](scenarios/14-carry-aggregation.md).

- **Observed — Forecast success can report no forecast mutation.** The forecast visitor reports success for any present value map, including empty or actual-only maps, so the engine can increment applied operations and rerun evaluation without changing a forecast cell. RateBindingNoForecastValues is reached only when the map is absent. Actual cells remain preserved. Distinguish applied changes from no-ops or narrow the diagnostic contract. Records: [012](scenarios/12-statement-bindings.md), [017](scenarios/17-reports-and-replay.md).

- **Observed — Applied-operation counts do not measure effective stress.** Unsupported instrument shocks are retained as metadata and counted with a downstream-consumption warning; some nonmatching composite children that fail decode or canonical serialization are silently skipped. Matrix prepay/default correlation returns an unchanged structure with a no-op message but is counted and labeled CorrelationClamped. Empty/unmatched volatility and base-correlation filters may also count. Reconcile effective-change/no-match/decode reporting before treating operations_applied as stress completeness or severity. Specify uniform no-match behavior and independently qualify each supported instrument/pricer route. Records: [010](scenarios/10-correlation-shocks.md), [011](scenarios/11-instrument-overrides.md), [017](scenarios/17-reports-and-replay.md).

- **Investigate — Native-node inverse solves and mixed-shock curve reconstruction.** Joint LM uses tolerance 1e-13 and an absolute 1e-12 postcheck across different target units, solving only requested times and touched pillars. Underdetermined shape follows initialization and solver behavior rather than an explicit economic regularizer. Forward rebuilding discards an earlier continuous transform and reconstructs visible node rates. Qualify off-pillar drift, scenario-as-of versus curve-base tenor origins, overlapping or nearly duplicate targets, extreme percentages, zero-time inflation and extrapolation; no numerical error bound was established. Independently benchmark nonflat already-transformed curves and sequential mixed parallel/node shocks; specify quantity-scaled tolerances, conditioning diagnostics and shape regularization. Records: [005](scenarios/05-direct-rate-shocks.md), [006](scenarios/06-node-delivery-solve.md), [008](scenarios/08-commodity-and-vol-index.md).

- **Decision — Par-CDS routing and approximation disclosures.** SolveToPar uses lossless recipes but can select discount curves by a three-letter ID prefix or sole available curve with a warning rather than verified currency alignment. Off-pillar quote snapping/rebootstrap uses an explicitly first-order normalized split. FirstOrderShift applies delta hazard = delta spread/(1-recovery), removes stale provenance and can prevent later lossless replay. Define acceptable routing/disclosure and qualify high recovery plus mixed rate/credit shocks without weakening the existing recipe requirement. Records: [007](scenarios/07-hazard-quote-shocks.md).

- **Decision — Volatility no-match and arbitrage diagnostics.** Expiry snapping uses max(0.02 years, 0.02 times expiry); off-grid secondary coordinates can silently select nothing. Preview rejects any zero grid volatility, including outside the selected bucket, and only warns on decreases in total variance at fixed grid coordinates. It does not check butterfly density or establish arbitrage for normal/shifted quote conventions. Define no-match and quote-specific diagnostic contracts and obtain independent examples. Records: [009](scenarios/09-volatility-surface-shocks.md).

- **Decision — Correlation clipping and model validity.** Sectored inter-sector shocks receive half the supplied change; matrix off-diagonals clip to [-0.99,0.99] without PSD validation/projection. Base-correlation clipping to [0,1] does not check tranche expected-loss arbitrage. Specify required downstream model validation, recursion through composite assets and effective-shift reporting, then qualify resulting prices with the core/model owners. Adopt clipping versus transformed-parameter shocks and the sectored half-weight rationale explicitly. Records: [010](scenarios/10-correlation-shocks.md).

- **Decision — Roll and carry information sets and completeness.** Future coupon fixings are deterministically materialized from original schedules in [t0,t1] before curve roll, with exact observations taking priority; FX, scalars, surfaces and surface axes remain static under shared core roll. Schedule failure aborts, while endpoint/cashflow failure can drop one carry row and still commit market/date changes. Carry uses nominal pre-roll cashflows in (t0,t1] without reinvestment, FX or position multipliers. Define completeness expectations and qualify seasoned instruments, fixing boundaries and exhausted curves; projected future fixings do not replace missing historical observations. Adopt frozen noncurve data, deterministic-forward versus stochastic fixing semantics and unsupported-schedule policy. Specify nominal cash conventions and external position-weight ownership, and qualify precision/determinism across parallel targets. Records: [013](scenarios/13-market-time-roll.md), [014](scenarios/14-carry-aggregation.md).

- **Investigate — Statement mutation overflow and flat bound-rate projection.** Only existing nonactual value-map entries change; no forecast cells or formulas are created. A binding assigns one curve-derived rate to every forecast period while preserving accumulation across clocks/compounding. Scalar percentage multiplication has no local result-finiteness gate, while Money mutations are checked and can leave earlier cell changes before a warning. Evaluator results are discarded apart from warnings. Qualify overflow/partial-cell failures and adopt the intended flat-rate input policy. Records: [012](scenarios/12-statement-bindings.md).

- **Decision — Horizon strictness and summary completeness.** Horizon analysis delegates with parallel execution and strict_validation=false, uses stamped endpoints and rejects instrument-mutating scenarios. Ratio return requires a positive same-currency opening mark; annualization uses actual days/365. Its nine-factor summary excludes residual and cross-factor fields retained in full attribution, so the summary need not add to total return. Define warning acceptance and summary disclosure and qualify approximation residuals with the attribution scope. Adopt the intended 365-day annualization scope and qualify representative nonlinear instruments. Records: [015](scenarios/15-horizon-attribution-and-returns.md).

- **Evidence — Historical stress templates lack historical calibration provenance.** Five fixed JSON templates contain 25 components and 40 operation entries with modern placeholder IDs but no source series, observation windows, calibration or probability provenance. Treat the exact documented parameters as stylized stresses until those authorities and quote conventions are supplied. SVB contains an instrument spread shock requiring inventory and is rejected by HorizonAnalysis; other credit-curve template shocks require recipes. Specify absent-target and unsupported-override completeness policy before relying on assembled event templates. Records: [016](scenarios/16-historical-template-parameters.md).

- **Decision — Replay bundles do not identify the full calculation.** The canonical scenario hash covers the scenario specification, not market state, instruments, calendars, provider or config. ApplicationEnvelope stores contexts/report but lacks an explicit rate-binding map, calendar object and complete execution provenance. Requested-shock descriptions generally omit effective before/after values except price scalars. Define the required replay bundle and prevent hash or count stability from being interpreted as numerical reproducibility. Records: [017](scenarios/17-reports-and-replay.md).

- **Decision — Scenario composition and hierarchy targeting.** Resolve mixed hierarchy-policy cumulative fallback, empty selector/no-op admission and commodity operation unit naming. Adopt equal-depth conflict handling, direct-versus-hierarchy precedence and whether unmatched or wrong-collection identifiers block production use. Correct the stale composition test name that describes a hazard-mode fallback while asserting rejection. Records: [001](scenarios/01-specification-and-composition.md), [003](scenarios/03-hierarchy-resolution.md).

- **Decision — Equity, FX and price-index shock conventions.** Decide stored-cross coverage, missing-equity-ID behavior and duplicate composition under the observed warning-only cross policy. Adopt unit-explicit commodity/volatility-index operations, allowed negative commodity outcomes, point-versus-relative index shocks and severity thresholds. Records: [004](scenarios/04-fx-and-equity-shocks.md), [008](scenarios/08-commodity-and-vol-index.md).

## Portfolio execution

[Scope inventory](portfolio-execution/README.md).

- **Observed — Native validation and materialization enforce different identities.** Portfolio::validate does not recheck publicly mutable quantity, live/reference instrument identity or entity map-key identity; add_position checks duplicate/book membership but not entity existence. Materialization checks these boundaries. Reconcile validation entry points and add malformed native-state cases; no mispricing was reproduced. Include non-finite attributes and bypassed native checks, oversized nested artifacts, bounded diagnostics, hash/version mismatches and dependency order/shape in the boundary cases. Records: [001](portfolio-execution/01-position-sizing-and-validation.md), [006](portfolio-execution/06-materialization-validation.md).

- **Observed — PV-only fallback can appear risk-complete.** Best-effort canonical pricing retries instrument.value after any canonical error. A PV-only fallback retains risk_error but sets risk_metrics_complete=true, so degraded_positions can be empty. The caller-config stamp does not establish that the retry honored the same selected options/provider behavior. Reconcile completeness and fallback eligibility with accepted execution requirements and qualify config-sensitive failures. Records: [003](portfolio-execution/03-valuation-profiles-and-failures.md).

- **Observed — Selective reuse identity relies on caller discipline.** Provenance does not include config or market fingerprints; reuse trusts caller-supplied changed keys and immutable-state discipline. Raw seeded reuse is index-only, with identity proof owned by the factor-stress caller. Reconcile the accepted complete-financial-meaning cache requirement and make the validated boundary explicit; no stale-price output was executed. Records: [004](portfolio-execution/04-execution-order-and-reduction.md), [005](portfolio-execution/05-dependencies-and-selective-reuse.md).

- **Observed — Direct book rollup can double count malformed maps.** The rollup entry point does not repeat full duplicate/reciprocal membership validation, although normal Portfolio construction rejects those states. Decide whether direct consumers must provide a validated graph or the public operation must enforce it, and qualify duplicate and inconsistent membership cases. Include deepest valid trees, malformed duplicate lists and order-independent construction. Records: [002](portfolio-execution/02-book-hierarchy-and-rollups.md).

- **Observed — Metric FX fallback and post-conversion finiteness.** Aggregation uses live FX first and falls back after any lookup error to the implied base/native PV ratio only when absolute native PV exceeds 1e-6; it ignores stored fx_rate. Nonfinite scaled additive metrics are skipped/reported, but post-FX overflow is not checked before Neumaier accumulation. Reconcile fallback eligibility, threshold units and arithmetic-finiteness requirements. Qualify FX failure and zero-PV paths, post-scaling overflow, additive-whitelist coverage and display units. Records: [010](portfolio-execution/10-metric-aggregation-and-reporting.md).

- **Investigate — Primitive and margin reductions bypass established accumulation rules.** Primitive quantity/measure totals and margin MTM/CSA/netting-set reductions use plain f64 sums; margin traverses hash maps before sorting wire output. Qualify mixed signs, wide dynamic ranges and ordering under existing compensated/deterministic accumulation rules. Money::new does not round to ingest scale, so these are Decimal/f64 representation and reduction issues rather than evidence of minor-unit rounding. Records: [011](portfolio-execution/11-primitive-exposures.md), [016](portfolio-execution/16-margin-netting-and-initial-margin.md), [017](portfolio-execution/17-margin-collateral-and-wire.md).

- **Decision — Artifact cache resource and reporting contracts.** The decode cache caps encoded bytes rather than live heap and holds its write lock through decode, serializing unrelated misses. Materialization unique_instruments counts artifact entries even when duplicate content is warned, and materializer version strings are not compatibility-checked. Define memory/concurrency guarantees, count semantics and version validation without claiming these observations are numerical defects. Qualify adversarial encoded/decoded size ratios, poisoned-lock recovery invariants and throughput for competing keys. Records: [006](portfolio-execution/06-materialization-validation.md), [007](portfolio-execution/07-artifact-cache-and-load-reporting.md).

- **Decision — NAV and cashflow FX conventions differ.** NAV uses effective-date spot while labeling policy CashflowDate; cash ladders explicitly use a covered-interest-parity forward. The helper has no explicit basis or forward-quote input, returns same-currency identity and checks curve factors for finite absolute magnitude at least 1e-14. Clarify labels and adopt/qualify the supported conversion convention, including signed or small factors, without inferring current market equivalence. Qualify cross-currency discounting identities, inversion/triangulation and precision boundaries. Records: [008](portfolio-execution/08-spot-and-forward-fx.md).

- **Decision — Factor engine support and profile provenance.** Wrappers reject assignment-driven empty-curve credit while direct engines permit empty mappings. Factor delta uses centered raw-value repricing; profiles use odd grids of 3–1001 multiples, default 5, and still value the base when no factors exist. Profile wire omits bump config and matrix conversion drops currency. No adaptive/one-sided bump or explicit common-random-number control exists. Define support/provenance and independently qualify stochastic consistency and boundary bumps. Include duplicate IDs, bucket ordering/weights, name collisions, volatility storage forms, direct-engine/wrapper domains, bump-size convergence, path reproducibility and small-PV-difference precision. Records: [012](portfolio-execution/12-factor-shocks-and-routing.md), [013](portfolio-execution/13-factor-deltas-and-pnl-profiles.md).

- **Decision — Replay mode, skipped-date and drawdown semantics.** PvOnly does not force an empty metric request—the default still includes DV01. BestEffort skips initial snapshot failures, but strict endpoint/attribution failures abort; the all-failed error asks for skipped_dates despite returning no result. MTM changes bridge surviving observations and exclude cash. Dollar and percentage drawdowns are separate, with percentage requiring positive peaks. Clarify mode and error/report contracts and qualify sparse-date histories. Cover mode/menu independence, skipped-date attribution, failure propagation and negative/zero NAV reporting; correct the unavailable-evidence instruction in the all-failed diagnostic. Records: [015](portfolio-execution/15-replay-mtm-and-drawdown.md).

- **Observed — Margin degradation can omit economically material contributions.** Per-position SIMM/VM/CCP failures degrade and omit contributions, while schedule errors abort. Current VM collateral is always zero; no-CSA bilateral VM equals negative gross MTM with explicit degradation; repo haircut terms are not consumed; cross-currency SIMM with nonempty FX delta is refused. total_positions counts successful VM contributions, while positions_without_margin includes untracked/degraded positions even with partial outputs, and only the first failure message survives. Reconcile completeness labels and unsupported terms with margin consumers. Records: [016](portfolio-execution/16-margin-netting-and-initial-margin.md), [017](portfolio-execution/17-margin-collateral-and-wire.md).

- **Decision — Margin result validation is narrower than full economic consistency.** Deserialization checks closure at absolute 1e-9, but cross-currency CSA accounts skip portfolio-collateral reconciliation without FX evidence. It does not validate every date, breakdown, sensitivity currency or count; helper unknown fields are accepted, and public mutation/serialization bypasses deserialize checks. Define the validated boundary and currency-aware reconciliation/materiality contract; no roundtrip failure was reproduced. Qualify absolute wire tolerance across scales and reorderings because canonical output order differs from runtime map traversal. Records: [017](portfolio-execution/17-margin-collateral-and-wire.md).

- **Decision — Scenario replacement identity and recalibration cache ownership.** The bridge keeps the position reference ID when replacing its live instrument, so an operation changing instrument ID needs explicit identity reconciliation. Its shared recalibration provider is separate from the per-market valuation cache and relies on calibration content-key completeness. Define the supported replacement/lifetime contract; no cache defect is asserted from the existence of these two caches alone. Compare scenario change manifests and endpoint results with unconditional fresh evaluation, including application-cache keys. Records: [014](portfolio-execution/14-scenario-application-and-pnl.md).

- **Decision — Projection and redundant-view completeness.** Table projections omit some completeness flags, aggregate currency and actual per-metric units; supplied result envelopes do not validate metrics against valuation correspondence. Deserialized cashflow redundant views are not cross-reconciled. Specify reporting fields and validation ownership so serialization is not mistaken for complete economic evidence. Qualify cashflow provider coverage, empty-placeholder meaning, partial-result labels, mixed-sign date netting and currency-conversion boundaries. Records: [009](portfolio-execution/09-cashflow-ladders.md), [010](portfolio-execution/10-metric-aggregation-and-reporting.md).

- **Evidence — Execution and factor tests need scoped independent interpretation.** Existing failure/cache/reduction/replay tests and synthetic drawdown arithmetic establish their named invariants; factor DV01 comparisons against a production metric are same-kernel reconciliation. They do not establish vendor pricing accuracy or current regulatory margin equivalence. Add independently sourced product/failure/FX/margin examples for the adopted contracts; no tests or benchmarks were executed in this audit. Execute thread-count/platform checks within the claimed deterministic tier; independently qualify applicability declarations and fallback behavior. Records: [003](portfolio-execution/03-valuation-profiles-and-failures.md), [007](portfolio-execution/07-artifact-cache-and-load-reporting.md), [010](portfolio-execution/10-metric-aggregation-and-reporting.md), [013](portfolio-execution/13-factor-deltas-and-pnl-profiles.md), [015](portfolio-execution/15-replay-mtm-and-drawdown.md), [016](portfolio-execution/16-margin-netting-and-initial-margin.md), [017](portfolio-execution/17-margin-collateral-and-wire.md).

## Portfolio attribution

[Scope inventory](portfolio-attribution/README.md).

- **Observed — Single-strategy inverse volatility suppresses invalid history.** The single-strategy branch returns unit weight and converts sample-volatility errors to None, unlike the multi-strategy branch that rejects nonfinite/insufficient history and zero/near-zero volatility. The trivial allocation remains valid but malformed-input and diagnostic contracts differ by strategy count. Decide whether the same history errors must be surfaced. Specify observation-frequency/alignment responsibility and fixed-weight tolerance versus normalized-capital reporting, and qualify highly uneven volatilities. Records: [001](portfolio-attribution/01-allocation-weights.md).

- **Observed — Excluded-data average objective reports an unnormalized numerator.** Exclude fixes missing-metric weights at existing levels and zeroes their coefficients, while budget still sums all weights to one. An unfiltered ValueWeightedAverage objective is therefore not divided by included weight: excluded weight 0.2 plus included weight 0.8 at metric 10 reports 8 rather than the included average 10. A positive fixed excluded weight need not change argmax, but value semantics differ. Reconcile objective reporting and add a direct arithmetic regression. Records: [011](portfolio-attribution/11-linear-program-formulation.md).

- **Observed — Nested composite FX translation moves inner FX P&L to residual.** The wrapper can translate native leaves into composite currency, aggregate them, and translate again into portfolio base. The shared translator replaces the existing FX translation bucket on the second conversion, while total P&L still closes by residual. A flat EUR leaf in a USD composite and CAD portfolio can therefore lose its EUR/USD translation from the FX bucket when outer USD/CAD is unchanged. Existing evidence uses the same composite and portfolio currency. Reconcile the shared transform and add this reachable nested regression; the source/algebra consequence was not executed. Records: [008](portfolio-attribution/08-portfolio-pnl-endpoints.md), [attribution 013](attribution/13-target-currency-and-scaling.md).

- **Investigate — Negative-only zero-PV candidates can evade the candidate gate.** The zero-PV candidate check requires absolute max_weight greater than 1e-12, so bounds min<0,max=0 evade it. LP can choose negative weight while near-zero-PV quantity reconstruction returns zero, creating a possible weight/quantity mismatch. Candidate bounds and metric constants also lack a comprehensive finite gate before LP construction. Reproduce these direct-Rust boundary cases and define admissible zero-PV trades. Include empty, short and percentage-unit portfolios in independent boundary qualification. Records: [010](portfolio-attribution/10-optimization-decision-space.md), [016](portfolio-attribution/16-optimization-results-and-rebalance.md).

- **Investigate — Shared-curve factor beta can differ between sensitivity and stress.** Sensitivity overlays use each position’s matching beta, but exact stress keeps the first encountered beta for a shared curve. Config validation lacks portfolio inventory and does not enforce same-curve/tag consistency. If positions sharing a curve resolve different betas, small full-repricing stress can diverge from summed sensitivities. Establish either consistency rejection or a market-representation contract and reproduce a conflicting-beta case. Qualify bump-size convergence, nested/composite dependency completeness and calibration-recipe availability. Records: [012](portfolio-attribution/12-factor-assignment-and-credit-exposures.md), [014](portfolio-attribution/14-factor-stress-and-position-what-if.md).

- **Observed — Nonannual forecast combines systematic and residual horizons.** FactorCovarianceForecast::factor_model_at replaces covariance with horizon-scaled covariance but clones matching unchanged. Portfolio construction reads annualized residual-adder volatility squared and residual application has no horizon input. A horizon other than one year can therefore combine horizon-scaled systematic variance with annual residual variance. Reconcile the factor/portfolio horizon contract and add a nonannual regression; source composition was verified, numerical impact was not executed. Records: [013](portfolio-attribution/13-credit-residual-risk-and-reporting.md).

- **Evidence — Brinson–Fachler bibliography does not identify its stated method.** The brinson-fachler-1985 anchor is headed Brinson–Fachler but lists Brinson/Hood/Beebower, Determinants of Portfolio Performance, while the kernel uses centered Fachler formulas. Correct the primary-source attribution and qualify the adopted zero-group convention; this catalogue entry is not verified provenance. Records: [005](portfolio-attribution/05-brinson-fachler.md), [attribution 016](attribution/16-benchmark-relative-return.md).

- **Investigate — Risk-budget convergence depends on covariance scale.** The absolute variance cutoff 1e-9 makes admissibility scale-dependent; negative or near-zero contributions fail even for a PSD covariance matrix. The 10,000-iteration cap and 1e-10 contribution tolerance have no independent convergence qualification. Define the supported covariance/risk-budget domain and test rescaling, weak factors and difficult feasible cases. Include negative-correlation admissibility, independent convergence/budget residuals/failure classification, near-zero variance and ill-conditioned matrices. Records: [002](portfolio-attribution/02-risk-budget-solver.md).

- **Decision — Return, signed-group and linking boundaries.** Modified Dietz trusts supplied timing fractions; nonpositive linking horizon is accepted without annualization. Direct return products reject overflow/underflow; single-period returns may be at or below -1 although Carino rejects them. Signed groups fail when absolute net is at most 1e-6 times gross. Grid linking validates top-level effects rather than supplied detail rows and permits changing grids. Define the compatible input/aggregation contracts and qualify these boundaries. Qualify long/high-return and near-bankruptcy sequences; document Dietz-to-investor sign conversion; specify zero-benchmark/short-weight rules, duplicate labels and finite-input overflow. For continuous-factor Brinson, establish scale-aware completeness tolerances, offsetting-exposure behavior and upstream fit/date provenance. For grids, adopt missing-cell/sector taxonomy and distinguish retained detail from reconciled aggregate links; specify dated alignment and equal-return/reconciliation thresholds. Records: [004](portfolio-attribution/04-performance-returns.md), [005](portfolio-attribution/05-brinson-fachler.md), [006](portfolio-attribution/06-continuous-factor-brinson.md), [015](portfolio-attribution/15-hierarchical-grid-attribution.md), [017](portfolio-attribution/17-carino-linking-and-reconciliation.md).

- **Decision — Campisi residual and reference-cell interpretation.** Campisi selection residual includes unmodeled roll, convexity and trading rather than pure skill. Reference midpoint zero-coupon return requires every midpoint exceed holding horizon but does not check curve date/currency alignment; reference-cell means are unweighted and one-decimal labels can collide. Adopt the economic meaning, alignment and identity rules and obtain independent reference cases. Qualify first-order error by yield/spread move and tenor, missing-sector comparators, near-zero-net and sector-sum cancellation; test cell-width sensitivity, one-decimal label limits, explicit clock/currency alignment and gap/extrapolation rules. Records: [007](portfolio-attribution/07-fixed-income-campisi.md), [009](portfolio-attribution/09-duration-cell-excess-returns.md).

- **Investigate — Average constraints lack a complete admissibility and postsolve gate.** Denominator sign is checked only after solve, so a negative-net optimum errors instead of solving over a nonnegative-denominator feasible domain. Near-zero denominator is described as vacuous/NaN, although a zero-net long/short basket can have nonzero weighted numerator and the lowered row can still bind. There is no general independent finite/feasibility postsolve gate. Reproduce these cases and specify intended admissibility before changing formulation. Use independent hand-solvable LPs to set finite-coefficient and post-solve tolerance policies. Records: [011](portfolio-attribution/11-linear-program-formulation.md).

- **Decision — LP baseline units and reconstruction limits.** Metrics remain frozen at baseline and amount-like coefficients can be weighted again unless callers supply matching semantics. Default net budget 1 differs from existing long/short gross normalization; UnitScaling requires explicit Budget and sums heterogeneous multipliers/quantities. Held variables override conflicting weight bounds. Diagnostic labels can overwrite, fixed binding/trade thresholds cross units, and no lot, cost, cash or revaluation layer exists. Optimal results are not provenance-bound to market/spec. Define the supported optimization contract and post-rebalance verification. Records: [010](portfolio-attribution/10-optimization-decision-space.md), [011](portfolio-attribution/11-linear-program-formulation.md), [016](portfolio-attribution/16-optimization-results-and-rebalance.md).

- **Decision — Residual credit risk and stress completeness.** Missing issuer metadata/calibration omits idiosyncratic risk, a near-flat net threshold zeros it, and residual aggregation uses plain sums. Stress freezes covariance and applies shifts in model order with duplicate shifts last-win. What-if proportionally scales existing sensitivities and cannot introduce new positions; PnL-only stress omits full baseline unmatched-dependency completeness checking. Define reporting/use limits and independently compare residual and stress behavior. Compare each supported risk measure and hedge configuration; include overlapping shifts, duplicate policy, extreme resize overflow and reuse against unconditional full repricing. Records: [013](portfolio-attribution/13-credit-residual-risk-and-reporting.md), [014](portfolio-attribution/14-factor-stress-and-position-what-if.md).

- **Decision — Endpoint closure is weaker than component accuracy.** The adapter freezes primitive composition and invokes the shared kernel with strict_validation=false, preserving invalid flags. Percentage zero detection uses default rounding context. Reconciliation proves arithmetic closure, not financial causality or correctness of each component. Specify invalid-result handling and independently qualify composition changes and component attribution. Qualify nondefault rounding-config percentage display and compare composite/multi-currency outputs with transaction-aware references. Records: [008](portfolio-attribution/08-portfolio-pnl-endpoints.md).

- **Decision — Scalar capital apportionment precision.** Confirm the ten-decimal public default and exact half-unit tie convention while keeping currency rounding and trade-lot allocation distinct. Independently qualify Decimal range failures and signed-capital conservation. Records: [003](portfolio-attribution/03-capital-apportionment.md).

## Attribution

[Scope inventory](attribution/README.md).

- **Observed — Repeated reporting-currency translation overwrites existing FX translation.** The translator replaces fx_translation_pnl while its factor visitor excludes that field. A second conversion retains earlier translation in headline total but moves its closing-FX-converted amount into recomputed residual. Portfolio primitive-to-composite-to-base conversion makes this reachable; the existing foreign-primitive fixture makes its second conversion a same-currency no-op. Reconcile the transform and add a nested currency regression in a later implementation slice, rather than treating arithmetic closure as validation. No numerical reproduction was run. Records: [013](attribution/13-target-currency-and-scaling.md), [portfolio-attribution 008](portfolio-attribution/08-portfolio-pnl-endpoints.md).

- **Observed — Taylor FX failure does not mark attribution invalid.** A failed FX factor adds a note, while ordinary failed factors and failed theta set result_invalid. A sufficiently small residual can therefore pass tolerance despite the FX failure. Reconcile failure qualification and add a focused failure-path regression in the authorized implementation work. Records: [007](attribution/07-taylor-volatility-and-restoration.md).

- **Observed — Taylor volatility coverage omits stored non-surface forms.** The ordinary volatility loop checks generic surfaces; scalar-vol restoration leaves closing surfaces/cubes in place and restores only scalars. Non-scalar SABR cubes, FX-delta surfaces and VolIndex movements lack a general remaining-volatility restoration and can fall into residual. Define supported storage forms and independently qualify them before claiming broad volatility coverage. Qualify duplicate dependencies and surface-average twist behavior alongside the storage-domain contract. Records: [007](attribution/07-taylor-volatility-and-restoration.md).

- **Observed — Default metrics and comments overstate covered attribution stages.** The metric menu includes Dividend01 without a dividend-specific attribution stage, and correlations have no general metrics stage. Seven metric cross pairs differ from nine default parallel pairs despite a comment claiming equality. Another comment fixes cash to [T0,T1), although the runtime follows each instrument’s [T0,T1) or (T0,T1] settlement boundary. Reconcile comments and exposed support with the actual algorithms; these are scope discrepancies rather than reproduced price defects. Adopt multi-underlying/multi-FX approximation domains and missing-metric qualification alongside volatility, dividend and correlation coverage. Records: [010](attribution/10-metric-other-factors.md).

- **Decision — Waterfall order, omission and pair-interaction policy.** Waterfall order determines allocation. Parallel correction covers selected second-order factor pairs (up to 36 in full mode), excluding time and higher-order interactions; pair totals use plain f64 sums and fixed 1e-12 currency materiality. Missing opening families and model-parameter errors have method-specific handling, and strict_validation is not global. Adopt report-specific order/omission policy and reconcile reduction/materiality with existing arithmetic requirements. Specify soft-failure visibility and benchmark nonlinear/time interactions and economic order effects. Records: [004](attribution/04-ordered-waterfall.md), [005](attribution/05-parallel-and-cross-factor.md).

- **Investigate — Finite-difference and metric curvature accuracy.** Minimum/maximum bumps and scalar curvature-warning thresholds are implementation controls, not accuracy bounds. Taylor rate loops can count a shared discount/forward ID twice, while metrics context deduplicates it. Rate metric curvature uses absolute-DV01-weighted moves; credit gamma uses unweighted bucket moves. Qualify twists, large notionals, cross-curve interactions, shared dependency IDs and bump sensitivity against independent calculations. Specify twist/large-move warning effects on report qualification and verify producer/consumer units across instruments. Records: [006](attribution/06-taylor-curve-risk.md), [009](attribution/09-metric-rates-and-credit.md).

- **Decision — Cash, FX and financing bases vary by method.** Period cash uses paid-date conversion inside the supplied opening snapshot and the instrument settlement boundary; reporting-currency cash addback later converts at closing FX. Flat-yield carry uses the first discount curve and generic annual compounding outside bonds; Taylor has no accrual/flat/funding split. Metric carry rejects unmatched producer horizons and requires funding alongside net CarryTotal. Adopt economic cash/FX and gross-versus-net financing contracts and qualify their cross-method interpretation. Independently qualify floating, multi-curve, optional and non-bond carry, omission versus invalid/error policy, exact quote pairing and coupon boundaries. Records: [003](attribution/03-total-return-carry.md), [008](attribution/08-metric-inputs-and-move-pairing.md), [013](attribution/13-target-currency-and-scaling.md).

- **Decision — Credit hierarchy selection and missing-factor allocation.** Detail assumes one issuer tag and first discount curve, equally averages measurable credit curves and suppresses scalar factor-measurement errors to absence. A partially matching configured factor list does not append omitted levels; missing moves route to adder. Define dependency completeness and partial hierarchy/error policy rather than reading adder as a single economic factor. Adopt equal-curve averaging, one discount/issuer assignment and warning thresholds; obtain independent calibrated multi-curve benchmarks. Records: [011](attribution/11-credit-factor-hierarchy.md).

- **Decision — Credit carry partition and warning thresholds.** Credit carry uses first hazard/discount, a credit-triangle yield share, rates ownership of all roll-down and allocation at hierarchy anchor levels. The 99-times unit check and 5% magnitude check are warning policies rather than independent statistical qualification. Adopt those conventions and independently qualify multi-curve, low-yield and large-move cases. Include negative rates, near-zero risky yields and credit roll-down in independent allocation evidence. Records: [012](attribution/12-credit-carry-partition.md).

- **Decision — Scaling, tolerances and aggregate mutation.** Tolerance inputs lack a local finite/nonnegative gate and remain numerically unchanged under quantity scaling or currency conversion; scaling also preserves residual_pct. Accumulation retains coarse aggregates only and can partially mutate before a later error. Define validation ownership, unit-aware materiality, relative residual semantics and atomicity. Records: [013](attribution/13-target-currency-and-scaling.md).

- **Decision — Return contribution input identity and tiny-book weights.** Contributions trust same-period/currency data; as_of is merely nonempty text, duplicate IDs are allowed and supplied weights are retained. An absolute 1e-9 denominator threshold gives zero weights to a tiny gross book. Define period/currency/identity validation and scale-aware denominator behavior. Records: [014](attribution/14-return-contribution-weights.md).

- **Decision — Benchmark dimension and zero-group conventions.** Benchmark dimension is selected implicitly—sector first, then lexical—and zero-group substitutions are explicit algorithm choices. Adopt the selection and empty-group rules and verify the Brinson/Fachler and Bacon primary provenance, including the separate bibliography mismatch. Records: [016](attribution/16-benchmark-relative-return.md), [portfolio-attribution 005](portfolio-attribution/05-brinson-fachler.md).

- **Decision — Long and wide output have different economic validation and detail.** Long output contains subtotals and components, so indiscriminate row summation double counts. It omits credit-carry hierarchy and absolute adder diagnostic; wide output validates currency while long views do not. Define consumer schema/detail requirements and currency validation, without interpreting table serialization as economic reconciliation. Records: [015](attribution/15-detail-and-reporting-contracts.md).

- **Evidence — External fixture coverage remains method and branch specific.** Stored QuantLib parity fixtures exist for bond, IRS and FX-forward attribution; the sampled bond fixture pins QuantLib 1.43. They are external-reference expectations, unlike same-library reconstruction checks, but were not executed or regenerated here. Preserve that provenance distinction and qualify unsupported products, alternative methods and the newly identified failure/translation branches independently. Records: [001](attribution/01-execution-and-endpoints.md), [003](attribution/03-total-return-carry.md), [005](attribution/05-parallel-and-cross-factor.md), [013](attribution/13-target-currency-and-scaling.md).

- **Decision — Prepared endpoint trust and market restoration boundaries.** Agree provenance ownership for prepared endpoints, method-specific failure behavior, tolerance input domains and reversed-date `pnl_bridge` use. Qualify changing dependency sets, replacement curve storage types under an ID and invalidated credit-index metadata before adopting family-restoration boundaries. Records: [001](attribution/01-execution-and-endpoints.md), [002](attribution/02-market-factor-restoration.md).

## Margin regulatory capital and XVA

[Scope inventory](margin-regulatory-xva/README.md).

- **Observed — FRTB duplicate wire factors and mutators net differently.** Tuple-array JSON reconstruction keeps the last duplicate factor, while `add_*` methods sum identical keys. Specify rejection, accumulation or overwrite semantics, and add a fixture comparing the two entry paths before treating equivalent feeds as interchangeable. Records: [001](margin-regulatory-xva/01-frtb-inputs-and-wire.md).

- **Observed — FRTB source narratives lag current formulas.** Curvature runtime squares both intra/inter-bucket correlations before scenario transformation and retains raw uncapped S. Source prose still describes half-squaring/scenario-before-square and capped S; an equity-vega parameter-test comment likewise predates the bucket 9–11 cap. Reconcile those comments without reporting the historical numerical behavior as a current defect. The affected curvature prose is in [correlation_scenarios.rs](../../../finstack-quant/margin/src/regulatory/frtb/params/correlation_scenarios.rs) and the curvature module overview; preserve the square-before-scenario and raw-S branch evidence while correcting it. Records: [009](margin-regulatory-xva/09-frtb-bucketed-vega.md), [011](margin-regulatory-xva/11-frtb-curvature.md).

- **Observed — SA-CCR reporting maturity is a different statistic.** The reported unmargined MF is the MF function of an equal-mean underlying end maturity, not a tenor/notional-weighted average of actual trade MFs, and ignores option expiry. It does not enter EAD. Qualify the result field and stale weighted-average description; also correct the claimed positive supervisory-duration floor, which is actually zero. Establish heterogeneous-option-expiry and long-MPOR validation targets, distinguishing the reporting summary from the per-trade factors used in exposure. Records: [015](margin-regulatory-xva/15-saccr-maturity-and-duration.md).

- **Observed — Capped EAD retains pre-cap components.** Margined EAD is capped at a recomputed unmargined EAD, while returned RC, PFE and add-on fields remain the original margined values. The result has no pre-cap EAD, cap amount or binding flag, so its components need not reconstruct the returned EAD. Define an explicit result/replay contract; no cap-adjusted component allocation is currently calculated. Records: [018](margin-regulatory-xva/18-saccr-rc-pfe-ead.md).

<a id="saccr-collateral-qualification"></a>

- **Observed — SA-CCR collateral methodology needs qualification.** The [central sign-conversion entry](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#sign-conversion-boundaries) owns the [accepted ADR 0044 component-input package](conventions/0044-financial-perspectives-and-boundary-conversions.md#accepted-amendment-explicit-collateral-components) and subsequent public-field/shared-C reconciliation. Numeric values alone cannot prove upstream economic non-overlap. Qualify the full margined and unmargined methodologies separately, including collateral recognition, eligibility, adjustments, and the unmargined NICA extension in `max(V - C, 0)`. Preserve the held-by-bank positive sign and accepted margined RC formula; this work does not reopen those conventions. Records: [018](margin-regulatory-xva/18-saccr-rc-pfe-ead.md).

- **Observed — Effective EPE is not an input to this SA-CCR engine.** XVA updates its running EPE maximum before integrating each preceding interval through min(maturity,1). The inspected SA-CCR engine consumes neither that statistic nor exposure profiles. Reconcile field/reference claims connecting it directly to SA-CCR and specify the right-endpoint/opening-node convention before assigning it regulatory meaning. Records: [019](margin-regulatory-xva/19-xva-profile-and-epe.md).

- **Observed — Positive DVA denotes a benefit.** Actual composition is CVA−DVA+FVA+MVA, with positive DVA representing own-default benefit. Reconcile the generic result overview that says positive adjustments are costs with the field and composition contract. Records: [021](margin-regulatory-xva/21-xva-funding-and-composition.md).

- **Observed — IM decay has no explicit valuation-time anchor.** ImProfile prohibits t=0, but MVA and DVA hold its first already-decayed sample back to zero. A single linear/square-root sample at decay maturity consequently produces zero IM/MVA despite positive current SIMM. This is a source-derived consequence, not an executed numerical reproduction. Agree opening-IM, grid/domain and extrapolation semantics before using the profile as expected future margin. Specify the minimum sampling resolution and opening-domain requirements; qualify low-level direct-helper inputs separately from the validated bridge. Records: [020](margin-regulatory-xva/20-xva-credit-adjustments.md), [022](margin-regulatory-xva/22-simm-deterministic-im-profile.md), [023](margin-regulatory-xva/23-mva-spread-and-quadrature.md).

- **Decision — FRTB input construction and risk perimeter.** Delta inputs are P&L per percentage-point/percent, vega is already volatility-scaled, and CVR is already loss-positive full-shock curvature after delta removal. Reporting currency does not convert values. Assign ownership for units, issuer/bucket identity, FX orientation and residual-risk eligibility; explicitly delimit omitted GIRR curve dimensions, pair/currency relief and non-securitisation-only DRC despite CSR securitisation SBA support. Confirm the 100-fold numeric spot/repo weight distinction, commodity delivery-location versus credit bond/CDS-basis mapping, gross-notional RRAO units and classification responsibility, GIRR vega scaling and surface interpolation onto both maturity axes, and the externally aggregated vega scope where basis/location axes are absent. Qualify the single implicit FX-vega pair bucket against the intended factor aggregation. Define removal of settled/matured DRC positions where zero maturity currently receives the quarter-year floor. Records: [001](margin-regulatory-xva/01-frtb-inputs-and-wire.md), [003](margin-regulatory-xva/03-frtb-girr-delta.md), [004](margin-regulatory-xva/04-frtb-credit-delta.md), [007](margin-regulatory-xva/07-frtb-fx-delta.md), [012](margin-regulatory-xva/12-frtb-default-risk.md), [013](margin-regulatory-xva/13-frtb-residual-risk.md), [005](margin-regulatory-xva/05-frtb-equity-delta.md), [006](margin-regulatory-xva/06-frtb-commodity-delta.md), [008](margin-regulatory-xva/08-frtb-girr-vega.md), [009](margin-regulatory-xva/09-frtb-bucketed-vega.md), [010](margin-regulatory-xva/10-frtb-fx-vega.md).

- **Evidence — Regulatory parameter provenance and applicability.** Fixed Rust tables and default result metadata do not identify an effective jurisdictional rulebook. The source’s historical verification assertion was not independently re-certified. Attach authoritative version/perimeter evidence and approved overrides before treating parameter or method names as compliance; independent mixed-sign, mixed-basis and multi-position benchmark books remain needed. Include mixed long/short equity spot-repo, issuer-assignment and index-bucket examples; commodity location/tenor cases; negative and cross-bucket vega, GIRR joint maturities and same-pair/multi-expiry FX vega; curvature ties, zero quadratics and other buckets; and DRC seniority offsets, rating/sector stresses across several simultaneous positions. SA-CCR books must cover exact 1/5-year IR boundaries, forward starts, multi-currency classification, cancellation within/across buckets and mixed sign/entity/category non-IR cases. Records: [001](margin-regulatory-xva/01-frtb-inputs-and-wire.md), [004](margin-regulatory-xva/04-frtb-credit-delta.md), [008](margin-regulatory-xva/08-frtb-girr-vega.md), [012](margin-regulatory-xva/12-frtb-default-risk.md), [014](margin-regulatory-xva/14-saccr-inputs-and-supervisory-delta.md), [018](margin-regulatory-xva/18-saccr-rc-pfe-ead.md), [005](margin-regulatory-xva/05-frtb-equity-delta.md), [006](margin-regulatory-xva/06-frtb-commodity-delta.md), [009](margin-regulatory-xva/09-frtb-bucketed-vega.md), [010](margin-regulatory-xva/10-frtb-fx-vega.md), [011](margin-regulatory-xva/11-frtb-curvature.md), [016](margin-regulatory-xva/16-saccr-ir-addon.md), [017](margin-regulatory-xva/17-saccr-non-ir-addons.md).

- **Decision — FRTB quadratic repairs and scenario defaults.** Ordinary delta/vega retries a negative cross-bucket quadratic using capped S; curvature applies its own zero floor without that retry. A tied default total selects the first scenario, Low. Agree repair observability, accepted numerical domain and tie/reporting policy, and qualify negative-quadratic and offset-heavy portfolios. Approve partial-scenario and partial-class diagnostic use separately from full-capital use; this includes the chosen tie label and result meaning. Records: [002](margin-regulatory-xva/02-frtb-scenarios-and-aggregation.md), [011](margin-regulatory-xva/11-frtb-curvature.md).

- **Investigate — Finite regulatory inputs do not ensure finite outputs.** FRTB products/aggregates and SA-CCR sums can overflow despite finite input checks, with no uniform final-finite guard. Sorted JSON also does not establish floating-point charge invariance under insertion order. Reproduce extreme-scale and reordered equivalent portfolios, then reconcile the accepted finite-result/reproducibility requirements; no financial impact has been measured here. Define and reproduce numerical bounds for NaN interaction with `max(0)`, positive-charge filtering and RRAO gross-notional accumulation before claiming safe behavior. Records: [001](margin-regulatory-xva/01-frtb-inputs-and-wire.md), [002](margin-regulatory-xva/02-frtb-scenarios-and-aggregation.md), [011](margin-regulatory-xva/11-frtb-curvature.md), [018](margin-regulatory-xva/18-saccr-rc-pfe-ead.md), [013](margin-regulatory-xva/13-frtb-residual-risk.md).

- **Decision — SA-CCR trade lifecycle and sign boundaries.** Validation does not reject blank identifiers, duplicate trades, reversed underlying dates or already-matured trades. Notionals are absolute-valued, and option direction is not cross-checked with the option long/short type. Matured FX/equity/commodity trades can retain the 0.2 MF floor. Define required upstream inclusion, delta-generation and normalization gates rather than infer economic validity from successful validation. Records: [014](margin-regulatory-xva/14-saccr-inputs-and-supervisory-delta.md), [015](margin-regulatory-xva/15-saccr-maturity-and-duration.md), [017](margin-regulatory-xva/17-saccr-non-ir-addons.md).

- **Decision — SA-CCR hedging labels control offset eligibility.** IR, commodity and FX grouping relies on caller hedging-set labels; credit/equity pool those labels. FX rho one fully nets unlike underliers within a shared label. Specify typed classification or an auditable feed contract, including adjusted notional and supervisory category assignment, and independently test allowed and disallowed offsets. Make factor identity replayable, including unlike currencies sharing an IR label and the omitted basis/volatility subsets or multi-currency trade decomposition. Records: [016](margin-regulatory-xva/16-saccr-ir-addon.md), [017](margin-regulatory-xva/17-saccr-non-ir-addons.md).

- **Decision — XVA profile and curve alignment.** Numeric exposure times are passed directly to curves without origin, day-count or currency reconciliation. Recorded roll/valuation failures are rejected, but total-point diagnostics are not reconciled. Curve kernels reject nonfinite evaluations without independently enforcing all curve-domain constraints. Establish caller guarantees, opening exposure back-extrapolation and failure completeness before accepting a profile for valuation. Specify the integrator curve contract where negative survival increments are clamped; this is separate from the validation already performed by curve constructors. Records: [019](margin-regulatory-xva/19-xva-profile-and-epe.md), [020](margin-regulatory-xva/20-xva-credit-adjustments.md).

- **Decision — Bilateral dependence and funding economics.** CVA/DVA use positive survival decrements and products of separate endpoint means; bilateral first-to-default is an independence-style approximation without joint-default or wrong-way calibration. Posted IM reduces ENE/DVA before averaging but not EPE/CVA or FVA. Agree collateral availability, legal close-out, survival/funding dependence and own-credit/funding double-counting treatment. Define own-credit/funding transfer pricing, netting/collateral perimeter and currency alignment; survival weighting alone does not settle those economic choices. Records: [020](margin-regulatory-xva/20-xva-credit-adjustments.md), [021](margin-regulatory-xva/21-xva-funding-and-composition.md).

- **Evidence — Deterministic future IM needs qualification.** The SIMM bridge calculates current SIMM once and applies constant, linear or square-root decay; it does not simulate future risk, recalibrate sensitivities or convert currency. Establish the projection’s intended economic use, calibration and comparison against path-consistent expected IM, including terminal runoff. Records: [022](margin-regulatory-xva/22-simm-deterministic-im-profile.md).

- **Evidence — XVA quadrature and convergence targets.** MVA evaluates linearly interpolated/flat-extrapolated spreads at IM-bucket midpoints and multiplies separate endpoint averages; spread knots do not refine the grid. Existing flat-hazard CVA/DVA examples use 2% tolerance, FVA 3%, and MVA simple hand integrations. Establish nonflat-curve/spread, coarse-opening-interval and joint-survival convergence targets beyond these special cases; replay and invariant tests are not independent model certification. Records: [019](margin-regulatory-xva/19-xva-profile-and-epe.md), [020](margin-regulatory-xva/20-xva-credit-adjustments.md), [021](margin-regulatory-xva/21-xva-funding-and-composition.md), [023](margin-regulatory-xva/23-mva-spread-and-quadrature.md).

## Factor models

[Scope inventory](factor/README.md).

- **Investigate — Finite position-risk inputs can overflow intermediate results.** Normal position risk validates finite weights/covariance but lacks the factor-risk path’s finite-variance guard. A static IEEE trace with weight 1e200 and covariance [1] can overflow variance and create nonfinite contributions; historical/stress scenario sums and budget positive-excess sums also lack final finite checks. Reproduce these paths and reconcile accepted finite-boundary requirements; this is not an executed reproduction. Records: [005](factor/factor-risk.md), [007](factor/position-normal-risk.md), [008](factor/position-historical-risk.md), [009](factor/tail-stress.md), [010](factor/risk-budgets-and-reports.md).

- **Observed — Credit artifact semantic validation is incomplete.** Ordinary serde validates structure and the typed marker but does not call `CreditFactorModel::validate()`. The explicit semantic validator checks selected identifiers, dimensions, tags, correlation/config structure and history lengths, but not all beta shape/finiteness, anchors, operative folds, volatility-state domains, history IDs/dates/finiteness or calibration ordering. It does not inspect `diagnostics.fold_ups`; decomposition rejects an out-of-range level later. It also does not reconcile top-level `issuer_betas` with duplicated `config.matching` rows consumed by different downstream paths. Establish validation responsibility and malformed-artifact evidence for these specific computational fields, and establish authority between duplicated issuer rows. The [central coverage entry](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#credit-artifact-validation) owns cross-entry-point, post-mutation and fold-up coverage; successful loading does not certify all computational invariants. Records: [019](factor/credit-artifacts.md).

- **Reconciliation — Credit artifact contract enforcement.** The [central semantic-validation entry](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#credit-artifact-validation) owns wire, loader, consumer/mutation coverage and deterministic schema checks under accepted [ADR 0047](../architecture/0047-credit-factor-wire-and-validation-contract.md). The [central guidance entry](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#credit-factor-guidance) records the completed Serde, `rust_json` and root-field wording reconciliation and any remaining guidance. Use those entries for progress and closure; acceptance does not certify enforcement. Records: [017](factor/credit-snapshots.md), [019](factor/credit-artifacts.md).

- **Observed — Risk-allocation descriptions overstate exact Euler behavior.** Normal position risk zeros components for sigma<=1e-6 while retaining a potentially nonzero total. Historical component VaR is componentES×VaR/ES, not a direct VaR derivative; tiny-ES and finite-precision cutoffs affect conservation. Name the chosen allocation and qualify exact-Euler claims, with boundary evidence. Qualify stressed, hedged and large-exposure normal cases, the empirical quantile/gain-tail policy, and uncertainty/coverage for intended historical sample sizes. Records: [007](factor/position-normal-risk.md), [008](factor/position-historical-risk.md).

- **Observed — Public sparse-calibration overview is stale.** The factor README’s opening sparse-history description conflicts with public validation, which rejects every missing observation and requires a complete regular grid. Private sparse/pairwise kernels do not establish a supported sparse public mode. Reconcile the overview with the supported domain without inferring new sparse-calibration authorization. Records: [011](factor/credit-panel.md).

- **Observed — Matrix mutation and shape boundaries differ from constructors.** Sensitivity setters accept nonfinite values and use assertions for bounds; zero construction/covariance shape arithmetic includes unchecked size products. Reconcile mutation, allocation and constructor behavior under the accepted public-boundary policy, and establish targeted malformed/oversized-input tests before asserting specific downstream failures. Specify scale-aware covariance acceptance and missing-ID semantics; independently qualify large-scale and near-singular cases. Records: [002](factor/matrix-invariants.md).

- **Observed — Snapshot entry points enforce different validation.** decompose_levels does not call model.validate or reject dotted runtime tags itself; period decomposition does not invoke snapshot finite validation, while convenience JSON loaders do validate finite values.
